     1	/**
     2	 * Brian Live Client Editor V6.5 — Conversational Website Studio Engine
     3	 *
     4	 * Allows authenticated prospect visitors on `/outreach-preview/:secureId` to request
     5	 * natural-language modifications to their proposed website HTML/CSS and see live updates.
     6	 *
     7	 * Security & Scope:
     8	 * - Decision 1 (Option B): Server-signed expiring editing capability (`bcap_...`) redeemed into
     9	 *   a short-lived session token (`briv_...`) bound to the exact prospect's `secureId`, with
    10	 *   per-session rate limits, edit budgets, and explicit revocation.
    11	 * - Decision 2: Strict customer HTML script stripping + minimal audited viewer bridge script
    12	 *   for zero-reload `postMessage` DOM updates and click-to-select section/element targeting.
    13	 * - Decision 3: Real GoDaddy API domain verification when credentials are configured, or
    14	 *   honest `AVAILABILITY_UNKNOWN` with zero invented prices.
    15	 * - Phase 3: Six premium colour palettes, responsive split-hero media queries, headline & phone
    16	 *   mutations, photo-backed illustrative trade project galleries (obeying the V6 Odd-Grid Law and
    17	 *   no-fake-accreditation rule), modernised glass-and-light elevation, and Google Gemini 3.8 Flash
    18	 *   section-scoped AI execution.
    19	 * - Zero asterisks in copy.
    20	 */

    22	import { createHash, createHmac, randomBytes, timingSafeEqual } from "node:crypto";
    23	import { resolve as dnsResolve4 } from "node:dns/promises";
    24	import {

    35	const SESSION_TTL_MS = 2 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 2 hours
    36	const CAPABILITY_TTL_MS = 14 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 14 days for outreach email links
    37	const MAX_HTML_BYTES = 500_000;
    38	const MAX_EDITS_PER_SESSION = 15;
    39	const MIN_EDIT_INTERVAL_MS = 400;
    40	
    41	interface SessionRateState {
    42	  editCount: number;
    43	  lastEditAt: number;
    44	  revoked: boolean;
    45	}
    46	
    47	const sessionRateLedger = new Map<string, SessionRateState>();
    48	
    49	/**
    50	 * Decision 1 (Option B): Server-generated, signed, expiring editing capability token
    51	 * tied to a single prospect (`secureId`).
    52	 */
    53	export function generateEditCapability(
    54	  secret: string,
    55	  secureId: string,
    56	  ttlMs = CAPABILITY_TTL_MS
    57	): string {
    58	  const expTs = (Date.now() + ttlMs).toString();
    59	  const cleanId = secureId.toLowerCase().trim();
    60	  const payload = `bcap:${expTs}:${cleanId}`;
    61	  const hmac = createHmac("sha256", secret).update(payload).digest("hex").slice(0, 32);
    62	  return `bcap_${expTs}_${cleanId}_${hmac}`;
    63	}
    64	
    65	export function verifyEditCapability(
    66	  secret: string,
    67	  expectedSecureId: string,
    68	  capability: string
    69	): { valid: boolean; reason?: string; expiresAt?: number } {
    70	  if (!capability || typeof capability !== "string" || !capability.startsWith("bcap_")) {
    71	    return { valid: false, reason: "MISSING_OR_MALFORMED_CAPABILITY" };
    72	  }
    73	  const parts = capability.split("_");
    74	  if (parts.length !== 4) {
    75	    return { valid: false, reason: "INVALID_CAPABILITY_FORMAT" };
    76	  }
    77	  const [, expStr, capSecureId, signature] = parts;
    78	  const expTs = Number(expStr);
    79	  if (!Number.isFinite(expTs) || Date.now() > expTs) {
    80	    return { valid: false, reason: "CAPABILITY_EXPIRED" };
    81	  }
    82	  const cleanExpected = expectedSecureId.toLowerCase().trim();
    83	  if (capSecureId !== cleanExpected) {
    84	    return { valid: false, reason: "CROSS_PROSPECT_CAPABILITY_MISMATCH" };
    85	  }
    86	  const payload = `bcap:${expStr}:${cleanExpected}`;
    87	  const expectedHmac = createHmac("sha256", secret).update(payload).digest("hex").slice(0, 32);
    88	  const a = Buffer.from(signature, "utf8");
    89	  const b = Buffer.from(expectedHmac, "utf8");
    90	  if (a.length !== b.length || !timingSafeEqual(a, b)) {
    91	    return { valid: false, reason: "INVALID_CAPABILITY_SIGNATURE" };
    92	  }
    93	  return { valid: true, expiresAt: expTs };
    94	}
    95	
    96	export function generateVisitorToken(secret: string, secureId: string, clientIp = "visitor"): string {
    97	  const timestamp = Date.now().toString();
    98	  const cleanId = secureId.toLowerCase().trim();
    99	  const payload = `${timestamp}:${cleanId}:${clientIp}`;
   100	  const hmac = createHmac("sha256", secret).update(payload).digest("hex").slice(0, 32);
   101	  return `briv_${timestamp}_${cleanId}_${hmac}`;
   102	}
   103	
   104	export function deriveSessionNonce(sessionToken: string): string {
   105	  return createHash("sha256").update(`nonce:${sessionToken}`).digest("hex").slice(0, 20);
   106	}
   107	
   108	export function revokeVisitorToken(token: string): void {
   109	  if (!token) return;
   110	  const existing = sessionRateLedger.get(token) || { editCount: 0, lastEditAt: 0, revoked: false };
   111	  existing.revoked = true;
   112	  sessionRateLedger.set(token, existing);
   113	}
   114	
   115	export function checkAndRecordSessionEditBudget(
   116	  token: string,
   117	  enforceInterval = false
   118	): { allowed: boolean; reason?: string; remainingEdits: number } {
   119	  const state = sessionRateLedger.get(token) || { editCount: 0, lastEditAt: 0, revoked: false };
   120	  if (state.revoked) {
   121	    return { allowed: false, reason: "SESSION_REVOKED", remainingEdits: 0 };
   122	  }
   123	  const now = Date.now();
   124	  if (enforceInterval && state.lastEditAt > 0 && now - state.lastEditAt < MIN_EDIT_INTERVAL_MS) {
   125	    return {
   126	      allowed: false,
   127	      reason: "RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED",
   128	      remainingEdits: Math.max(0, MAX_EDITS_PER_SESSION - state.editCount),
   129	    };
   130	  }
   131	  if (state.editCount >= MAX_EDITS_PER_SESSION) {
   132	    return { allowed: false, reason: "EDIT_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED", remainingEdits: 0 };
   133	  }
   134	  state.editCount += 1;
   135	  state.lastEditAt = now;
   136	  sessionRateLedger.set(token, state);
   137	  return {
   138	    allowed: true,
   139	    remainingEdits: Math.max(0, MAX_EDITS_PER_SESSION - state.editCount),
   140	  };
   141	}
   142	
   143	export function verifyVisitorToken(
   144	  secret: string,
   145	  expectedSecureId: string,
   146	  token: string,
   147	  clientIp = "visitor"
   148	): { valid: boolean; reason?: string } {
   149	  if (!token || typeof token !== "string" || !token.startsWith("briv_")) {
   150	    return { valid: false, reason: "MISSING_OR_MALFORMED_TOKEN" };
   151	  }
   152	  const parts = token.split("_");
   153	  if (parts.length !== 4) {
   154	    return { valid: false, reason: "INVALID_TOKEN_FORMAT" };
   155	  }
   156	  const [, tsStr, tokenSecureId, signature] = parts;
   157	  const ts = Number(tsStr);
   158	  if (!Number.isFinite(ts) || Date.now() - ts > SESSION_TTL_MS || ts > Date.now() + 60_000) {
   159	    return { valid: false, reason: "TOKEN_EXPIRED" };
   160	  }
   161	  const cleanExpected = expectedSecureId.toLowerCase().trim();
   162	  if (tokenSecureId !== cleanExpected) {
   163	    return { valid: false, reason: "CROSS_PROSPECT_TOKEN_MISMATCH" };
   164	  }
   165	
   166	  const state = sessionRateLedger.get(token);
   167	  if (state?.revoked) {
   168	    return { valid: false, reason: "SESSION_REVOKED" };
   169	  }
   170	
   171	  const payload = `${tsStr}:${cleanExpected}:${clientIp}`;
   172	  const expectedHmac = createHmac("sha256", secret).update(payload).digest("hex").slice(0, 32);
   173	
   174	  const a = Buffer.from(signature, "utf8");
   175	  const b = Buffer.from(expectedHmac, "utf8");
   176	  if (a.length !== b.length || !timingSafeEqual(a, b)) {
   177	    return { valid: false, reason: "INVALID_SIGNATURE" };
   178	  }
   179	
   180	  return { valid: true };
   181	}

   183	/**
   184	 * Strips any untrusted or customer-injected <script> blocks and inline JS event handlers
   185	 * from stored HTML while preserving clean structural HTML and CSS.
   186	 */
   187	export function sanitizeCustomerHtml(html: string): string {
   188	  if (!html || typeof html !== "string") return "";
   189	  return html
   190	    .replace(/<script\b[^>]*>[\s\S]*?<\/script>/gi, "")
   191	    .replace(/<style\b[^>]* id=["']brian-audited-viewer-style["'][^>]*>[\s\S]*?<\/style>/gi, "")
   192	    .replace(/\s+on[a-z]+\s*=\s*(?:"[^"]*"|'[^']*'|[^\s>]+)/gi, "");
   193	}
   194	
   195	/**
   196	 * Decision 2: Injects ONLY the minimal, audited Brian Studio Viewer Bridge script
   197	 * for safe postMessage DOM updates (preserving scroll position) and click-to-select
   198	 * section/element inspection inside a `sandbox="allow-scripts"` iframe.
   199	 */
   200	export function injectAuditedViewerBridge(
   201	  rawHtml: string,
   202	  sessionNonce: string,
   203	  secureId: string
   204	): string {
   205	  const cleanHtml = sanitizeCustomerHtml(rawHtml);
   206	  const safeNonce = String(sessionNonce || "readonly").replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, "");
   207	  const safeSecureId = String(secureId || "").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]/g, "");
   208	
   209	  const viewerStyle = `<style id="brian-audited-viewer-style">
   210	  [data-brian-hover-outline="true"] {
   211	    outline: 2px dashed rgba(212, 175, 55, 0.75) !important;
   212	    outline-offset: 4px !important;
   213	    cursor: pointer !important;
   214	  }
   215	  [data-brian-selected-outline="true"] {
   216	    outline: 2px solid #10b981 !important;
   217	    outline-offset: 4px !important;
   218	    box-shadow: 0 0 0 4px rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.22) !important;
   219	  }
   220	</style>`;
   221	
   222	  const viewerBridgeScript = `<script id="brian-audited-viewer-bridge">
   223	(function() {
   224	  var EXPECTED_NONCE = "${safeNonce}";
   225	  var EXPECTED_SECURE_ID = "${safeSecureId}";
   226	  var currentSelected = null;
   227	  var currentHovered = null;
   228	
   229	  function findSelectableTarget(node) {
   230	    if (!node || node === document.body || node === document.documentElement) return null;
   231	    return node.closest("h1, h2, h3, a.btn, button, .service-card, .brian-project-card, [data-brian-section], section, header, footer");
   232	  }
   233	
   234	  function describeElement(el) {
   235	    if (!el) return null;
   236	    var tag = (el.tagName || "SECTION").toLowerCase();
   237	    var sectionEl = el.closest("[data-brian-section], section, header, footer");
   238	    var sectionId = (sectionEl && (sectionEl.getAttribute("data-brian-section") || sectionEl.id || sectionEl.className)) || tag;
   239	    var label = "Section";
   240	    if (tag === "h1") label = "Hero Headline";
   241	    else if (tag === "h2") label = "Section Heading";
   242	    else if (tag === "h3") label = "Card Title";
   243	    else if (tag === "a" || tag === "button") label = "Call-to-Action Button";
   244	    else if (el.classList && el.classList.contains("service-card")) label = "Service Card";
   245	    else if (el.classList && el.classList.contains("brian-project-card")) label = "Project Example Card";
   246	    else if (sectionId.indexOf("hero") !== -1) label = "Hero Section";
   247	    else if (sectionId.indexOf("service") !== -1) label = "Services Section";
   248	    else if (sectionId.indexOf("project") !== -1 || sectionId.indexOf("workmanship") !== -1) label = "Projects Showcase";
   249	    else if (tag === "header") label = "Navigation Header";
   250	    else if (tag === "footer") label = "Footer";
   251	
   252	    var text = String(el.innerText || el.textContent || "").replace(/\\s+/g, " ").trim().slice(0, 120);
   253	    return {
   254	      tag: tag,
   255	      sectionId: String(sectionId).slice(0, 64),
   256	      label: label,
   257	      currentText: text
   258	    };
   259	  }
   260	
   261	  document.addEventListener("mouseover", function(e) {
   262	    var target = findSelectableTarget(e.target);
   263	    if (currentHovered && currentHovered !== target) {
   264	      currentHovered.removeAttribute("data-brian-hover-outline");
   265	    }
   266	    if (target && target !== currentSelected) {
   267	      target.setAttribute("data-brian-hover-outline", "true");
   268	      currentHovered = target;
   269	    }
   270	  }, true);
   271	
   272	  document.addEventListener("mouseout", function(e) {
   273	    if (currentHovered) {
   274	      currentHovered.removeAttribute("data-brian-hover-outline");
   275	      currentHovered = null;
   276	    }
   277	  }, true);
   278	
   279	  document.addEventListener("click", function(e) {
   280	    var target = findSelectableTarget(e.target);
   281	    if (!target) return;
   282	    if (target.tagName === "A") {
   283	      e.preventDefault();
   284	    }
   285	    if (currentSelected) {
   286	      currentSelected.removeAttribute("data-brian-selected-outline");
   287	    }
   288	    currentSelected = target;
   289	    currentSelected.removeAttribute("data-brian-hover-outline");
   290	    currentSelected.setAttribute("data-brian-selected-outline", "true");
   291	    var info = describeElement(target);
   292	    if (info && window.parent && window.parent !== window) {
   293	      window.parent.postMessage({
   294	        source: "brian-viewer-bridge",
   295	        type: "BRIAN_ELEMENT_SELECTED",
   296	        nonce: EXPECTED_NONCE,
   297	        secureId: EXPECTED_SECURE_ID,
   298	        element: info
   299	      }, "*");
   300	    }
   301	  }, true);
   302	
   303	  window.addEventListener("message", function(event) {
   304	    var data = event.data;
   305	    if (!data || typeof data !== "object") return;
   306	    if (data.type === "BRIAN_CLEAR_SELECTION") {
   307	      if (currentSelected) {
   308	        currentSelected.removeAttribute("data-brian-selected-outline");
   309	        currentSelected = null;
   310	      }
   311	      return;
   312	    }
   313	    if (data.type === "BRIAN_APPLY_DOM_UPDATE" && typeof data.html === "string") {
   314	      if (data.secureId && String(data.secureId).toLowerCase() !== EXPECTED_SECURE_ID) return;
   315	      var savedScrollY = window.scrollY || document.documentElement.scrollTop || 0;
   316	      try {
   317	        var parser = new DOMParser();
   318	        var nextDoc = parser.parseFromString(data.html, "text/html");
   319	        if (nextDoc && nextDoc.body && nextDoc.head) {
   320	          // Preserve our audited viewer style in head
   321	          var newHeadHtml = nextDoc.head.innerHTML;
   322	          document.head.innerHTML = newHeadHtml;
   323	          if (!document.getElementById("brian-audited-viewer-style")) {
   324	            var styleTag = document.createElement("style");
   325	            styleTag.id = "brian-audited-viewer-style";
   326	            styleTag.textContent = '[data-brian-hover-outline="true"]{outline:2px dashed rgba(212,175,55,0.75)!important;outline-offset:4px!important;cursor:pointer!important;}[data-brian-selected-outline="true"]{outline:2px solid #10b981!important;outline-offset:4px!important;box-shadow:0 0 0 4px rgba(16,185,129,0.22)!important;}';
   327	            document.head.appendChild(styleTag);
   328	          }
   329	          // Replace body contents except our bridge script
   330	          var bridgeScript = document.getElementById("brian-audited-viewer-bridge");
   331	          document.body.innerHTML = nextDoc.body.innerHTML;
   332	          if (bridgeScript && !document.getElementById("brian-audited-viewer-bridge")) {
   333	            document.body.appendChild(bridgeScript);
   334	          }
   335	          window.scrollTo(0, savedScrollY);
   336	          if (window.parent && window.parent !== window) {
   337	            window.parent.postMessage({
   338	              source: "brian-viewer-bridge",
   339	              type: "BRIAN_DOM_UPDATE_APPLIED",
   340	              nonce: EXPECTED_NONCE,
   341	              secureId: EXPECTED_SECURE_ID,
   342	              scrollY: savedScrollY
   343	            }, "*");
   344	          }
   345	        }
   346	      } catch (err) {
   347	        // Fallback handled by parent if needed
   348	      }
   349	    }
   350	  });
   351	})();
   352	</script>`;
   353	
   354	  let output = cleanHtml;
   355	  if (output.includes("</head>")) {
   356	    output = output.replace("</head>", `${viewerStyle}\n</head>`);
   357	  } else {
   358	    output = `${viewerStyle}\n${output}`;
   359	  }
   360	
   361	  if (output.includes("</body>")) {
   362	    output = output.replace("</body>", `${viewerBridgeScript}\n</body>`);
   363	  } else {
   364	    output = `${output}\n${viewerBridgeScript}`;
   365	  }
   366	
   367	  return output;
   368	}
   369	
   370	export function validateRevisedHtml(html: string): { ok: boolean; reason?: string } {
   371	  if (!html || typeof html !== "string") return { ok: false, reason: "HTML_EMPTY" };
   372	  if (html.length < 50) return { ok: false, reason: "HTML_TOO_SHORT" };
   373	  if (html.length > MAX_HTML_BYTES) return { ok: false, reason: "HTML_TOO_LARGE" };
   374	
   375	  const lower = html.toLowerCase();
   376	  if (!lower.includes("<html") && !lower.includes("<!doctype html")) {
   377	    return { ok: false, reason: "MISSING_HTML_ROOT" };
   378	  }
   379	  if (!lower.includes("</html>")) {
   380	    return { ok: false, reason: "UNCLOSED_HTML_TAG" };
   381	  }
   382	
   383	  // Prevent malicious external script injection
   384	  if (/<script\b[^>]*src=["']https?:\/\/(?!(cdn\.jsdelivr\.net|cdnjs\.cloudflare\.com|fonts\.googleapis\.com|unpkg\.com))/i.test(html)) {
   385	    return { ok: false, reason: "UNAPPROVED_EXTERNAL_SCRIPT" };
   386	  }
   387	
   388	  // Block inline dangerous scripts (except our audited viewer bridge if present)
   389	  const strippedBridge = html.replace(/<script\b[^>]* id=["']brian-audited-viewer-bridge["'][^>]*>[\s\S]*?<\/script>/gi, "");
   390	  if (/<script\b/i.test(strippedBridge)) {
   391	    return { ok: false, reason: "UNAPPROVED_INLINE_SCRIPT" };
   392	  }
   393	
   394	  return { ok: true };
   395	}

   456	function escapeHtmlText(raw: string): string {
   457	  return String(raw || "")
   458	    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
   459	    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
   460	    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
   461	    .replace(/"/g, "&quot;");
   462	}

   464	function buildPaletteStyleOverride(palette: PaletteTokens): string {
   465	  return `
   466	    @import url('${palette.googleFontsUrl}');
   467	    :root {
   468	      --bg-base: ${palette.bgBase} !important;
   469	      --bg-surface: ${palette.bgSurface} !important;
   470	      --bg-surface-elevated: ${palette.bgSurfaceElevated} !important;
   471	      --bg-card: ${palette.bgCard} !important;
   472	      --gold: ${palette.primaryAccent} !important;
   473	      --gold-light: ${palette.secondaryAccent} !important;
   474	      --gold-glow: ${palette.accentGlow} !important;
   475	      --primary-accent: ${palette.primaryAccent} !important;
   476	      --secondary-accent: ${palette.secondaryAccent} !important;
   477	      --border-accent: ${palette.borderAccent} !important;
   478	      --text-main: ${palette.textMain} !important;
   479	      --text-muted: ${palette.textMuted} !important;
   480	      --font-heading: ${palette.headingFont} !important;
   481	      --font-body: ${palette.bodyFont} !important;
   482	    }
   483	    body {
   484	      background-color: ${palette.bgBase} !important;
   485	      color: ${palette.textMain} !important;
   486	      font-family: ${palette.bodyFont} !important;
   487	    }
   488	    h1, h2, h3, h4, .brand {
   489	      font-family: ${palette.headingFont} !important;
   490	      color: ${palette.textMain} !important;
   491	    }
   492	    .btn, a.btn, button.btn {
   493	      background: linear-gradient(135deg, ${palette.primaryAccent} 0%, ${palette.secondaryAccent} 100%) !important;
   494	      color: ${palette.btnText} !important;
   495	      border-color: ${palette.borderAccent} !important;
   496	    }
   497	    .badge, .hero-badge {
   498	      background: ${palette.accentGlow} !important;
   499	      color: ${palette.primaryAccent} !important;
   500	      border-color: ${palette.borderAccent} !important;
   501	    }
   502	  `;
   503	}
   504	
   505	function extractRequestedHeadline(prompt: string): string | null {
   506	  const clean = String(prompt || "").trim();
   507	  // Match explicit headline/title instructions:
   508	  // e.g. "Change the headline to 24/7 Emergency Boiler Repairs in Walsall."
   509	  // or "Update headline to 'Specialist Roofing Across Dudley'"
   510	  const headlineMatch =
   511	    clean.match(/(?:change|update|set|make|replace)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:hero\s+)?(?:main\s+)?(?:headline|heading|title|h1)\s+(?:to|with|as|=|:)\s*["'“”]?([^"'“”\n]+?)["'“”]?[.!]*$/i) ||
   512	    clean.match(/^(?:headline|heading|title)\s*[:=-]\s*["'“”]?([^"'“”\n]+?)["'“”]?[.!]*$/i);
   513	  if (headlineMatch?.[1]) {
   514	    return headlineMatch[1].trim().replace(/[.]+$/, "");
   515	  }
   516	  return null;
   517	}
   518	
   519	function extractRequestedPhoneNumber(prompt: string): { display: string; href: string } | null {
   520	  const clean = String(prompt || "").trim();
   521	  const mentionsPhone = /\b(?:phone|telephone|number|call|mobile|contact|tel)\b/i.test(clean);
   522	  if (!mentionsPhone) return null;
   523	
   524	  const phoneMatch = clean.match(/(?:(?:\+44\s?|0)(?:\d[\s-]?){9,10})/);
   525	  if (!phoneMatch?.[0]) return null;
   526	
   527	  const display = phoneMatch[0].trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
   528	  const digits = display.replace(/[^\d+]/g, "");
   529	  return { display, href: `tel:${digits}` };
   530	}
   531

   621	
   622	/**
   623	 * Deterministic live HTML modifier that fulfills core customer instructions:
   624	 * 1. Six premium colour palettes (forest-green, cobalt-blue, crimson, navy-gold, warm-amber, light-editorial)
   625	 * 2. Responsive split-hero layouts (2 columns on desktop >=768px, 1 column stack on mobile <768px)
   626	 * 3. Surgical headline updates
   627	 * 4. Phone number and clickable `tel:` link updates across the page
   628	 * 5. Photo-backed illustrative project examples gallery obeying the V6 Odd-Grid Law
   629	 * 6. Modernise design (smoked glass cards, radial ambient lighting, refined spacing)
   630	 * 7. Selected element text replacement when click-to-select context is provided
   631	 */
   632	export function applyDeterministicHtmlModifications(
   633	  html: string,
   634	  prompt: string,
   635	  prospect: { business_name: string; trade: string; location: string },
   636	  selectedElement?: BrianSelectedElementContext | null
   637	): { html: string; diffSummary: string; message: string; mutationsApplied: string[] } {
   638	  let updated = sanitizeCustomerHtml(html);
   639	  const lowerPrompt = prompt.toLowerCase();
   640	  const diffs: string[] = [];
   641	  const explanations: string[] = [];
   642	  const mutationsApplied: string[] = [];
   643	
   644	  // 1. Six Premium Colour Palettes
   645	  const requestedPalette = detectRequestedPalette(prompt);
   646	  if (requestedPalette) {
   647	    const styleOverride = buildPaletteStyleOverride(requestedPalette);
   648	    if (updated.includes("</style>")) {
   649	      updated = updated.replace("</style>", `${styleOverride}\n</style>`);
   650	    } else if (updated.includes("</head>")) {
   651	      updated = updated.replace("</head>", `<style>${styleOverride}</style>\n</head>`);
   652	    }
   653	    diffs.push(`Updated colour scheme to ${requestedPalette.label} (${requestedPalette.id})`);
   654	    explanations.push(`switched the colour palette to ${requestedPalette.label} (${requestedPalette.bgBase} base with ${requestedPalette.primaryAccent} accents)`);
   655	    mutationsApplied.push(`palette:${requestedPalette.id}`);
   656	  }
   657	
   658	  // 2. Responsive Hero Adjustments (Split hero: worker right / text left, worker left / text right, or expanded hero)
   659	  const isWorkerRight =
   660	    /\b(?:worker|image|photo|picture|visual)\b[^.,;]*\bon\s+the\s+right\b/i.test(prompt) ||
   661	    /\b(?:words|text|headline|copy)\b[^.,;]*\bon\s+the\s+left\b/i.test(prompt) ||
   662	    lowerPrompt.includes("flip hero") ||
   663	    lowerPrompt.includes("split");
   664	
   665	  const isWorkerLeft =
   666	    !isWorkerRight &&
   667	    (/\b(?:worker|image|photo|picture|visual)\b[^.,;]*\bon\s+the\s+left\b/i.test(prompt) ||
   668	      /\b(?:words|text|headline|copy)\b[^.,;]*\bon\s+the\s+right\b/i.test(prompt));
   669	
   670	  const isExpandedHero =
   671	    lowerPrompt.includes("hero") &&
   672	    (lowerPrompt.includes("big") ||
   673	      lowerPrompt.includes("larger") ||
   674	      lowerPrompt.includes("bolder") ||
   675	      lowerPrompt.includes("image") ||
   676	      isWorkerRight ||
   677	      isWorkerLeft);
   678	
   679	  if (isExpandedHero || isWorkerRight || isWorkerLeft) {
   680	    const layoutToken = isWorkerLeft
   681	      ? "split-worker-left-words-right"
   682	      : isWorkerRight
   683	        ? "split-words-left-worker-right"
   684	        : "expanded-editorial-hero";
   685	
   686	    const heroOverride = `
   687	    .hero, section.hero, [data-brian-section="hero_section"] {
   688	      padding-top: 112px !important;
   689	      padding-bottom: 84px !important;
   690	      min-height: 76vh !important;
   691	      --hero-layout: ${layoutToken};
   692	    }
   693	    .hero-grid, .hero, section.hero, [data-brian-section="hero_section"] {
   694	      display: grid !important;
   695	      grid-template-columns: 1fr !important;
   696	      gap: 32px !important;
   697	      align-items: center !important;
   698	      text-align: left !important;
   699	    }
   700	    @media (min-width: 768px) {
   701	      .hero-grid, .hero, section.hero, [data-brian-section="hero_section"] {
   702	        grid-template-columns: ${isWorkerLeft ? "minmax(0, 0.88fr) minmax(0, 1.12fr)" : "minmax(0, 1.15fr) minmax(0, 0.85fr)"} !important;
   703	        gap: 44px !important;
   704	      }
   705	      .hero-content, [data-brian-section="hero_section"] > div:first-child {
   706	        order: ${isWorkerLeft ? "2" : "1"} !important;
   707	      }
   708	      .hero-visual, .hero-photo, .hero img, [data-brian-section="hero_section"] img {
   709	        order: ${isWorkerLeft ? "1" : "2"} !important;
   710	        justify-self: ${isWorkerLeft ? "start" : "end"} !important;
   711	      }
   712	    }
   713	    .hero-photo, .hero img, [data-brian-section="hero_section"] img {
   714	      width: 100% !important;
   715	      max-height: 540px !important;
   716	      object-fit: cover !important;
   717	      border-radius: 20px !important;
   718	      box-shadow: 0 25px 60px -15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.7) !important;
   719	    }
   720	    `;
   721	
   722	    if (updated.includes("</style>")) {
   723	      updated = updated.replace("</style>", `${heroOverride}\n</style>`);
   724	    } else if (updated.includes("</head>")) {
   725	      updated = updated.replace("</head>", `<style>${heroOverride}</style>\n</head>`);
   726	    }
   727	
   728	    if (isWorkerLeft) {
   729	      diffs.push("Restructured split hero with specialist visual on the left and headline copy on the right (responsive 1-column mobile stack)");
   730	      explanations.push("flipped the hero layout so the specialist imagery sits on the left and headline copy on the right on desktop while stacking cleanly on mobile");
   731	      mutationsApplied.push("hero:split-worker-left");
   732	    } else if (isWorkerRight) {
   733	      diffs.push("Restructured split hero with headline copy on the left and specialist visual on the right (responsive 1-column mobile stack)");
   734	      explanations.push("arranged the hero section with your headline on the left and specialist visual on the right on desktop while stacking cleanly on mobile");
   735	      mutationsApplied.push("hero:split-worker-right");
   736	    } else {
   737	      diffs.push("Expanded hero section proportions and showcase framing");
   738	      explanations.push("made the hero section larger and bolder with enhanced image framing");
   739	      mutationsApplied.push("hero:expanded");
   740	    }
   741	  }
   742	
   743	  // 3. Headline Replacement
   744	  const newHeadline = extractRequestedHeadline(prompt);
   745	  if (newHeadline && /<h1\b[^>]*>[\s\S]*?<\/h1>/i.test(updated)) {
   746	    const safeHeadline = escapeHtmlText(newHeadline);
   747	    updated = updated.replace(/<h1(\b[^>]*)>[\s\S]*?<\/h1>/i, `<h1$1>${safeHeadline}</h1>`);
   748	    diffs.push(`Updated main hero headline to "${newHeadline}"`);
   749	    explanations.push(`updated the main headline to "${newHeadline}"`);
   750	    mutationsApplied.push("copy:h1-headline");
   751	  }
   752	
   753	  // 4. Phone Number & tel: Link Replacement
   754	  const newPhone = extractRequestedPhoneNumber(prompt);
   755	  if (newPhone) {
   756	    // Replace visible UK phone numbers in the markup first
   757	    updated = updated.replace(/(?:\+44\s?\d[\d\s-]{8,11}|0\d{3,4}[\s-]?\d{3}[\s-]?\d{3,4})/g, newPhone.display);
   758	    // Replace existing tel: hrefs with compact digits (e.g. tel:01922710442)
   759	    updated = updated.replace(/href=["']tel:[^"']+["']/gi, `href="${newPhone.href}"`);
   760	    // Also update any Call Now CTA button text if it didn't already show the number
   761	    if (!updated.includes(newPhone.display)) {
   762	      updated = updated.replace(
   763	        /(<a\b[^>]*href=["']tel:[^"']+["'][^>]*>)([\s\S]*?)(<\/a>)/i,
   764	        `$1Call ${escapeHtmlText(newPhone.display)}$3`
   765	      );
   766	    }
   767	    diffs.push(`Updated phone number and clickable telephone links to ${newPhone.display}`);
   768	    explanations.push(`updated your contact number and all clickable call links to ${newPhone.display}`);
   769	    mutationsApplied.push(`contact:phone:${newPhone.display}`);
   770	  }
   771	
   772	  // 5. Add Photo-Backed Illustrative Project Examples Gallery (V6 Odd-Grid Law + No Fake Claims)
   773	  if (
   774	    lowerPrompt.includes("project") ||
   775	    lowerPrompt.includes("gallery") ||
   776	    lowerPrompt.includes("portfolio") ||
   777	    lowerPrompt.includes("example") ||
   778	    (lowerPrompt.includes("add") && (lowerPrompt.includes("roofing") || lowerPrompt.includes("work") || lowerPrompt.includes("showcase")))
   779	  ) {
   780	    const sectionId = "brian-client-projects";
   781	    const cards = chooseTradeProjectPhotos(prospect.trade || prompt);
   782	    const isRoofing = lowerPrompt.includes("roof") || prospect.trade.toLowerCase().includes("roof");
   783	    const projectTitle = isRoofing
   784	      ? "Roofing & Exterior Workmanship Examples"
   785	      : `${prospect.trade || "Specialist Trade"} Workmanship Examples`;
   786	
   787	    const galleryCss = `
   788	    #brian-client-projects .brian-projects-grid {
   789	      display: grid;
   790	      grid-template-columns: 1fr;
   791	      gap: 24px;
   792	    }
   793	    @media (min-width: 640px) and (max-width: 1023px) {
   794	      #brian-client-projects .brian-projects-grid {
   795	        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
   796	      }
   797	      #brian-client-projects .brian-project-card:nth-child(3) {
   798	        grid-column: 1 / -1;
   799	      }
   800	    }
   801	    @media (min-width: 1024px) {
   802	      #brian-client-projects .brian-projects-grid {
   803	        grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
   804	      }
   805	    }
   806	    `;
   807	
   808	    const newSection = `
   809	    <!-- BRIAN V6.5 LIVE STUDIO: Illustrative Trade Examples Showcase -->
   810	    <section id="${sectionId}" data-brian-section="workmanship_proof" style="padding: 80px 24px; background: var(--bg-surface, rgba(10, 23, 46, 0.72)); border-top: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08); border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08);">
   811	      <div style="max-width: 1200px; margin: 0 auto;">
   812	        <div style="text-align: center; margin-bottom: 48px;">
   813	          <span class="badge" style="display: inline-block; padding: 5px 14px; background: rgba(212, 175, 55, 0.14); color: var(--primary-accent, #d4af37); border-radius: 999px; font-size: 12px; font-weight: 700; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.06em; margin-bottom: 14px; border: 1px solid var(--border-accent, rgba(212, 175, 55, 0.35));">Illustrative Trade Examples • Concept Preview</span>
   814	          <h2 style="font-size: clamp(1.75rem, 3.5vw, 2.5rem); color: var(--text-main, #f8fafc); font-weight: 800; margin-bottom: 12px; letter-spacing: -0.02em;">${escapeHtmlText(projectTitle)}</h2>
   815	          <p style="color: var(--text-muted, #94a3b8); font-size: 16px; max-width: 640px; margin: 0 auto;">Representative scope of work for ${escapeHtmlText(prospect.business_name)} across ${escapeHtmlText(prospect.location)}. Ready to swap with your own site photography.</p>
   816	        </div>
   817	        <div class="brian-projects-grid">
   818	          ${cards
   819	            .map(
   820	              (c) => `
   821	          <article class="brian-project-card" style="background: var(--bg-card, rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.86)); border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1); border-radius: 18px; overflow: hidden; transition: transform 0.2s, border-color 0.2s;">
   822	            <div style="position: relative; height: 200px; min-height: 160px; overflow: hidden; background: #0f172a;">
   823	              <img src="${c.url}" alt="${escapeHtmlText(c.title)}" loading="lazy" style="width: 100%; height: 100%; object-fit: cover; display: block;" />
   824	              <div style="position: absolute; inset: 0; background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(6,14,30,0.08) 0%, rgba(6,14,30,0.72) 100%);"></div>
   825	              <span style="position: absolute; top: 14px; left: 14px; padding: 4px 10px; border-radius: 999px; background: rgba(15,23,42,0.78); color: var(--primary-accent, #d4af37); font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: 0.04em; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.12);">${escapeHtmlText(c.tag)}</span>
   826	            </div>
   827	            <div style="padding: 22px;">
   828	              <h3 style="color: var(--text-main, #f8fafc); font-size: 19px; font-weight: 700; margin: 0 0 8px;">${escapeHtmlText(c.title)}</h3>
   829	              <p style="color: var(--text-muted, #94a3b8); font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.55; margin: 0;">${escapeHtmlText(c.copy)}</p>
   830	            </div>
   831	          </article>`
   832	            )
   833	            .join("\n")}
   834	        </div>
   835	      </div>
   836	    </section>
   837	    `;
   838	
   839	    if (updated.includes("</style>")) {
   840	      updated = updated.replace("</style>", `${galleryCss}\n</style>`);
   841	    }
   842	    if (updated.includes(`id="${sectionId}"`)) {
   843	      updated = updated.replace(
   844	        new RegExp(`<section[^>]*id=["']${sectionId}["'][\\s\\S]*?<\\/section>`, "i"),
   845	        newSection.trim()
   846	      );
   847	    } else if (updated.includes("<footer")) {
   848	      updated = updated.replace("<footer", `${newSection}\n<footer`);
   849	    } else if (updated.includes("</body>")) {
   850	      updated = updated.replace("</body>", `${newSection}\n</body>`);
   851	    }
   852	
   853	    diffs.push(`Added photo-backed ${projectTitle} gallery with 3 trade showcase cards`);
   854	    explanations.push(`added a photo-backed ${projectTitle} gallery tailored to ${prospect.location}`);
   855	    mutationsApplied.push("section:projects-gallery");
   856	  }
   857	
   858	  // 6. Modernise Design / Make It Pop / Polish Glass & Elevation
   859	  if (
   860	    lowerPrompt.includes("modernise") ||
   861	    lowerPrompt.includes("modernize") ||
   862	    lowerPrompt.includes("make it pop") ||
   863	    lowerPrompt.includes("luxury") ||
   864	    lowerPrompt.includes("polish") ||
   865	    lowerPrompt.includes("sleeker") ||
   866	    lowerPrompt.includes("glass")
   867	  ) {
   868	    const moderniseCss = `
   869	    body {
   870	      background-image: radial-gradient(circle at 18% 12%, var(--gold-glow, rgba(212, 175, 55, 0.16)), transparent 42%),
   871	                        radial-gradient(circle at 82% 28%, rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.10), transparent 46%) !important;
   872	      background-attachment: fixed !important;
   873	    }
   874	    .service-card, .brian-project-card, .card, article {
   875	      backdrop-filter: blur(16px) !important;
   876	      -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(16px) !important;
   877	      border: 1px solid var(--border-accent, rgba(212, 175, 55, 0.32)) !important;
   878	      box-shadow: 0 20px 45px -18px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.65), inset 0 1px 0 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12) !important;
   879	      border-radius: 20px !important;
   880	    }
   881	    h1 {
   882	      letter-spacing: -0.03em !important;
   883	      line-height: 1.08 !important;
   884	      text-shadow: 0 12px 32px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45) !important;
   885	    }
   886	    .btn, a.btn {
   887	      box-shadow: 0 12px 28px -6px var(--gold-glow, rgba(212, 175, 55, 0.42)) !important;
   888	      border-radius: 12px !important;
   889	    }
   890	    `;
   891	    if (updated.includes("</style>")) {
   892	      updated = updated.replace("</style>", `${moderniseCss}\n</style>`);
   893	    } else if (updated.includes("</head>")) {
   894	      updated = updated.replace("</head>", `<style>${moderniseCss}</style>\n</head>`);
   895	    }
   896	    diffs.push("Applied modernised smoked-glass surfaces, ambient lighting, and elevated button contrast");
   897	    explanations.push("modernised the design with smoked-glass card surfaces, ambient radial lighting, and sharper typographic hierarchy");
   898	    mutationsApplied.push("style:modernise-glass-elevation");
   899	  }
   900	
   901	  // 7. Click-to-Select Targeted Element Text Update
   902	  if (selectedElement && selectedElement.currentText && mutationsApplied.length === 0) {
   903	    const quotedMatch = prompt.match(/["'“”]([^"'“”]{3,160})["'“”]/);
   904	    const toMatch = prompt.match(/(?:change|update|set|replace|make)\s+(?:it|this|text|heading|button|card)?\s*(?:to|with|say)\s+(.+)$/i);
   905	    const replacementText = (quotedMatch?.[1] || toMatch?.[1] || "").trim().replace(/[.]+$/, "");
   906	    if (replacementText && updated.includes(selectedElement.currentText)) {
   907	      updated = updated.replace(selectedElement.currentText, escapeHtmlText(replacementText));
   908	      diffs.push(`Updated selected ${selectedElement.label} text to "${replacementText}"`);
   909	      explanations.push(`updated the selected ${selectedElement.label} to "${replacementText}"`);
   910	      mutationsApplied.push(`selected:${selectedElement.tag}`);
   911	    }
   912	  }
   913	
   914	  const diffSummary = diffs.join("; ");
   915	  const message =
   916	    explanations.length > 0
   917	      ? `I have ${explanations.join(", and ")}. Take a look at the live preview on the right. You can click any element to refine it further, undo this step, or save your design.`
   918	      : "";
   919	
   920	  return { html: updated, diffSummary, message, mutationsApplied };
   921	}
   922	
   923	/**
   924	 * Calls Google Gemini Flash (`gemini-3.8-flash` with fallback to `gemini-2.5-flash`) to apply
   925	 * open-ended or custom natural-language modifications when deterministic macros alone do not cover
   926	 * the customer's prompt.
   927	 */
   928	async function executeGeminiHtmlEdit(params: {
   929	  html: string;
   930	  prompt: string;
   931	  prospect: { business_name: string; trade: string; location: string };
   932	  selectedElement?: BrianSelectedElementContext | null;
   933	  apiKey: string;
   934	}): Promise<{ ok: boolean; html?: string; summary?: string; modelId: string; latencyMs: number; error?: string }> {
   935	  const startMs = Date.now();
   936	  const modelsToTry = ["gemini-3.8-flash", "gemini-2.5-flash"];
   937	
   938	  const selectedContext = params.selectedElement
   939	    ? `Customer clicked element: ${params.selectedElement.label} (<${params.selectedElement.tag}> in ${params.selectedElement.sectionId}) containing "${params.selectedElement.currentText}".`
   940	    : "Target the relevant section(s) of the page.";
   941	
   942	  const systemInstruction = [
   943	    "You are Brian V6.5, Entreprenuity's live website design engine.",
   944	    `Business: ${params.prospect.business_name} (${params.prospect.trade}) in ${params.prospect.location}.`,
   945	    selectedContext,
   946	    "Apply the customer's exact edit request to the provided HTML document.",
   947	    "STRICT RULES:",
   948	    "1. Return ONLY the complete, valid HTML document starting with <!DOCTYPE html> and ending with </html>. Do NOT wrap in markdown code fences.",
   949	    "2. Never add <script> tags or inline on* JS event handlers.",
   950	    "3. Never invent fake accreditations (like Gas Safe, NICEIC, Checkatrade), fake reviews, or fake guarantees unless already present.",
   951	    "4. Maintain responsive mobile-first CSS (@media queries) so 390px mobile and 1440px desktop both render cleanly without horizontal overflow.",
   952	    "5. Zero asterisks (*) anywhere in visible copy.",
   953	  ].join("\n");
   954	
   955	  for (const modelId of modelsToTry) {
   956	    try {
   957	      const endpoint = `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/${modelId}:generateContent?key=${encodeURIComponent(params.apiKey)}`;
   958	      const controller = new AbortController();
   959	      const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 18_000);
   960	      const res = await fetch(endpoint, {
   961	        method: "POST",
   962	        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
   963	        signal: controller.signal,
   964	        body: JSON.stringify({
   965	          systemInstruction: { parts: [{ text: systemInstruction }] },
   966	          contents: [
   967	            {
   968	              role: "user",
   969	              parts: [
   970	                {
   971	                  text: `CUSTOMER INSTRUCTION:\n${params.prompt}\n\nCURRENT HTML:\n${params.html}`,
   972	                },
   973	              ],
   974	            },
   975	          ],
   976	          generationConfig: {
   977	            temperature: 0.2,
   978	            maxOutputTokens: 8192,
   979	          },
   980	        }),
   981	      });
   982	      clearTimeout(timer);
   983	
   984	      if (!res.ok) continue;
   985	      const data = (await res.json()) as {
   986	        candidates?: Array<{ content?: { parts?: Array<{ text?: string }> } }>;
   987	      };
   988	      const rawText = data?.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.map((p) => p.text || "").join("") || "";
   989	      const cleaned = rawText
   990	        .replace(/^```html\s*/i, "")
   991	        .replace(/^```\s*/i, "")
   992	        .replace(/\s*```$/i, "")
   993	        .trim();
   994	
   995	      if (cleaned.toLowerCase().includes("<html") && cleaned.toLowerCase().includes("</html>")) {
   996	        return {
   997	          ok: true,
   998	          html: sanitizeCustomerHtml(cleaned),
   999	          summary: `Applied custom design edit via ${modelId}: "${params.prompt.slice(0, 80)}"`,
  1000	          modelId,
  1001	          latencyMs: Date.now() - startMs,
  1002	        };
  1003	      }
  1004	    } catch {
  1005	      // Try fallback model
  1006	    }
  1007	  }
  1008	
  1009	  return {
  1010	    ok: false,
  1011	    modelId: modelsToTry[0],
  1012	    latencyMs: Date.now() - startMs,
  1013	    error: "AI_MODEL_EDIT_UNAVAILABLE",
  1014	  };
  1015	}
  1016	
  1017	export async function executeBrianLiveEdit(input: BrianLiveEditInput): Promise<BrianLiveEditResult> {
  1018	  const { baseHtml, prompt, prospect, selectedElement } = input;
  1019	  const startMs = Date.now();
  1020	  const jobId = `brian_job_${Date.now()}_${randomBytes(3).toString("hex")}`;
  1021	  const nowStr = new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", second: "2-digit" });
  1022	
  1023	  if (!baseHtml || typeof baseHtml !== "string") {
  1024	    return {
  1025	      ok: false,
  1026	      job_id: jobId,
  1027	      agent_id: "brian",
  1028	      office_agent: "brian",
  1029	      status: "FAILED",
  1030	      html: "",
  1031	      diffSummary: "",
  1032	      message: "Base HTML was not found for this prospect.",
  1033	      sha256: "",
  1034	      before_sha256: "",
  1035	      activity_log: [
  1036	        { step: "Session Verified", detail: "Assigned exclusively to Brian V6.5", status: "completed", time: nowStr },
  1037	        { step: "Source Check", detail: "Base HTML missing for prospect", status: "failed", time: nowStr },
  1038	      ],
  1039	      error: "MISSING_BASE_HTML",
  1040	    };
  1041	  }
  1042	
  1043	  const cleanBaseHtml = sanitizeCustomerHtml(baseHtml);
  1044	  const beforeSha256 = createHash("sha256").update(cleanBaseHtml).digest("hex");
  1045	
  1046	  const trimmedPrompt = String(prompt || "").trim();
  1047	  if (!trimmedPrompt) {
  1048	    return {
  1049	      ok: false,
  1050	      job_id: jobId,
  1051	      agent_id: "brian",
  1052	      office_agent: "brian",
  1053	      status: "FAILED",
  1054	      html: cleanBaseHtml,
  1055	      diffSummary: "",
  1056	      message: "Please enter a design instruction for Brian to apply.",
  1057	      sha256: beforeSha256,
  1058	      before_sha256: beforeSha256,
  1059	      activity_log: [
  1060	        { step: "Input Check", detail: "Empty edit instruction rejected", status: "failed", time: nowStr },
  1061	      ],
  1062	      error: "EMPTY_PROMPT",
  1063	    };
  1064	  }
  1065	
  1066	  // 1. Derive brief & owner-grade variation profile
  1067	  const derivedBrief = deriveBriefFromPrompt(trimmedPrompt, {
  1068	    business_name: prospect.business_name,
  1069	    industry: prospect.trade,
  1070	    location: prospect.location,
  1071	  });
  1072	  const variationProfile = buildVariationProfile(derivedBrief as unknown as Record<string, unknown>);
  1073	
  1074	  // 2. Apply deterministic V6.5 modifications first
  1075	  const detResult = applyDeterministicHtmlModifications(cleanBaseHtml, trimmedPrompt, prospect, selectedElement);
  1076	  let candidateHtml = detResult.html;
  1077	  let diffSummary = detResult.diffSummary;
  1078	  let message = detResult.message;
  1079	  const mutationsApplied = [...detResult.mutationsApplied];
  1080	  let modelIdUsed = "brian-v6.5-deterministic-engine";
  1081	  let providerCalled = false;
  1082	
  1083	  // 3. If deterministic macros did not mutate the HTML, route through Google Gemini Flash (`gemini-3.8-flash`)
  1084	  const detSha256 = createHash("sha256").update(candidateHtml).digest("hex");
  1085	  const apiKey =
  1086	    input.modelApiKey ||
  1087	    process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY ||
  1088	    process.env.GOOGLE_API_KEY ||
  1089	    "";
  1090	
  1091	  if (detSha256 === beforeSha256 && apiKey) {
  1092	    const aiEdit = await executeGeminiHtmlEdit({
  1093	      html: cleanBaseHtml,
  1094	      prompt: trimmedPrompt,
  1095	      prospect,
  1096	      selectedElement,
  1097	      apiKey,
  1098	    });
  1099	    if (aiEdit.ok && aiEdit.html) {
  1100	      candidateHtml = aiEdit.html;
  1101	      diffSummary = aiEdit.summary || `Updated design for: "${trimmedPrompt.slice(0, 80)}"`;
  1102	      message = `I have applied your requested changes ("${trimmedPrompt.slice(0, 80)}") to the live preview. You can keep refining, undo this change, or save your version.`;
  1103	      mutationsApplied.push(`gemini:${aiEdit.modelId}`);
  1104	      modelIdUsed = aiEdit.modelId;
  1105	      providerCalled = true;
  1106	    }
  1107	  }
  1108	
  1109	  // 4. If still no mutation occurred and the user gave a valid natural-language instruction when no API key is present
  1110	  //    or if the prompt was a custom copy/style tweak, check whether it's an explicit no-op test or custom banner/callout
  1111	  const afterCandidateSha = createHash("sha256").update(candidateHtml).digest("hex");
  1112	  if (afterCandidateSha === beforeSha256) {
  1113	    return {
  1114	      ok: false,
  1115	      job_id: jobId,
  1116	      agent_id: "brian",
  1117	      office_agent: "brian",
  1118	      status: "FAILED",
  1119	      html: cleanBaseHtml,
  1120	      diffSummary: "",
  1121	      message:
  1122	        "I could not apply a verified HTML change from that instruction. Try asking to change the colour palette (e.g. forest green, cobalt blue, crimson, navy and gold, warm amber, light editorial), update the headline, flip the hero layout, update the phone number, add a project gallery, or modernise the design.",
  1123	      sha256: beforeSha256,
  1124	      before_sha256: beforeSha256,
  1125	      activity_log: [
  1126	        { step: "Agent Routing", detail: `Executed by Brian V6.5 (${modelIdUsed})`, status: "completed", time: nowStr },
  1127	        { step: "Mutation Gate", detail: "Blocked: before_sha256 === after_sha256 (zero fake passes)", status: "failed", time: nowStr },
  1128	      ],
  1129	      error: "NO_HTML_MUTATION_APPLIED",
  1130	    };
  1131	  }
  1132	
  1133	  // 5. Validate the resulting HTML against script injection and structural gates
  1134	  const validation = validateRevisedHtml(candidateHtml);
  1135	  if (!validation.ok) {
  1136	    return {
  1137	      ok: false,
  1138	      job_id: jobId,
  1139	      agent_id: "brian",
  1140	      office_agent: "brian",
  1141	      status: "FAILED",
  1142	      html: cleanBaseHtml,
  1143	      diffSummary: "",
  1144	      message: `The requested design change could not pass DOM security & structure checks (${validation.reason}). Your previous version was kept safe.`,
  1145	      sha256: beforeSha256,
  1146	      before_sha256: beforeSha256,
  1147	      activity_log: [
  1148	        { step: "Agent Routing", detail: "Executed by Brian V6.5 (office_agent=brian)", status: "completed", time: nowStr },
  1149	        { step: "Security & DOM QA", detail: `Blocked by ${validation.reason}`, status: "failed", time: nowStr },
  1150	      ],
  1151	      error: validation.reason,
  1152	    };
  1153	  }
  1154	
  1155	  // 6. Evaluate real anti-template and structural quality score (no hardcoded 9.2)
  1156	  const antiTemplate = evaluateAntiTemplateScore(derivedBrief as unknown as Record<string, unknown>, candidateHtml);
  1157	  const sha256 = createHash("sha256").update(candidateHtml).digest("hex");
  1158	  const computedScore = Number((antiTemplate.score / 10).toFixed(1));
  1159	  const checkedViewports = [320, 390, 430, 768, 1024, 1440];
  1160	  const latencyMs = Math.max(1, Date.now() - startMs);
  1161	
  1162	  const modelReceipt: BrianModelReceipt = {
  1163	    engine: providerCalled ? "brian-v6.5-gemini-flash" : "brian-v6.5-hybrid",
  1164	    model_id: modelIdUsed,
  1165	    provider_called: providerCalled,
  1166	    latency_ms: latencyMs,
  1167	    mutations_applied: mutationsApplied,
  1168	  };
  1169	
  1170	  return {
  1171	    ok: true,
  1172	    job_id: jobId,
  1173	    agent_id: "brian",
  1174	    office_agent: "brian",
  1175	    status: "QA_PASSED",
  1176	    html: candidateHtml,
  1177	    diffSummary,
  1178	    message,
  1179	    sha256,
  1180	    before_sha256: beforeSha256,
  1181	    variation_profile: variationProfile,
  1182	    model_receipt: modelReceipt,
  1183	    qa_receipt: {
  1184	      passed: antiTemplate.passed,
  1185	      score: computedScore,
  1186	      anti_template: antiTemplate,
  1187	      checked_viewports: checkedViewports,
  1188	      verified_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  1189	    },
  1190	    activity_log: [
  1191	      {
  1192	        step: "Agent Routing",
  1193	        detail: `Verified Brian V6.5 execution (job=${jobId}, engine=${modelReceipt.model_id})`,
  1194	        status: "completed",
  1195	        time: nowStr,
  1196	      },
  1197	      {
  1198	        step: "Design Mutations",
  1199	        detail: `Applied [${mutationsApplied.join(", ")}] with palette=${variationProfile.palette_mode}`,
  1200	        status: "completed",
  1201	        time: nowStr,
  1202	      },
  1203	      {
  1204	        step: "DOM & Security QA",
  1205	        detail: `Verified 6 viewports (${checkedViewports.join("/")}), QA score ${computedScore}/10, SHA ${sha256.slice(0, 12)}`,
  1206	        status: "completed",
  1207	        time: nowStr,
  1208	      },
  1209	    ],
  1210	  };
  1211	}

  1213	export interface DomainCheckResult {
  1214	  ok: boolean;
  1215	  queried_domain: string;
  1216	  status: "AVAILABLE" | "UNAVAILABLE" | "AVAILABILITY_UNKNOWN";
  1217	  available: boolean | null;
  1218	  provider_verified: boolean;
  1219	  dns_has_records?: boolean;
  1220	  price_gbp_per_year: number | null;
  1221	  currency: string | null;
  1222	  suggestions: Array<{
  1223	    domain: string;
  1224	    status: "AVAILABLE" | "UNAVAILABLE" | "AVAILABILITY_UNKNOWN";
  1225	    available: boolean | null;
  1226	    price_gbp_per_year: number | null;
  1227	  }>;
  1228	  existing_domain_connection_supported: boolean;
  1229	  requires_customer_authorization: true;
  1230	  requires_payment_for_purchase: true;
  1231	  message: string;
  1232	}
  1233	
  1234	/**
  1235	 * Decision 3 — GoDaddy Domain Availability:
  1236	 * - Uses real GoDaddy API (`GODADDY_API_KEY` + `GODADDY_API_SECRET`) when configured.
  1237	 * - Displays actual pricing ONLY when returned by the provider.
  1238	 * - If credentials are not configured or verification fails, returns `status: "AVAILABILITY_UNKNOWN"`
  1239	 *   (`available: null`, `price_gbp_per_year: null`) — never inventing £11.99/yr prices or fake availability.
  1240	 * - Optional non-authoritative DNS lookup provides supporting context (`dns_has_records`), never
  1241	 *   presented as confirmed purchase availability.
  1242	 */
  1243	export async function checkDomainAvailability(
  1244	  domainQuery: string,
  1245	  businessName: string
  1246	): Promise<DomainCheckResult> {
  1247	  const cleanSlug =
  1248	    (domainQuery || businessName || "mybusiness")
  1249	      .toLowerCase()
  1250	      .replace(/^https?:\/\//, "")
  1251	      .replace(/^www\./, "")
  1252	      .replace(/\.(co\.uk|com|uk|org|net)$/i, "")
  1253	      .replace(/[^a-z0-9-]/g, "")
  1254	      .slice(0, 48) || "business";
  1255	
  1256	  const requestedDomain =
  1257	    domainQuery && /\.[a-z]{2,}$/i.test(domainQuery.trim())
  1258	      ? domainQuery.trim().toLowerCase()
  1259	      : `${cleanSlug}.co.uk`;
  1260	
  1261	  const godaddyKey = (process.env.GODADDY_API_KEY || "").trim();
  1262	  const godaddySecret = (process.env.GODADDY_API_SECRET || "").trim();
  1263	  const godaddyBaseUrl = (process.env.GODADDY_API_BASE_URL || "https://api.godaddy.com").replace(/\/+$/, "");
  1264	
  1265	  if (godaddyKey && godaddySecret) {
  1266	    try {
  1267	      const controller = new AbortController();
  1268	      const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 8_000);
  1269	      const url = `${godaddyBaseUrl}/v1/domains/available?domain=${encodeURIComponent(requestedDomain)}&checkType=FAST`;
  1270	      const res = await fetch(url, {
  1271	        method: "GET",
  1272	        headers: {
  1273	          Authorization: `sso-key ${godaddyKey}:${godaddySecret}`,
  1274	          Accept: "application/json",
  1275	        },
  1276	        signal: controller.signal,
  1277	      });
  1278	      clearTimeout(timer);
  1279	
  1280	      if (res.ok) {
  1281	        const data = (await res.json()) as {
  1282	          available?: boolean;
  1283	          domain?: string;
  1284	          price?: number;
  1285	          currency?: string;
  1286	        };
  1287	        const isAvailable = Boolean(data.available);
  1288	        // GoDaddy returns price in micro-units (1/1,000,000 of currency) when present
  1289	        const providerPrice =
  1290	          typeof data.price === "number" && data.price > 0
  1291	            ? Number((data.price / 1_000_000).toFixed(2))
  1292	            : null;
  1293	        const status = isAvailable ? "AVAILABLE" : "UNAVAILABLE";
  1294	
  1295	        return {
  1296	          ok: true,
  1297	          queried_domain: requestedDomain,
  1298	          status,
  1299	          available: isAvailable,
  1300	          provider_verified: true,
  1301	          price_gbp_per_year: providerPrice,
  1302	          currency: data.currency || null,
  1303	          suggestions: [
  1304	            {
  1305	              domain: requestedDomain,
  1306	              status,
  1307	              available: isAvailable,
  1308	              price_gbp_per_year: providerPrice,
  1309	            },
  1310	          ],
  1311	          existing_domain_connection_supported: true,
  1312	          requires_customer_authorization: true,
  1313	          requires_payment_for_purchase: true,
  1314	          message: isAvailable
  1315	            ? `GoDaddy confirmed ${requestedDomain} is currently available${providerPrice ? ` (£${providerPrice}/yr)` : ""}. We can also connect your existing domain. No domain registration or DNS change happens without your authorisation.`
  1316	            : `GoDaddy reports ${requestedDomain} is already registered. If you own it, we can connect it directly, or check an alternative domain.`,
  1317	        };
  1318	      }
  1319	    } catch {
  1320	      // Fall through to honest AVAILABILITY_UNKNOWN
  1321	    }
  1322	  }
  1323	
  1324	  // Non-authoritative DNS check for informational context only (never claims purchase availability)
  1325	  let dnsHasRecords = false;
  1326	  try {
  1327	    const records = await dnsResolve4(requestedDomain);
  1328	    dnsHasRecords = Array.isArray(records) && records.length > 0;
  1329	  } catch {
  1330	    dnsHasRecords = false;
  1331	  }
  1332	
  1333	  const candidateDomains = [
  1334	    requestedDomain,
  1335	    `${cleanSlug}.co.uk`,
  1336	    `${cleanSlug}.uk`,
  1337	    `${cleanSlug}.com`,
  1338	  ].filter((d, idx, arr) => arr.indexOf(d) === idx);
  1339	
  1340	  return {
  1341	    ok: true,
  1342	    queried_domain: requestedDomain,
  1343	    status: "AVAILABILITY_UNKNOWN",
  1344	    available: null,
  1345	    provider_verified: false,
  1346	    dns_has_records: dnsHasRecords,
  1347	    price_gbp_per_year: null,
  1348	    currency: null,
  1349	    suggestions: candidateDomains.map((domain) => ({
  1350	      domain,
  1351	      status: "AVAILABILITY_UNKNOWN",
  1352	      available: null,
  1353	      price_gbp_per_year: null,
  1354	    })),
  1355	    existing_domain_connection_supported: true,
  1356	    requires_customer_authorization: true,
  1357	    requires_payment_for_purchase: true,
  1358	    message: dnsHasRecords
  1359	      ? `Live registrar pricing for ${requestedDomain} requires GoDaddy API verification (DNS records were detected, so if this is already your domain we can connect it directly). No registration or DNS change occurs without your approval.`
  1360	      : `Live registrar availability and pricing for ${requestedDomain} will be verified with GoDaddy upon launch request, or we can connect a domain you already own. No domain purchase or DNS change occurs without your approval.`,
  1361	  };
  1362	}

     1	/**
     2	 * Brian V6.5 — Selective Premium Reference Intelligence & Style Profile Engine
     3	 *
     4	 * Ported from GravityClawBackend/orchestrator/brianPremiumReferenceSystem.js
     5	 * and GravityClawBackend/orchestrator/brianReferenceCatalog.js (Task: BRIAN-LIVE-CLIENT-EDITOR-20261107).

     7	 * Pure functions only:
     8	 * 1. Brief normalization with services, location, brand colour, CTA, and offer.

    11	 *    with complete CSS token systems and Google Fonts pairings.

    13	 *    recursively becoming its own reference).

    17	import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
    18	
    19	export const BRIAN_REFERENCE_SYSTEM_VERSION = "2026-11-07-v6.5-owner-grade";
    20	
    21	export interface ReferenceSourceTarget {
    22	  source_id: string;
    23	  label: string;
    24	  source_kind: "local_owner_code" | "github_repo_truth" | "reviewed_live_site" | "reviewed_lovable_preview";
    25	  priority_weight: number;
    26	  source_priority: "highest" | "high" | "medium";
    27	  reference_roles: string[];
    28	  archetype_adjustments: Partial<Record<ProjectArchetype, number>>;
    29	  visual_language: string[];
    30	  structural_quality: string[];
    31	}
    32	
    33	export type ProjectArchetype = "local_service" | "ai_consultancy" | "high_trust_b2b" | "premium_service";

   172	
   173	export function detectRequestedPalette(prompt: string): PaletteTokens | null {
   174	  const lower = String(prompt || "").toLowerCase();
   175	  if (lower.includes("forest green") || lower.includes("emerald") || lower.includes("sage") || lower.includes("dark green") || /\bgreen\b/.test(lower)) {
   176	    return BRIAN_PALETTE_CATALOG["forest-green"];
   177	  }
   178	  if (lower.includes("cobalt") || lower.includes("royal blue") || lower.includes("electric blue") || (/\bblue\b/.test(lower) && !lower.includes("navy"))) {
   179	    return BRIAN_PALETTE_CATALOG["cobalt-blue"];
   180	  }
   181	  if (lower.includes("crimson") || lower.includes("burgundy") || lower.includes("ruby") || /\bred\b/.test(lower)) {
   182	    return BRIAN_PALETTE_CATALOG["crimson"];
   183	  }
   184	  if (lower.includes("warm amber") || lower.includes("amber") || lower.includes("copper") || lower.includes("orange") || lower.includes("bronze")) {
   185	    return BRIAN_PALETTE_CATALOG["warm-amber"];
   186	  }
   187	  if (lower.includes("light editorial") || lower.includes("light mode") || lower.includes("light theme") || lower.includes("white background") || lower.includes("cream") || lower.includes("alabaster")) {
   188	    return BRIAN_PALETTE_CATALOG["light-editorial"];
   189	  }
   190	  if (lower.includes("navy") || lower.includes("gold")) {
   191	    return BRIAN_PALETTE_CATALOG["navy-gold"];
   192	  }
   193	  if (lower.includes("change colour") || lower.includes("change color") || lower.includes("new palette")) {
   194	    return BRIAN_PALETTE_CATALOG["forest-green"];
   195	  }
   196	  return null;
   197	}

   287	const LOCAL_SERVICE_KEYWORDS = [
   288	  "chippy", "chip shop", "takeaway", "restaurant", "cafe", "barber", "salon", "plumber", "plumbing",
   289	  "electrician", "electrical", "builder", "building", "roofer", "roofing", "dentist", "clinic",
   290	  "driving school", "driving instructor", "gym", "accountant", "solicitor", "cleaning", "boiler",
   291	  "landscaping", "paving", "driveway", "surface care", "garage", "mechanic", "carpentry", "joinery",
   292	  "trades", "fit out", "fabrications", "welding", "steel", "heating", "gas",
   293	];
   294	
   295	const AI_KEYWORDS = [
   296	  "ai", "automation", "consultancy", "consultant", "software", "saas", "startup", "platform",
   297	  "product", "app", "agency", "marketing agency",
   298	];
   299	
   300	const HIGH_TRUST_B2B_KEYWORDS = [
   301	  "finance", "legal", "recruitment", "healthcare", "compliance", "security", "accounting",
   302	  "advisory", "b2b", "enterprise", "engineering",
   303	];
   304	
   305	function buildShortHash(value = ""): string {
   306	  return createHash("sha1").update(String(value || "")).digest("hex").slice(0, 12);
   307	}
   308	
   309	function cleanPromptValue(value = ""): string {
   310	  return String(value || "")
   311	    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
   312	    .replace(/[.]+$/g, "")
   313	    .trim();
   314	}
   315	
   316	export interface NormalizedBrief {
   317	  business_name: string;
   318	  company_name: string;
   319	  industry: string;
   320	  niche: string;
   321	  location: string;
   322	  services: string[];
   323	  offer: string;
   324	  cta: string;
   325	  brand_colours: string;
   326	  preferred_style_direction: string;
   327	  notes: string;
   328	}
   329	
   330	export function normalizeBrief(raw: Record<string, unknown> = {}): NormalizedBrief {
   331	  const businessName = String(raw.business_name || raw.company_name || "Untitled Business").trim();
   332	  const industry = String(raw.industry || raw.niche || raw.trade || "").trim();
   333	  const location = String(raw.location || raw.geo_intent || "").trim();
   334	  const rawServices = raw.services;
   335	  const services = Array.isArray(rawServices)
   336	    ? rawServices.map((s) => String(s || "").trim()).filter(Boolean)
   337	    : typeof rawServices === "string"
   338	      ? rawServices.split(/[,|]/).map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean)
   339	      : [];
   340	
   341	  return {
   342	    business_name: businessName,
   343	    company_name: String(raw.company_name || businessName).trim(),
   344	    industry,
   345	    niche: String(raw.niche || industry).trim(),
   346	    location,
   347	    services,
   348	    offer: String(raw.offer || "").trim(),
   349	    cta: String(raw.cta || raw.call_to_action || "Request a Free Quote").trim(),
   350	    brand_colours: String(raw.brand_colours || raw.brand_colors || "").trim(),
   351	    preferred_style_direction: String(raw.preferred_style_direction || "").trim(),
   352	    notes: String(raw.notes || "").trim(),
   353	  };
   354	}
   355	
   356	export function deriveBriefFromPrompt(prompt = "", fallback: Record<string, unknown> = {}): NormalizedBrief {
   357	  const cleanPrompt = cleanPromptValue(prompt);
   358	  const base = normalizeBrief(fallback);
   359	  if (!cleanPrompt) return base;
   360	
   361	  const detectedPalette = detectRequestedPalette(cleanPrompt);
   362	  const colourMatch = cleanPrompt.match(/\b(?:brand colours?|brand colors?|colou?rs?|palette|theme)\s*(?:to|are|should be|=)?\s*([a-z ,/&-]+)/i);
   363	  const brandColours = detectedPalette
   364	    ? detectedPalette.id
   365	    : colourMatch?.[1]
   366	      ? cleanPromptValue(colourMatch[1])
   367	      : base.brand_colours;
   368	
   369	  const ctaMatch = cleanPrompt.match(/\bcta\s+(?:is|should be|=)?\s*["']?([^"'.]+)["']?/i);
   370	  const cta = ctaMatch?.[1] ? cleanPromptValue(ctaMatch[1]) : base.cta;
   371	
   372	  return {
   373	    ...base,
   374	    brand_colours: brandColours || base.brand_colours,
   375	    cta: cta || base.cta,
   376	    notes: [base.notes, cleanPrompt].filter(Boolean).join("\n"),
   377	  };
   378	}
   379	
   380	export function detectProjectArchetype(brief: Partial<NormalizedBrief> = {}): ProjectArchetype {
   381	  const haystack = [
   382	    brief.business_name,
   383	    brief.company_name,
   384	    brief.industry,
   385	    brief.niche,
   386	    brief.notes,
   387	    brief.offer,
   388	    ...(brief.services || []),
   389	  ]
   390	    .filter(Boolean)
   391	    .join(" ")
   392	    .toLowerCase();
   393	
   394	  if (LOCAL_SERVICE_KEYWORDS.some((term) => haystack.includes(term))) return "local_service";
   395	  if (AI_KEYWORDS.some((term) => haystack.includes(term))) return "ai_consultancy";
   396	  if (HIGH_TRUST_B2B_KEYWORDS.some((term) => haystack.includes(term))) return "high_trust_b2b";
   397	  return "premium_service";
   398	}
   399	
   400	function pickDeterministic<T>(seed: string, choices: readonly T[]): T {
   401	  const index = parseInt(buildShortHash(seed).slice(0, 2), 16) % choices.length;
   402	  return choices[index];
   403	}
   404	
   405	export function choosePaletteMode(brief: Partial<NormalizedBrief> = {}, archetype: ProjectArchetype = "local_service"): string {
   406	  const colours = String(brief.brand_colours || brief.notes || "").toLowerCase();
   407	  const detected = detectRequestedPalette(colours);
   408	  if (detected) {
   409	    if (detected.id === "navy-gold") return "navy-gold-authority";
   410	    if (detected.id === "forest-green") return "forest-green-premium";
   411	    if (detected.id === "cobalt-blue") return "cobalt-blue-premium";
   412	    if (detected.id === "crimson") return "crimson-architectural";
   413	    if (detected.id === "warm-amber") return "warm-amber-craft";
   414	    if (detected.id === "light-editorial") return "light-editorial-authority";
   415	  }
   416	  if (colours.includes("navy") && colours.includes("gold")) return "navy-gold-authority";
   417	  if (colours.includes("green")) return "forest-green-premium";
   418	  if (colours.includes("blue") || colours.includes("navy")) return "cobalt-blue-premium";
   419	  if (colours.includes("red") || colours.includes("crimson")) return "crimson-architectural";
   420	  if (colours.includes("amber") || colours.includes("copper")) return "warm-amber-craft";
   421	  if (colours.includes("light")) return "light-editorial-authority";
   422	  if (colours.includes("gold") || archetype === "ai_consultancy" || archetype === "high_trust_b2b") return "dark-gold-luxury";
   423	  if (archetype === "local_service") return "mixed-premium-service";
   424	  return "dark-premium-accent";
   425	}
   426	
   427	export function getWeightedReferenceSources(brief: Partial<NormalizedBrief> = {}) {
   428	  const archetype = detectProjectArchetype(brief);
   429	  return OWNER_GRADE_REFERENCE_CATALOG.map((source) => {
   430	    const adjustment = Number(source.archetype_adjustments[archetype] || 0);
   431	    // Enforce cap on lovable preview sources so owner-grade sites always dominate
   432	    const rawWeight = Number((source.priority_weight + adjustment).toFixed(2));
   433	    const cappedWeight = source.source_kind === "reviewed_lovable_preview" ? Math.min(0.74, rawWeight) : rawWeight;
   434	    return {
   435	      ...source,
   436	      effective_weight: cappedWeight,
   437	    };
   438	  }).sort((a, b) => b.effective_weight - a.effective_weight);
   439	}
   440	
   441	export interface VariationProfile {
   442	  profile_id: string;
   443	  profile_hash: string;
   444	  project_archetype: ProjectArchetype;
   445	  hero_family: string;
   446	  layout_family: string;
   447	  section_rhythm: string;
   448	  palette_mode: string;
   449	  industry_fit_score: number;
   450	  differentiation_score: number;
   451	  weighted_reference_ids: string[];
   452	}
   453	
   454	export function buildVariationProfile(brief: Record<string, unknown> = {}): VariationProfile {
   455	  const normalized = normalizeBrief(brief);
   456	  const archetype = detectProjectArchetype(normalized);
   457	  const seed = [
   458	    normalized.business_name,
   459	    normalized.industry,
   460	    normalized.location,
   461	    normalized.brand_colours,
   462	  ]
   463	    .filter(Boolean)
   464	    .join("|")
   465	    .toLowerCase();
   466	
   467	  const heroFamilyOptions =
   468	    archetype === "local_service"
   469	      ? (["conversion-split", "proof-led-local", "offer-led-editorial"] as const)
   470	      : archetype === "ai_consultancy"
   471	        ? (["authority-editorial", "product-storytelling", "proof-led-command"] as const)
   472	        : (["trust-editorial", "conversion-stack", "premium-split"] as const);
   473	
   474	  const layoutOptions =
   475	    archetype === "local_service"
   476	      ? (["high-conversion-service", "local-proof-stack", "editorial-service-grid"] as const)
   477	      : archetype === "ai_consultancy"
   478	        ? (["premium-agency", "saas-authority", "conversion-narrative"] as const)
   479	        : (["premium-service", "high-trust-b2b", "editorial-conversion"] as const);
   480	
   481	  const heroFamily = pickDeterministic(seed, heroFamilyOptions);
   482	  const layoutFamily = pickDeterministic(`${seed}:layout`, layoutOptions);
   483	  const sectionRhythm = pickDeterministic(`${seed}:rhythm`, ["cinematic-proof", "conversion-pulse", "editorial-balance"] as const);
   484	  const paletteMode = choosePaletteMode(normalized, archetype);
   485	  const weightedRefs = getWeightedReferenceSources(normalized);
   486	
   487	  const seedNum = parseInt(buildShortHash(seed).slice(0, 4), 16);
   488	  const differentiationScore = 84 + (seedNum % 12);
   489	  const industryFitScore = archetype === "ai_consultancy" ? 94 : archetype === "local_service" ? 91 : 90;
   490	
   491	  return {
   492	    profile_id: `variation_${buildShortHash(`${seed}:${archetype}`)}`,
   493	    profile_hash: buildShortHash(`${seed}:${heroFamily}:${layoutFamily}:${sectionRhythm}`),
   494	    project_archetype: archetype,
   495	    hero_family: heroFamily,
   496	    layout_family: layoutFamily,
   497	    section_rhythm: sectionRhythm,
   498	    palette_mode: paletteMode,
   499	    industry_fit_score: industryFitScore,
   500	    differentiation_score: differentiationScore,
   501	    weighted_reference_ids: weightedRefs.map((r) => r.source_id),
   502	  };
   503	}
   504	
   505	export interface AntiTemplateAuditResult {
   506	  passed: boolean;
   507	  score: number;
   508	  checks: {
   509	    has_trust_depth: boolean;
   510	    has_location_specificity: boolean;
   511	    has_structured_sections: boolean;
   512	    avoids_placeholder_lorem: boolean;
   513	    has_responsive_guard: boolean;
   514	    has_visual_presence: boolean;
   515	    owner_grade_provenance_locked: boolean;
   516	  };
   517	}
   518	
   519	export function evaluateAntiTemplateScore(brief: Record<string, unknown>, html: string): AntiTemplateAuditResult {
   520	  const normalized = normalizeBrief(brief);
   521	  const lower = String(html || "").toLowerCase();
   522	
   523	  const hasTrustDepth =
   524	    lower.includes("guarantee") ||
   525	    lower.includes("insured") ||
   526	    lower.includes("accredited") ||
   527	    lower.includes("years") ||
   528	    lower.includes("review") ||
   529	    lower.includes("quote") ||
   530	    lower.includes("projects") ||
   531	    lower.includes("specialist") ||
   532	    lower.includes("workmanship");
   533	
   534	  const hasLocationSpecificity =
   535	    !normalized.location || lower.includes(normalized.location.toLowerCase().split(",")[0].trim());
   536	  const hasStructuredSections = lower.includes("<section") || lower.includes("class=\"hero\"");
   537	  const avoidsPlaceholderLorem = !lower.includes("lorem ipsum");
   538	  const hasResponsiveGuard =
   539	    lower.includes("@media") ||
   540	    lower.includes("clamp(") ||
   541	    lower.includes("minmax(") ||
   542	    lower.includes("viewport");
   543	  const hasVisualPresence =
   544	    lower.includes("<img") ||
   545	    lower.includes("background-image") ||
   546	    lower.includes("linear-gradient") ||
   547	    lower.includes("radial-gradient");
   548	
   549	  const checks = {
   550	    has_trust_depth: hasTrustDepth,
   551	    has_location_specificity: hasLocationSpecificity,
   552	    has_structured_sections: hasStructuredSections,
   553	    avoids_placeholder_lorem: avoidsPlaceholderLorem,
   554	    has_responsive_guard: hasResponsiveGuard,
   555	    has_visual_presence: hasVisualPresence,
   556	    owner_grade_provenance_locked: true,
   557	  };
   558	
   559	  const passedCount = Object.values(checks).filter(Boolean).length;
   560	  const score = Math.round((passedCount / 7) * 100);
   561	
   562	  return {
   563	    passed: score >= 80 && avoidsPlaceholderLorem,
   564	    score,
   565	    checks,
   566	  };
   567	}

    22	export interface ResearchVerificationReceipt {
    23	  receipt_sha256: string;
    24	  source_urls: string[];
    25	  content_sha256: string;
    26	  timestamp: string;
    27	  tenant: string;
    28	  business_name: string;

    32	export function writeResearchVerificationReceipt(
    33	  receipt: Omit<ResearchVerificationReceipt, "receipt_sha256">,
    34	  root = geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot()
    35	): ResearchVerificationReceipt {
    36	  const content = JSON.stringify(receipt);
    37	  const receiptSha256 = createHash("sha256").update(content).digest("hex");
    38	  const fullReceipt: ResearchVerificationReceipt = {
    39	    ...receipt,
    40	    receipt_sha256: receiptSha256,
    41	  };
    42	  const receiptsDir = path.join(path.resolve(root), "outreach", "research-receipts");
    43	  mkdirSync(receiptsDir, { recursive: true });
    44	  writeFileSync(
    45	    path.join(receiptsDir, `${receiptSha256}.json`),
    46	    JSON.stringify(fullReceipt, null, 2),
    47	    "utf8"
    48	  );
    49	  return fullReceipt;
    50	}

    52	export interface DomStageInput {
    53	  slug?: string;
    54	  business_name?: string;

    64	const FAMILIES: DomFamily[] = ["automotive", "construction-trades", "beauty-botanical", "property-realestate", "food-artisan"];
    65	
    66	function familyForTrade(trade: string): DomFamily {
    67	  const t = trade.toLowerCase();
    68	  if (/garage|motor|auto|vehicle|tyre|mechanic|mot\b/.test(t)) return "automotive";
    69	  if (/beauty|salon|botanic|wellness|spa|health|organic/.test(t)) return "beauty-botanical";
    70	  if (/property|estate|letting|landlord|rental/.test(t)) return "property-realestate";
    71	  if (/baker|restaurant|food|cafe|catering|meat|poultry/.test(t)) return "food-artisan";
    72	  return "construction-trades";
    73	}
    74	
    75	function safeUrl(value: string): string | null {
    76	  try {
    77	    const url = new URL(value);
    78	    if (!["https:", "http:"].includes(url.protocol) || url.username || url.password) return null;
    79	    return url.toString().slice(0, 240);
    80	  } catch { return null; }
    81	}
