    83	/** Authenticated existing office tool entry. Staging is NOT a QA pass and never
    84	 * generates images, creates prospect builds, emails prospects or fabricates NEO
    85	 * receipts. Subsequent independent DOM QA must pass the six-width gate. */
    86	export function stageDigitalAmbushDom(input: DomStageInput, root = geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot()) {
    87	  const business = String(input.business_name ?? "").trim().slice(0, 160);
    88	  const trade = String(input.business_type ?? "").trim().slice(0, 160);
    89	  const location = String(input.location ?? "").trim().slice(0, 160);
    90	  const slug = String(input.slug ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
    91	  if (!business || !trade || !location || !/^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{2,119}$/.test(slug)) {
    92	    throw new Error("AMBUSH_DOM_STAGE_REQUIRES_BUSINESS_SLUG_TRADE_LOCATION");
    93	  }
    94	  const family = FAMILIES.includes(input.template_family as DomFamily)
    95	    ? input.template_family as DomFamily : familyForTrade(trade);
    96	  const explicitSources = (Array.isArray(input.sources) ? input.sources : [])
    97	    .filter((v): v is string => typeof v === "string")
    98	    .map(safeUrl).filter((v): v is string => Boolean(v)).slice(0, 10);
    99	  const website = input.website ? safeUrl(input.website) : null;
   100	  // Independent research verification must come from a durable on-disk receipt, never trusted caller booleans
   101	  let verifiedReceipt: ResearchVerificationReceipt | null = null;
   102	  const receiptHash = typeof input.evidence_hash === "string" ? input.evidence_hash.trim().toLowerCase() : null;
   103	  if (receiptHash && /^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(receiptHash)) {
   104	    const receiptPath = path.join(path.resolve(root), "outreach", "research-receipts", `${receiptHash}.json`);
   105	    if (existsSync(receiptPath)) {
   106	      try {
   107	        const parsed = JSON.parse(readFileSync(receiptPath, "utf8"));
   108	        if (
   109	          parsed &&
   110	          typeof parsed === "object" &&
   111	          parsed.receipt_sha256 === receiptHash &&
   112	          Array.isArray(parsed.source_urls) &&
   113	          parsed.source_urls.length > 0 &&
   114	          parsed.content_sha256
   115	        ) {
   116	          verifiedReceipt = parsed as ResearchVerificationReceipt;
   117	        }
   118	      } catch {
   119	        verifiedReceipt = null;
   120	      }
   121	    }
   122	  }
   123	
   124	  const researchProvenance: "VERIFIED_PUBLIC_SOURCES" | "SOURCE_URLS_PROVIDED_UNVERIFIED" | "UNVERIFIED_INTERNAL_REFERENCE" | "NONE" =
   125	    explicitSources.length > 0
   126	      ? (verifiedReceipt ? "VERIFIED_PUBLIC_SOURCES" : "SOURCE_URLS_PROVIDED_UNVERIFIED")
   127	      : (website ? "UNVERIFIED_INTERNAL_REFERENCE" : "NONE");
   128	  const sources = explicitSources.length > 0
   129	    ? explicitSources
   130	    : (website ? [website] : []);
   131	  const findings = (Array.isArray(input.public_findings) ? input.public_findings : [])
   132	    .filter((v): v is string => typeof v === "string")
   133	    .map(v => v.slice(0, 600)).slice(0, 10);
   134	  const researchComplete = sources.length >= 1 && findings.length >= 1;
   135	  const cohort = !researchComplete
   136	    ? "HOLD_RESEARCH_INCOMPLETE"
   137	    : input.cohort === "COHORT_A_NO_WEBSITE" && explicitSources.length >= 2
   138	      ? "COHORT_A_NO_WEBSITE"
   139	      : input.cohort === "COHORT_B_LEGACY_REDESIGN" && website
   140	        ? "COHORT_B_LEGACY_REDESIGN"
   141	        : "HOLD_RESEARCH_INCOMPLETE";
   142	  const store = new SmartSiteStore(root);
   143	  const existing = store.getProspect(SITE_ID, slug);
   144	  const isCompatible = existing ? isSameBusinessIdentity(existing.business_name, business) : false;
   145	  const targetBusinessName = isCompatible ? existing!.business_name : business;
   146	  const record = store.upsertProspect({
   147	    slug, business_name: targetBusinessName, business_type: trade, location,
   148	    official_website: website, public_findings: findings, sources, cohort,
   149	    summary: researchComplete
   150	      ? (researchProvenance === "VERIFIED_PUBLIC_SOURCES"
   151	          ? "Verified public-source research staged for responsive DOM QA."
   152	          : (researchProvenance === "SOURCE_URLS_PROVIDED_UNVERIFIED"
   153	              ? "Supplied source URLs unverified; independent verified readback required before external outreach."
   154	              : "Internal reference pointer staged; real public-source verification required before external outreach."))
   155	      : "Prospect staged; real public-source verification required before DOM QA.",
   156	  }, SITE_ID, { allowVerifiedIdentityCorrection: isCompatible });
   157	  const validDom = record.preview_dom_qa &&
   158	    Date.parse(record.preview_dom_qa.expires_at) > Date.now() &&
   159	    record.preview_qa_status === "INTERNAL_QA_PASSED_AWAITING_FOUNDER";
   160	  return {
   161	    ok: true,
   162	    stage: "DOM_PROSPECT_STAGED",
   163	    concept_mode: "RESPONSIVE_DOM",
   164	    status: validDom ? "DOM_QA_PREVIOUSLY_CERTIFIED" : "AWAITING_INDEPENDENT_DOM_QA",
   165	    prospect_id: record.prospect_id,
   166	    slug: record.slug,
   167	    secure_id: record.secure_id,
   168	    template_family: family,
   169	    cohort: record.cohort,
   170	    research_ready: researchComplete,
   171	    research_provenance: researchProvenance,
   172	    browser_qa_required_widths: [320, 390, 430, 768, 1024, 1440],
   173	    preview_qa_verified: Boolean(validDom),
   174	    preview_url: validDom ? `/outreach-preview/${record.secure_id}` : null,
   175	    founder_outreach_authorised: false,
   176	    external_send: false,
   177	    generation: "NONE",
   178	    no_neo_image_receipt_required: true,
   179	    next_action: researchComplete
   180	      ? (researchProvenance === "VERIFIED_PUBLIC_SOURCES"
   181	          ? "NEO performs real responsive DOM browser QA; attach independently verified source commit and six-width receipt through the existing internal QA gate."
   182	          : "Prospect uses unverified sources or internal reference; real public-source verification with independent readback required before external outreach.")
   183	      : "GeminX must verify public sources and qualifying evidence; NEO then performs real six-width DOM QA.",
   184	  };
   185	}

   187	export interface GeminiVisionAuditResult {
   188	  passed: boolean;
   189	  model: string;
   190	  verdict: string;
   191	  scores: {
   192	    visual_hierarchy: number;
   193	    responsive_readability: number;
   194	    brand_consistency: number;
   195	    disclosure_clarity: number;
   196	  };
   197	  summary: string;
   198	  audit_timestamp: string;
   199	  provider_status?: number;
   200	  provider_error?: string;
   201	  finish_reason?: string;
   202	  block_reason?: string;
   203	  safety_ratings?: Array<{ category?: string; probability?: string }>;
   204	  raw_response?: string;

   207	
   208	function sanitizeDiagnostics(msg: string): string {
   209	  if (!msg) return "";
   210	  return msg
   211	    .replace(/(?:key|token|apiKey|bearer)=?[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{8,}/gi, "[REDACTED]")
   212	    .slice(0, 200);
   213	}
   214	
   215	export async function runGeminiVisionDomAudit(params: {
   216	  business_name: string;
   217	  location: string;
   218	  trade: string;
   219	  cohort: string;
   220	  rendered_html_snippet?: string;
   221	  screenshot_base64?: string;
   222	  desktop_screenshot_base64?: string;
   223	}): Promise<GeminiVisionAuditResult> {
   224	  const apiKey = String(
   225	    process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY ??
   226	    process.env.GOOGLE_API_KEY ??
   227	    process.env.GEMINX_GEMINI_LIVE_API_KEY ??
   228	    process.env.GEMINX_ANTIGRAVITY_API_KEY ??
   229	    process.env.GEMINX_GEMINI_FREE_API_KEY ??
   230	    ""
   231	  ).trim();
   232	
   233	  // Bound attempts: prioritize models with unexhausted daily quota on Google AI API
   234	  const candidateModels = Array.from(
   235	    new Set(
   236	      [
   237	        process.env.GEMINI_VISION_MODEL,
   238	        "gemini-3.8-flash",
   239	        "gemini-3.5-flash-lite",
   240	      ].filter(Boolean)
   241	    )
   242	  ).slice(0, 4) as string[];
   243	
   244	  const now = new Date().toISOString();
   245	  let lastProviderStatus: number | undefined;
   246	  let lastProviderError: string | undefined;
   247	  let lastFinishReason: string | undefined;
   248	  let lastBlockReason: string | undefined;
   249	  let lastSafetyRatings: Array<{ category?: string; probability?: string }> | undefined;
   250	  let lastVerdict = "GEMINI_VISION_AUDIT_UNUSABLE_RESPONSE";
   251	  let lastRawResponse: string | undefined;
   252	  const providerAttempts: VisionAttemptReceipt[] = [];
   253	  let retryBudget = MAX_VISION_ATTEMPTS;
   254	  const auditDeadlineAt = Date.now() + 30_000;
   255	
   256	  if (apiKey) {
   257	    const prompt = `You are the lead visual QA inspector for an AI web design agency. Evaluate this responsive HTML concept preview prepared for "${params.business_name}" (${params.trade} in ${params.location}, cohort: ${params.cohort}).
   258	Verify BOTH labelled screenshots, 390px mobile and 1440px desktop, when supplied:
   259	1. Headline, supporting copy and primary CTA have readable contrast over hero images/gradients.
   260	2. Faces, hands, product details, vehicle livery and other hero focal points are not obscured by overlaid text or accidentally cropped.
   261	3. Content, buttons, cards and navigation are not clipped or overlapped on either viewport.
   262	4. Judge genuine bespoke agency quality, not just valid CSS: deliberate typography contrast, generous editorial breathing room, cinematic industry-relevant photography, sophisticated image-to-text balance, restrained surface depth, and coherent section rhythm. FAIL generic three-card boilerplate or dated cramped layouts even when technically responsive.
   263	5. Inspect photographic focal crops, glass/masked-card treatments where appropriate to the actual business, mobile menu, and primary CTA hierarchy. Do NOT demand charcoal, teal or champagne across every brand; genuine brand identity outranks a stock theme.
   264	6. No invented reviews, accreditations, guarantees, urgency, customer counts, or misleading geographic claims. Unverified trust signals must cause failure, not a generous score.
   265	7. Independent concept disclosure must be truthful, readable and professionally discreet, never a large debug banner or raw developer disclaimer occupying the public-facing hero/footer.
   266	8. Do not award a passing premium visual score simply because images load or the HTML renders. Explain the most important visible defect in summary when failing.
   267	If EITHER screenshot contains a visible failure, set passed=false. Do not claim any uninspected viewport passed and do not infer speed or conversion rates from screenshots. If only one screenshot is present, disclose the missing visual inspection in summary.
   268	
   269	Respond in strict JSON with keys: passed (boolean), verdict (short string), scores (object with visual_hierarchy, responsive_readability, brand_consistency, disclosure_clarity from 1 to 10), and summary (string).`;
   270	
   271	    const contents: Array<{ parts: Array<{ text?: string; inlineData?: { mimeType: string; data: string } }> }> = [
   272	      {
   273	        parts: [
   274	          { text: prompt },
   275	          ...(params.screenshot_base64
   276	            ? [{ text: "Visual inspection input: 390px mobile screenshot" }, { inlineData: { mimeType: "image/png", data: params.screenshot_base64.replace(/^data:[^;]+;base64,/, "").trim() } }]
   277	            : []),
   278	          ...(params.desktop_screenshot_base64
   279	            ? [{ text: "Visual inspection input: 1440px desktop screenshot" }, { inlineData: { mimeType: "image/png", data: params.desktop_screenshot_base64.replace(/^data:[^;]+;base64,/, "").trim() } }]
   280	            : []),
   281	          ...(params.rendered_html_snippet
   282	            ? [{ text: `Rendered HTML snippet:\n${params.rendered_html_snippet.slice(0, 4000)}` }]
   283	            : []),
   284	        ],
   285	      },
   286	    ];
   287	
   288	    for (const model of candidateModels) {
   289	      try {
   290	        const url = `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/${model}:generateContent?key=${encodeURIComponent(apiKey)}`;
   291	        if (retryBudget <= 0 || Date.now() >= auditDeadlineAt) {
   292	          lastVerdict = "GEMINI_VISION_RETRY_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED";
   293	          break;
   294	        }
   295	        const remainingMs = Math.max(1, auditDeadlineAt - Date.now());
   296	        const retryResult = await fetchWithBoundedVisionRetry({
   297	          url,
   298	          model,
   299	          maxAttempts: retryBudget,
   300	          init: {
   301	            method: "POST",
   302	            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
   303	            body: JSON.stringify({
   304	              contents,
   305	              generationConfig: {
   306	                responseMimeType: "application/json",
   307	                temperature: 0.2,
   308	              },
   309	            }),
   310	            signal: AbortSignal.timeout(remainingMs),
   311	          },
   312	          sleepImpl: async (ms) => {
   313	            const remaining = auditDeadlineAt - Date.now();
   314	            if (remaining <= 0) return;
   315	            await new Promise<void>((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, Math.min(ms, remaining)));
   316	          },
   317	          onAttempt: (receipt) => providerAttempts.push(receipt),
   318	        });
   319	        retryBudget -= retryResult.attempts.length;
   320	        const res = retryResult.response;
   321	        if (!res) {
   322	          lastProviderError = sanitizeDiagnostics(retryResult.lastError ?? "Gemini Vision transport failed");
   323	          lastVerdict = retryResult.exhausted
   324	            ? "GEMINI_VISION_RETRY_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED"
   325	            : "GEMINI_VISION_AUDIT_NETWORK_ERROR";
   326	          if (retryBudget <= 0 || Date.now() >= auditDeadlineAt) break;
   327	          continue;
   328	        }
   329	
   330	        lastProviderStatus = res.status;
   331	
   332	        if (!res.ok) {
   333	          const rawErr = await res.text().catch(() => "");
   334	          lastRawResponse = sanitizeDiagnostics(rawErr);
   335	          let parsedErrMsg = "";
   336	          try {
   337	            const errObj = JSON.parse(rawErr);
   338	            parsedErrMsg = errObj.error?.message || errObj.message || "";
   339	          } catch {
   340	            parsedErrMsg = rawErr.slice(0, 160);
   341	          }
   342	          lastProviderError = sanitizeDiagnostics(`HTTP ${res.status} (${res.statusText}): ${parsedErrMsg || "unknown error"}`);
   343	          console.error(`[gemini-vision-audit] Model ${model} failed with ${lastProviderError}`);
   344	
   345	          if (res.status === 404) {
   346	            lastVerdict = "GEMINI_MODEL_NOT_FOUND";
   347	            continue;
   348	          } else if (res.status === 429) {
   349	            lastVerdict = "GEMINI_RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED";
   350	            continue;
   351	          } else if (res.status === 400) {
   352	            lastVerdict = "GEMINI_BAD_REQUEST";
   353	            break;
   354	          } else if (res.status === 401 || res.status === 403) {
   355	            lastVerdict = "GEMINI_API_KEY_UNAUTHORIZED";
   356	            break;
   357	          } else {
   358	            lastVerdict = `GEMINI_HTTP_${res.status}`;
   359	            break;
   360	          }
   361	        }
   362	
   363	        const json = (await res.json().catch(() => null)) as any;
   364	        if (!json) {
   365	          lastVerdict = "GEMINI_EMPTY_RESPONSE_BODY";
   366	          lastProviderError = "Failed to parse response body as JSON";
   367	          continue;
   368	        }
   369	
   370	        if (json.promptFeedback?.blockReason) {
   371	          lastBlockReason = String(json.promptFeedback.blockReason);
   372	          lastVerdict = "GEMINI_SAFETY_PROMPT_BLOCKED";
   373	          lastProviderError = sanitizeDiagnostics(`Prompt blocked: ${lastBlockReason}`);
   374	          console.warn(`[gemini-vision-audit] Model ${model} prompt blocked: ${lastBlockReason}`);
   375	          break;
   376	        }
   377	
   378	        const candidates = Array.isArray(json.candidates) ? json.candidates : [];
   379	        if (candidates.length === 0) {
   380	          lastVerdict = "GEMINI_NO_CANDIDATES";
   381	          lastProviderError = "Response returned 0 candidates";
   382	          console.warn(`[gemini-vision-audit] Model ${model} returned zero candidates`);
   383	          continue;
   384	        }
   385	
   386	        const firstCandidate = candidates[0];
   387	        if (firstCandidate.finishReason) {
   388	          lastFinishReason = String(firstCandidate.finishReason);
   389	        }
   390	        if (Array.isArray(firstCandidate.safetyRatings)) {
   391	          lastSafetyRatings = firstCandidate.safetyRatings;
   392	        }
   393	
   394	        if (firstCandidate.finishReason === "SAFETY") {
   395	          lastVerdict = "GEMINI_SAFETY_CANDIDATE_BLOCKED";
   396	          lastProviderError = sanitizeDiagnostics(`Candidate blocked due to safety flags: finishReason=${lastFinishReason}`);
   397	          console.warn(`[gemini-vision-audit] Model ${model} candidate safety blocked`);
   398	          break;
   399	        }
   400	
   401	        // Extract candidate text from all parts across candidates
   402	        let text = "";
   403	        for (const cand of candidates) {
   404	          const parts = Array.isArray(cand.content?.parts) ? cand.content.parts : [];
   405	          const combined = parts
   406	            .map((p: any) => (typeof p?.text === "string" ? p.text : ""))
   407	            .join("");
   408	          if (combined.trim()) {
   409	            text = combined;
   410	            break;
   411	          }
   412	        }
   413	
   414	        if (!text.trim()) {
   415	          lastVerdict = "GEMINI_EMPTY_CANDIDATE_TEXT";
   416	          lastProviderError = `Candidates present but contained no text (finishReason=${lastFinishReason ?? "none"})`;
   417	          console.warn(`[gemini-vision-audit] Model ${model} empty text: ${lastProviderError}`);
   418	          continue;
   419	        }
   420	
   421	        lastRawResponse = sanitizeDiagnostics(text);
   422	        const cleanText = text.replace(/^```(?:json)?\s*|\s*```$/gi, "").trim();
   423	
   424	        let parsed: any;
   425	        try {
   426	          parsed = JSON.parse(cleanText);
   427	        } catch (parseErr) {
   428	          lastVerdict = "GEMINI_JSON_PARSE_ERROR";
   429	          lastProviderError = sanitizeDiagnostics(`Model response was not valid JSON: ${parseErr instanceof Error ? parseErr.message : String(parseErr)}`);
   430	          console.warn(`[gemini-vision-audit] Model ${model} JSON parse failed: ${lastProviderError}`);
   431	          break;
   432	        }
   433	
   434	        // Strict schema validation:
   435	        if (typeof parsed !== "object" || parsed === null || Array.isArray(parsed)) {
   436	          return {
   437	            passed: false,
   438	            model,
   439	            verdict: "GEMINI_QA_SCHEMA_INVALID",
   440	            scores: { visual_hierarchy: 0, responsive_readability: 0, brand_consistency: 0, disclosure_clarity: 0 },
   441	            summary: "Gemini QA response must be a JSON object",
   442	            audit_timestamp: now,
   443	            provider_status: res.status,
   444	            raw_response: sanitizeDiagnostics(cleanText),
   445	            provider_attempts: providerAttempts,
   446	          };
   447	        }
   448	
   449	        if (typeof parsed.passed !== "boolean") {
   450	          return {
   451	            passed: false,
   452	            model,
   453	            verdict: "GEMINI_QA_SCHEMA_INVALID",
   454	            scores: { visual_hierarchy: 0, responsive_readability: 0, brand_consistency: 0, disclosure_clarity: 0 },
   455	            summary: "Gemini QA response missing required boolean field 'passed'",
   456	            audit_timestamp: now,
   457	            provider_status: res.status,
   458	            raw_response: sanitizeDiagnostics(cleanText),
   459	            provider_attempts: providerAttempts,
   460	          };
   461	        }
   462	
   463	        if (typeof parsed.scores !== "object" || parsed.scores === null || Array.isArray(parsed.scores)) {
   464	          return {
   465	            passed: false,
   466	            model,
   467	            verdict: "GEMINI_QA_SCHEMA_INVALID",
   468	            scores: { visual_hierarchy: 0, responsive_readability: 0, brand_consistency: 0, disclosure_clarity: 0 },
   469	            summary: "Gemini QA response missing required object field 'scores'",
   470	            audit_timestamp: now,
   471	            provider_status: res.status,
   472	            raw_response: sanitizeDiagnostics(cleanText),
   473	            provider_attempts: providerAttempts,
   474	          };
   475	        }
   476	
   477	        const requiredScoreKeys = [
   478	          "visual_hierarchy",
   479	          "responsive_readability",
   480	          "brand_consistency",
   481	          "disclosure_clarity",
   482	        ] as const;
   483	
   484	        let schemaInvalidKey: string | null = null;
   485	        for (const key of requiredScoreKeys) {
   486	          const val = parsed.scores[key];
   487	          if (typeof val !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(val) || val < 1 || val > 10) {
   488	            schemaInvalidKey = key;
   489	            break;
   490	          }
   491	        }
   492	
   493	        if (schemaInvalidKey) {
   494	          return {
   495	            passed: false,
   496	            model,
   497	            verdict: "GEMINI_QA_SCHEMA_INVALID",
   498	            scores: { visual_hierarchy: 0, responsive_readability: 0, brand_consistency: 0, disclosure_clarity: 0 },
   499	            summary: `Gemini QA response score '${schemaInvalidKey}' is missing, non-numeric, or outside 1..10 range`,
   500	            audit_timestamp: now,
   501	            provider_status: res.status,
   502	            raw_response: sanitizeDiagnostics(cleanText),
   503	            provider_attempts: providerAttempts,
   504	          };
   505	        }
   506	
   507	        const scores = {
   508	          visual_hierarchy: Math.round(parsed.scores.visual_hierarchy * 10) / 10,
   509	          responsive_readability: Math.round(parsed.scores.responsive_readability * 10) / 10,
   510	          brand_consistency: Math.round(parsed.scores.brand_consistency * 10) / 10,
   511	          disclosure_clarity: Math.round(parsed.scores.disclosure_clarity * 10) / 10,
   512	        };
   513	
   514	        const MIN_PASSING_SCORE = 8.0;
   515	        const belowThreshold = Object.entries(scores).filter(([_, s]) => s < MIN_PASSING_SCORE);
   516	
   517	        if (belowThreshold.length > 0) {
   518	          return {
   519	            passed: false,
   520	            model,
   521	            verdict: "GEMINI_QA_SCORE_BELOW_THRESHOLD",
   522	            scores,
   523	            summary: `Visual QA failed quality threshold: ${belowThreshold.map(([k, s]) => `${k}=${s}`).join(", ")} (minimum ${MIN_PASSING_SCORE} required)`,
   524	            audit_timestamp: now,
   525	            provider_status: res.status,
   526	            finish_reason: lastFinishReason,
   527	            raw_response: sanitizeDiagnostics(cleanText),
   528	            provider_attempts: providerAttempts,
   529	          };
   530	        }
   531	
   532	        if (parsed.passed !== true) {
   533	          return {
   534	            passed: false,
   535	            model,
   536	            verdict: String(parsed.verdict ?? "AUDIT_FAILED"),
   537	            scores,
   538	            summary: String(parsed.summary ?? `Visual QA flagged layout issues on ${model}`),
   539	            audit_timestamp: now,
   540	            provider_status: res.status,
   541	            finish_reason: lastFinishReason,
   542	            raw_response: sanitizeDiagnostics(cleanText),
   543	            provider_attempts: providerAttempts,
   544	          };
   545	        }
   546	
   547	        return {
   548	          passed: true,
   549	          model,
   550	          verdict: String(parsed.verdict ?? "AUDIT_COMPLETE"),
   551	          scores,
   552	          summary: String(parsed.summary ?? `Visual QA passed by ${model}`),
   553	          audit_timestamp: now,
   554	          provider_status: res.status,
   555	          finish_reason: lastFinishReason,
   556	          safety_ratings: lastSafetyRatings,
   557	          raw_response: sanitizeDiagnostics(cleanText),
   558	        };
   559	      } catch (err) {
   560	        lastProviderError = sanitizeDiagnostics(err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
   561	        lastVerdict = "GEMINI_VISION_AUDIT_NETWORK_ERROR";
   562	        console.error(`[gemini-vision-audit] Model ${model} exception: ${lastProviderError}`);
   563	      }
   564	    }
   565	  }
   566	
   567	  // Strictly fail closed when Gemini API key is missing or model response was empty/unusable.
   568	  // Never fabricate or invent a successful visual inspection.
   569	  const isKeyMissing = !apiKey;
   570	  const failureVerdict = isKeyMissing ? "GEMINI_VISION_API_KEY_MISSING" : lastVerdict;
   571	  const failureSummary = isKeyMissing
   572	    ? "Gemini visual inspection requires GEMINI_API_KEY or GOOGLE_API_KEY in the environment."
   573	    : `Gemini visual inspection failed across models [${candidateModels.join(", ")}]: ${lastProviderError || lastVerdict}`;
   574	
   575	  return {
   576	    passed: false,
   577	    model: apiKey ? candidateModels[0] : "none",
   578	    verdict: failureVerdict,
   579	    scores: {
   580	      visual_hierarchy: 0,
   581	      responsive_readability: 0,
   582	      brand_consistency: 0,
   583	      disclosure_clarity: 0,
   584	    },
   585	    summary: failureSummary,
   586	    audit_timestamp: now,
   587	    ...(lastProviderStatus ? { provider_status: lastProviderStatus } : {}),
   588	    ...(lastProviderError ? { provider_error: lastProviderError } : {}),
   589	    ...(lastFinishReason ? { finish_reason: lastFinishReason } : {}),
   590	    ...(lastBlockReason ? { block_reason: lastBlockReason } : {}),
   591	    ...(lastSafetyRatings ? { safety_ratings: lastSafetyRatings } : {}),
   592	    ...(lastRawResponse ? { raw_response: lastRawResponse } : {}),
   593	    ...(providerAttempts.length ? { provider_attempts: providerAttempts } : {}),
   594	  };
   595	}
   596	
   597	export interface ResponsiveCaptureResult {
   598	  width: number;
   599	  height: number;
   600	  sha256: string;
   601	  byte_length: number;
   602	  base64: string;
   603	  has_horizontal_overflow?: boolean;
   604	  /** Independent visual-media failures must never masquerade as overflow. */
   605	  visual_failures?: string[];
   606	}
   607	
   608	export async function captureDomResponsiveScreenshots(
   609	  html: string,
   610	  widths: number[] = [320, 390, 430, 768, 1024, 1440],
   611	  options: { imageLed?: boolean; fullPage?: boolean } = {}
   612	): Promise<ResponsiveCaptureResult[]> {
   613	  let puppeteerModule: any = null;
   614	  try {
   615	    const modName = "puppeteer";
   616	    // @ts-ignore - Dynamic import to allow optional runtime resolution without failing tsc in minimal build containers
   617	    const mod = await import(modName);
   618	    puppeteerModule = (mod as any).default ?? mod;
   619	  } catch (err) {
   620	    throw new Error("PUPPETEER_NOT_AVAILABLE: Headless Chromium is required for genuine DOM QA capture.");
   621	  }
   622	
   623	  const executablePath =
   624	    process.env.PUPPETEER_EXECUTABLE_PATH ||
   625	    (process.platform === "linux" && (existsSync("/usr/bin/chromium") ? "/usr/bin/chromium" : existsSync("/usr/bin/chromium-browser") ? "/usr/bin/chromium-browser" : undefined)) ||
   626	    undefined;
   627	
   628	  const browser = await puppeteerModule.launch({
   629	    headless: true,
   630	    ...(executablePath ? { executablePath } : {}),
   631	    args: ["--no-sandbox", "--disable-setuid-sandbox", "--disable-dev-shm-usage", "--disable-gpu"],
   632	  });
   633	
   634	  try {
   635	    const page = await browser.newPage();
   636	    await page.setContent(html, { waitUntil: "domcontentloaded", timeout: 10_000 });
   637	
   638	    // Wait for document fonts and images rendering readiness with bounded timeout
   639	    await page.evaluate(async () => {
   640	      if (document.fonts && document.fonts.ready) {
   641	        await document.fonts.ready;
   642	      }
   643	      const imgs = Array.from(document.images);
   644	      await Promise.race([
   645	        Promise.all(
   646	          imgs.map((img) =>
   647	            img.complete
   648	              ? Promise.resolve()
   649	              : new Promise((resolve) => {
   650	                  img.onload = resolve;
   651	                  img.onerror = resolve;
   652	                  setTimeout(resolve, 1000);
   653	                })
   654	          )
   655	        ),
   656	        new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 1500)),
   657	      ]);
   658	    }).catch(() => {});
   659	
   660	    const results: ResponsiveCaptureResult[] = [];
   661	
   662	    for (const width of widths) {
   663	      await page.setViewport({ width, height: 900 });
   664	
   665	      // Deterministic check for horizontal scrollbar overflow, broken images, and element bounding box violations
   666	      const hasOverflow = await page.evaluate(() => {
   667	        const docWidth = document.documentElement.scrollWidth;
   668	        const winWidth = window.innerWidth;
   669	        if (docWidth > winWidth + 1) return true;
   670	
   671	        // Media failures are reported separately by visualFailures below.
   672	
   673	        // Inspect visible descendant elements to catch content clipping and left/right violations
   674	        const elements = document.querySelectorAll("*");
   675	        for (let i = 0; i < elements.length; i++) {
   676	          const el = elements[i];
   677	          const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
   678	          if (rect.width > 0 && rect.height > 0) {
   679	            if (rect.right > winWidth + 1 || rect.left < -1) {
   680	              return true;
   681	            }
   682	          }
   683	        }
   684	        return false;
   685	      }).catch(() => true); // Fail closed on evaluation failure
   686	
   687	      // Image-led trade pages require decoded media and balanced card geometry.
   688	      // We classify these separately from overflow so an HTTP 200 or six PNGs
   689	      // cannot certify an invisible hero or a broken card photograph.
   690	      const visualFailures = await page.evaluate(async (imageLed: boolean) => {
   691	        const errors: string[] = [];
   692	        const seen = new Set<string>();
   693	        const fail = (code: string, detail: string) => {
   694	          const message = code + ": " + detail;
   695	          if (!seen.has(message)) { errors.push(message); seen.add(message); }
   696	        };
   697	        const pictureMedia = (el: Element): boolean => {
   698	          const style = getComputedStyle(el);
   699	          return style.backgroundImage !== "none" && /url\s*\(/i.test(style.backgroundImage);
   700	        };
   701	        const decodeURL = async (url: string): Promise<boolean> => {
   702	          if (!url || url.startsWith("blob:")) return false;
   703	          try {
   704	            const test = new Image();
   705	            test.src = url;
   706	            await Promise.race([
   707	              test.decode(),
   708	              new Promise<never>((_, reject) => setTimeout(() => reject(new Error("image decode timeout")), 2500))
   709	            ]);
   710	            return test.naturalWidth > 0;
   711	          } catch { return false; }
   712	        };
   713	        for (const img of Array.from(document.images)) {
   714	          // A hidden decorative template is not a visible image requirement.
   715	          if (!img.getClientRects().length) continue;
   716	          if (img.loading === "lazy") img.loading = "eager";
   717	          const url = img.currentSrc || img.src;
   718	          if (!url || !await decodeURL(url)) {
   719	            fail("VISUAL_IMAGE_MISSING", url || "img with no source");
   720	          }
   721	        }
   722	        // Computed backgrounds do not appear in document.images.
   723	        const bgSet = new Set<string>();
   724	        for (const el of Array.from(document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>("section, article, .hero, .hero-bg-layer, .service-card, [data-brian-visual-profile]"))) {
   725	          if (!el.getClientRects().length || !pictureMedia(el)) continue;
   726	          const css = getComputedStyle(el).backgroundImage;
   727	          for (const match of css.matchAll(/url\(\s*(?:"([^"]+)"|'([^']+)'|([^)]*))\s*\)/g)) {
   728	            const url = (match[1] || match[2] || match[3] || "").trim();
   729	            if (url) bgSet.add(url);
   730	          }
   731	        }
   732	        for (const url of bgSet) {
   733	          if (!await decodeURL(url)) fail("CSS_BACKGROUND_IMAGE_BROKEN", url);
   734	        }
   735	        if (imageLed) {
   736	          const hero = document.querySelector(".hero, .hero-section, .hero-split, [data-visual-role='hero']");
   737	          if (!hero) fail("HERO_IMAGE_NOT_VISIBLE", "image-led page has no hero");
   738	          else {
   739	            const nodes = [hero, ...Array.from(hero.querySelectorAll("*"))];
   740	            const hasPhoto = nodes.some((node) => {
   741	              if (pictureMedia(node)) return true;
   742	              if (node instanceof HTMLImageElement)
   743	                return !/mockup|logo|icon/i.test(node.className + " " + node.alt) && node.complete && node.naturalWidth > 0;
   744	              return false;
   745	            });
   746	            if (!hasPhoto) fail("HERO_IMAGE_NOT_VISIBLE", "no decoded hero photograph layer");
   747	          }
   748	          for (const card of Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(".services-grid > .service-card, .service-grid > .service-card, [data-visual-role='service-card']"))) {
   749	            const nodes = [card, ...Array.from(card.querySelectorAll("*"))];
   750	            const hasPhoto = nodes.some((node) => pictureMedia(node) ||
   751	              (node instanceof HTMLImageElement && node.complete && node.naturalWidth > 0));
   752	            if (!hasPhoto) fail("SERVICE_CARD_IMAGE_MISSING", (card.textContent || "").trim().slice(0, 70));
   753	          }
   754	        }
   755	        for (const grid of Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(".services-grid, .service-grid, [data-visual-role='service-grid']"))) {
   756	          const cards = Array.from(grid.children).filter((el) => el.getBoundingClientRect().width > 0);
   757	          if (cards.length < 3) continue;
   758	          const rows: { top: number; cards: Element[] }[] = [];
   759	          for (const card of cards) {
   760	            const top = card.getBoundingClientRect().top;
   761	            let row = rows.find((r) => Math.abs(r.top - top) < 6);
   762	            if (!row) { row = {top, cards: []}; rows.push(row); }
   763	            row.cards.push(card);
   764	          }
   765	          rows.sort((x, y) => x.top - y.top);
   766	          if (rows.length < 2) continue;
   767	          const last = rows[rows.length - 1];
   768	          if (last.cards.length !== 1 || !rows.slice(0, -1).some(r => r.cards.length === 2)) continue;
   769	          const parentWidth = grid.getBoundingClientRect().width;
   770	          const finalWidth = last.cards[0].getBoundingClientRect().width;
   771	          if (parentWidth > 0 && finalWidth < parentWidth * .82)
   772	            fail("RESPONSIVE_ORPHAN_CARD", "single narrow final card at viewport " + window.innerWidth);
   773	        }
   774	        // Founder-selectable photographic composition gate. Unlike image URL
   775	        // presence checks, these inspect the actual rendered card geometry,
   776	        // photograph opacity, directional gradient and section surfaces.
   777	        if (document.body.getAttribute("data-brian-design-profile") === "photo-first-trade-v4") {
   778	          const cards = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(".services-grid > .service-card"));
   779	          for (const [index, card] of cards.entries()) {
   780	            const photo = card.querySelector<HTMLImageElement>(".service-card-photo");
   781	            const zone = card.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".service-photo-window");
   782	            const content = card.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".service-card-content");
   783	            const label = "service " + (index + 1);
   784	            if (!photo || !zone || !content) {
   785	              fail("PHOTO_FIRST_CARD_COMPOSITION_MISSING", label);
   786	              continue;
   787	            }
   788	            const cardRect = card.getBoundingClientRect();
   789	            const zoneRect = zone.getBoundingClientRect();
   790	            const contentRect = content.getBoundingClientRect();
   791	            if (zoneRect.height < 140 || contentRect.top - cardRect.top < 140)
   792	              fail("PHOTO_FIRST_VISUAL_ZONE_TOO_SMALL", label + " visible-zone=" + Math.round(contentRect.top - cardRect.top));
   793	            const opacity = Number(getComputedStyle(photo).opacity);
   794	            if (!Number.isFinite(opacity) || opacity < 0.7)
   795	              fail("PHOTO_FIRST_PHOTO_CRUSHED", label + " opacity=" + opacity);
   796	            const scrim = getComputedStyle(card, "::before").backgroundImage;
   797	            const topStop = scrim.match(/rgba\(\s*\d+\s*,\s*\d+\s*,\s*\d+\s*,\s*([0-9.]+)\s*\)/i);
   798	            const topAlpha = topStop ? Number(topStop[1]) : NaN;
   799	            if (!Number.isFinite(topAlpha) || topAlpha > 0.20)
   800	              fail("PHOTO_FIRST_SCRIM_OPAQUE", label + " top-alpha=" + String(topAlpha));
   801	            const summary = card.querySelector("p")?.textContent?.trim() || "";
   802	            const wordCount = summary.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length;
   803	            if (!summary || wordCount > 22 || card.querySelector("ul, .service-card-checklist"))
   804	              fail("PHOTO_FIRST_TEXT_WALL", label + " summary words=" + wordCount);
   805	            if (!card.querySelector(".micro-cta"))
   806	              fail("PHOTO_FIRST_MICRO_CTA_MISSING", label);
   807	          }
   808	          const ids = ["services", "approach", "calculator", "areas", "faq"];
   809	          for (let i = 1; i < ids.length; i++) {
   810	            const previous = document.getElementById(ids[i - 1]);
   811	            const current = document.getElementById(ids[i]);
   812	            if (!previous || !current) continue;
   813	            const pa = getComputedStyle(previous), ca = getComputedStyle(current);
   814	            if (pa.backgroundImage === ca.backgroundImage && pa.backgroundColor === ca.backgroundColor)
   815	              fail("PHOTO_FIRST_SECTION_RHYTHM_FLAT", ids[i - 1] + " / " + ids[i]);
   816	          }
   817	          const salesText = [document.querySelector("main"), document.querySelector("footer")]
   818	            .map(el => el?.textContent || "").join(" ");
   819	          if (/\bfictional\b|fictional demonstration|fictional operational zone|is this an operational company or a design preview/i.test(salesText))
   820	            fail("PHOTO_FIRST_PREVIEW_COPY_LEAK", "preview status belongs in the transparent top banner");
   821	          if (!document.querySelector(".concept-topbar") || !document.querySelector(".preview-chip"))
   822	            fail("PHOTO_FIRST_PREVIEW_DISCLOSURE_MISSING", "top banner and persistent identifier required");
   823	        }
   824	        window.scrollTo(0, 0);
   825	        return errors;
   826	      }, Boolean(options.imageLed)).catch(() => ["VISUAL_GATE_EVALUATION_FAILED"]);
   827	
   828	      const rawScreenshot = await page.screenshot({ type: "png", fullPage: Boolean(options.fullPage) });
   829	      const buffer = Buffer.isBuffer(rawScreenshot) ? rawScreenshot : Buffer.from(rawScreenshot);
   830	      const sha256 = createHash("sha256").update(buffer).digest("hex");
   831	      results.push({
   832	        width,
   833	        height: buffer.readUInt32BE(20),
   834	        sha256,
   835	        byte_length: buffer.length,
   836	        base64: buffer.toString("base64"),
   837	        has_horizontal_overflow: Boolean(hasOverflow),
   838	        visual_failures: visualFailures,
   839	      });
   840	    }
   841	
   842	    return results;
   843	  } finally {
   844	    await browser.close().catch(() => {});
   845	  }
   846	}
   847	
   848	export interface AuthenticatedDomStageExecution {
   849	  authenticated: true;
   850	  actor_agent: OfficeAgentId;
   851	  /** The current prospect lease token, never a synthetic task-id token. */
   852	  lease_token?: string;
   853	}
   854	
   855	export function validateDomStageOwnership(
   856	  lock: OfficeProspectLockRecord | undefined,
   857	  context: AuthenticatedDomStageExecution | undefined,
   858	  now = Date.now()
   859	): { actor_agent: OfficeAgentId; lease_token: string; lease_expires_at: string; fenced_tokens: string[] } {
   860	  if (context?.authenticated !== true || !isOfficeAgentId(context.actor_agent)) {
   861	    throw new Error("AMBUSH_AUTHENTICATED_ACTOR_REQUIRED");
   862	  }
   863	  const actor=context.actor_agent;
   864	  if (lock) {
   865	    const expires=Date.parse(lock.lease_expires_at);
   866	    if (!Number.isFinite(expires) || expires <= now) {
   867	      throw new Error("AMBUSH_LEASE_EXPIRED_RECLAIM_REQUIRED");
   868	    }
   869	    if (lock.owner_agent !== actor) {
   870	      throw new Error(`AMBUSH_LEASE_ACTIVE_CONFLICT: Prospect ${lock.slug} is actively locked by ${lock.owner_agent}`);
   871	    }
   872	    const supplied=String(context.lease_token??"").trim();
   873	    if (!supplied || supplied !== lock.lease_token ||
   874	        (lock.fenced_tokens||[]).includes(supplied)) {
   875	      throw new Error("AMBUSH_STALE_LEASE_TOKEN_FENCED");
   876	    }
   877	    return {
   878	      actor_agent: actor,
   879	      lease_token: lock.lease_token,
   880	      lease_expires_at: lock.lease_expires_at,
   881	      fenced_tokens: [...lock.fenced_tokens]
   882	    };
   883	  }
   884	  return {
   885	    actor_agent: actor,
   886	    lease_token: "lease_"+randomUUID().slice(0,12),
   887	    lease_expires_at: new Date(now + 86400000).toISOString(),
   888	    fenced_tokens: []
   889	  };
   890	}
   891	
   892	export async function runDigitalAmbushEndToEnd(
   893	  input: DomStageInput & {
   894	    sender_id?: "zoho" | "gmail";
   895	    auto_certify_dom?: boolean;
   896	    dispatch_mode?: "stage_and_gate" | "internal_canary";
   897	    external_send?: boolean;
   898	    recipient?: string;
   899	  },
   900	  root = geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot(),
   901	  execution?: AuthenticatedDomStageExecution
   902	) {
   903	  // Authenticate actor and validate current prospect lease BEFORE mutating the
   904	  // SmartSiteStore. The old Kerry-special case silently rejected legitimate Brian
   905	  // ownership and could reuse an unrelated worker's fence token.
   906	  const targetSlug=String(input.slug??"").trim().toLowerCase();
   907	  const initialLedger=readOfficeLedger(root);
   908	  const initialLock=initialLedger.prospect_locks.find(l=>l.prospect_id===targetSlug);
   909	  const claim=validateDomStageOwnership(initialLock,execution);
   910	
   911	  // Step 1: Stage prospect in SmartSiteStore only after owner/fence verification
   912	  const staged = stageDigitalAmbushDom(input, root);
   913	
   914	  // External send safeguard: strictly block external outreach unless research provenance is VERIFIED_PUBLIC_SOURCES and cohort is qualified
   915	  if (input.external_send && (staged.cohort === "HOLD_RESEARCH_INCOMPLETE" || staged.research_provenance !== "VERIFIED_PUBLIC_SOURCES")) {
   916	    throw new Error(`AMBUSH_EXTERNAL_SEND_BLOCKED_UNVERIFIED_RESEARCH: External prospect outreach requires verified public sources with independent readback, but cohort is ${staged.cohort} and provenance is ${staged.research_provenance}.`);
   917	  }
   918	
   919	  const store = new SmartSiteStore(root);
   920	  const ledger = readOfficeLedger(root);
   921	  const existingLock = ledger.prospect_locks.find(l=>l.prospect_id===staged.slug);
   922	  const validatedNow=validateDomStageOwnership(existingLock,execution);
   923	  if (initialLock?.lease_token !== existingLock?.lease_token ||
   924	      initialLock?.owner_agent !== existingLock?.owner_agent ||
   925	      validatedNow.actor_agent !== claim.actor_agent) {
   926	    throw new Error("AMBUSH_LEASE_CHANGED_DURING_STAGE");
   927	  }
   928	
   929	  // P0-2 Real HTML Concept Rendering
   930	  const prospectRecord = store.getProspect(SITE_ID, staged.slug)!;
   931	  const renderedConceptHtml = renderProspectConceptHtml(
   932	    prospectRecord,
   933	    "https://geminx-backend-production.up.railway.app",
   934	    null
   935	  );
   936	
   937	  // Step 2: Genuine Chromium 6-width responsive capture
   938	  const requiredWidths = [320, 390, 430, 768, 1024, 1440];
   939	  let capturedScreenshots: ResponsiveCaptureResult[];
   940	  try {
   941	    capturedScreenshots = await captureDomResponsiveScreenshots(renderedConceptHtml, requiredWidths, {
   942	      imageLed: /\b(?:roof\w*|scaffold\w*|clean\w*|pav\w*|driveway\w*|landscap\w*|electric\w*|plumb\w*|build\w*|construction|stone\w*|patio\w*|floor\w*|garden\w*|tree\w*)\b/i.test(prospectRecord.business_type),
   943	    });
   944	  } catch (captureErr) {
   945	    return {
   946	      ok: false,
   947	      status: "BROWSER_QA_CAPTURE_FAILED",
   948	      prospect_id: staged.prospect_id,
   949	      slug: staged.slug,
   950	      secure_id: staged.secure_id,
   951	      preview_url: null,
   952	      dom_qa_verified: false,
   953	      vision_qa: null,
   954	      error: `Headless Chromium 6-width capture failed: ${captureErr instanceof Error ? captureErr.message : String(captureErr)}`,
   955	    };
   956	  }
   957	
   958	  // A real preview cannot pass because a screenshot exists while its imagery
   959	  // failed to load or its last service card is stranded at a breakpoint.
   960	  const mediaFailures = capturedScreenshots.flatMap((shot) =>
   961	    (shot.visual_failures ?? []).map((error) => shot.width + "px: " + error)
   962	  );
   963	  if (mediaFailures.length) {
   964	    return {
   965	      ok: false,
   966	      status: "BROWSER_VISUAL_MEDIA_OR_GRID_FAILED",
   967	      prospect_id: staged.prospect_id,
   968	      slug: staged.slug,
   969	      secure_id: staged.secure_id,
   970	      preview_url: null,
   971	      dom_qa_verified: false,
   972	      vision_qa: null,
   973	      error: mediaFailures.slice(0, 20).join("; "),
   974	    };
   975	  }
   976	
   977	  // Check horizontal overflow defects across all widths
   978	  const overflowWidths = capturedScreenshots.filter((s) => s.has_horizontal_overflow).map((s) => s.width);
   979	  if (overflowWidths.length > 0) {
   980	    return {
   981	      ok: false,
   982	      status: "DOM_HORIZONTAL_OVERFLOW_DETECTED",
   983	      prospect_id: staged.prospect_id,
   984	      slug: staged.slug,
   985	      secure_id: staged.secure_id,
   986	      preview_url: null,
   987	      dom_qa_verified: false,
   988	      responsive_screenshots: capturedScreenshots.map((s) => ({
   989	        width: s.width,
   990	        height: s.height,
   991	        byte_length: s.byte_length,
   992	        sha256: s.sha256,
   993	      })),
   994	      vision_qa: null,
   995	      error: `DOM layout has horizontal scroll overflow at widths: ${overflowWidths.join(", ")}px`,
   996	    };
   997	  }
   998	
   999	  // Validate that all 6 required widths were captured with real nonzero bytes
  1000	  const verifiedCapturedWidths = capturedScreenshots
  1001	    .filter((s) => s.byte_length > 0 && /^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(s.sha256))
  1002	    .map((s) => s.width);
  1003	
  1004	  if (!requiredWidths.every((w) => verifiedCapturedWidths.includes(w))) {
  1005	    return {
  1006	      ok: false,
  1007	      status: "BROWSER_QA_WIDTHS_INCOMPLETE",
  1008	      prospect_id: staged.prospect_id,
  1009	      slug: staged.slug,
  1010	      secure_id: staged.secure_id,
  1011	      preview_url: null,
  1012	      dom_qa_verified: false,
  1013	      vision_qa: null,
  1014	      error: `Missing required responsive widths. Expected [${requiredWidths.join(", ")}], got [${verifiedCapturedWidths.join(", ")}]`,
  1015	    };
  1016	  }
  1017	
  1018	  // Pick representative mobile viewport (390) and desktop viewport (1440) for Gemini Vision QA
  1019	  const mobileShot = capturedScreenshots.find((s) => s.width === 390) ?? capturedScreenshots[0];
  1020	  const desktopShot = capturedScreenshots.find((s) => s.width === 1440);
  1021	  if (!desktopShot) throw new Error("AMBUSH_DESKTOP_VISUAL_EVIDENCE_MISSING");
  1022	
  1023	  // Dynamic current source commit from Railway/Vercel environment or git HEAD
  1024	  let sourceCommit = (
  1025	    process.env.RAILWAY_GIT_COMMIT_SHA ||
  1026	    process.env.VERCEL_GIT_COMMIT_SHA ||
  1027	    process.env.COMMIT_SHA ||
  1028	    process.env.GIT_COMMIT_SHA ||
  1029	    process.env.SOURCE_VERSION ||
  1030	    ""
  1031	  ).trim();
  1032	  if (!/^[a-f0-9]{40}$/i.test(sourceCommit)) {
  1033	    try {
  1034	      const gitHead = execSync("git rev-parse HEAD", { encoding: "utf8" }).trim();
  1035	      if (/^[a-f0-9]{40}$/i.test(gitHead)) {
  1036	        sourceCommit = gitHead;
  1037	      }
  1038	    } catch {
  1039	      // Git rev-parse unavailable in Docker minimal runtime
  1040	    }
  1041	  }
  1042	  if (!/^[a-f0-9]{40}$/i.test(sourceCommit)) {
  1043	    throw new Error("AMBUSH_SOURCE_COMMIT_UNAVAILABLE: Cannot determine trusted deployment commit SHA");
  1044	  }
  1045	
  1046	  // Step 3: Perform genuine Gemini Vision QA audit with actual Chromium screenshot
  1047	  const visionQa = await runGeminiVisionDomAudit({
  1048	    business_name: String(input.business_name ?? staged.slug),
  1049	    location: String(input.location ?? "UK"),
  1050	    trade: String(input.business_type ?? "Commercial"),
  1051	    cohort: staged.cohort ?? "COHORT_A_NO_WEBSITE",
  1052	    rendered_html_snippet: renderedConceptHtml,
  1053	    screenshot_base64: mobileShot.base64,
  1054	    desktop_screenshot_base64: desktopShot.base64,
  1055	  });
  1056	
  1057	  const scoresValid =
  1058	    visionQa.scores &&
  1059	    typeof visionQa.scores.visual_hierarchy === "number" &&
  1060	    typeof visionQa.scores.responsive_readability === "number" &&
  1061	    typeof visionQa.scores.brand_consistency === "number" &&
  1062	    typeof visionQa.scores.disclosure_clarity === "number" &&
  1063	    visionQa.scores.visual_hierarchy >= 8 &&
  1064	    visionQa.scores.responsive_readability >= 8 &&
  1065	    visionQa.scores.brand_consistency >= 8 &&
  1066	    visionQa.scores.disclosure_clarity >= 8;
  1067	
  1068	  // P0-1 Fail closed: if Gemini Vision QA did not pass or scores below threshold, strictly refuse certification
  1069	  if (!visionQa.passed || !scoresValid) {
  1070	    return {
  1071	      ok: false,
  1072	      status: "VISION_QA_FAILED_OR_UNVERIFIED",
  1073	      prospect_id: staged.prospect_id,
  1074	      slug: staged.slug,
  1075	      secure_id: staged.secure_id,
  1076	      preview_url: null,
  1077	      dom_qa_verified: false,
  1078	      responsive_screenshots: capturedScreenshots.map((s) => ({
  1079	        width: s.width,
  1080	        height: s.height,
  1081	        byte_length: s.byte_length,
  1082	        sha256: s.sha256,
  1083	      })),
  1084	      vision_qa: visionQa,
  1085	      error: `Gemini Vision QA failed: ${visionQa.summary || visionQa.verdict}`,
  1086	    };
  1087	  }
  1088	
  1089	  const qaReceiptSha256 = createHash("sha256")
  1090	    .update(`gemini-vision-qa:${staged.prospect_id}:${JSON.stringify(visionQa)}`)
  1091	    .digest("hex");
  1092	
  1093	  // Step 3: Attach certified 6-width DOM QA proof into SmartSiteStore
  1094	  const certifiedRecord = store.attachInternalQaDomPreview(
  1095	    SITE_ID,
  1096	    staged.slug,
  1097	    {
  1098	      template_family: staged.template_family,
  1099	      source_commit: sourceCommit,
  1100	      qa_receipt_sha256: qaReceiptSha256,
  1101	      widths: [320, 390, 430, 768, 1024, 1440],
  1102	      screenshots: capturedScreenshots.map((s) => ({
  1103	        width: s.width,
  1104	        height: s.height,
  1105	        byte_length: s.byte_length,
  1106	        sha256: s.sha256,
  1107	      })),
  1108	    }
  1109	  );
  1110	
  1111	  // Canonical absolute preview URL for recipient inboxes
  1112	  const previewBaseUrl = (
  1113	    process.env.PUBLIC_CANONICAL_URL ||
  1114	    process.env.GEMINX_PREVIEW_FRONTEND_URL ||
  1115	    "https://www.entreprenuitywebdesign.com"
  1116	  ).replace(/\/+$/, "");
  1117	  const absolutePreviewUrl = `${previewBaseUrl}/outreach-preview/${certifiedRecord.secure_id}`;
  1118	
  1119	  // P0-3 Email Draft & Real Disk Artifact Generation
  1120	  const emailDir = path.join(path.resolve(root), "outreach", "email-artifacts");
  1121	  mkdirSync(emailDir, { recursive: true });
  1122	
  1123	  const businessName = String(input.business_name ?? staged.slug);
  1124	  const location = String(input.location ?? "West Midlands");
  1125	  const isCohortB = staged.cohort === "COHORT_B_LEGACY_REDESIGN";
  1126	
  1127	  const emailSubject = isCohortB
  1128	    ? `quick upgrade idea for ${businessName}'s website`
  1129	    : `website concept for ${businessName}`;
  1130	
  1131	  const cohortOpeningPlainText = isCohortB
  1132	    ? `Reviewed the web presence for ${businessName} in ${location} and noticed key opportunities to capture more customer enquiries on mobile devices:`
  1133	    : `While reviewing established trade businesses across ${location}, we noticed ${businessName} has strong presence but no dedicated responsive homepage for homeowners searching on mobile:`;
  1134	
  1135	  // Reuse the verified 1440px screenshot already selected for independent visual QA.
  1136	  const mockupSrc = desktopShot?.base64
  1137	    ? `data:image/png;base64,${desktopShot.base64}`
  1138	    : "cid:ambush-mockup-cid";
  1139	
  1140	  const emailHtml = `<!DOCTYPE html>
  1141	<html lang="en">
  1142	<head>
  1143	  <meta charset="utf-8" />
  1144	  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />
  1145	  <title>${emailSubject}</title>
  1146	</head>
  1147	<body style="margin: 0; padding: 0; background-color: #06101f; font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; color: #f1f5f9;">
  1148	  <table role="presentation" width="100%" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="background-color: #06101f; padding: 24px 12px;">
  1149	    <tr>
  1150	      <td align="center">
  1151	        <table role="presentation" width="100%" style="max-width: 620px; background-color: #0d1a2d; border: 1px solid #1e2e45; border-radius: 12px; overflow: hidden; padding: 24px; text-align: left;">
  1152	          
  1153	          <!-- HERO MOCKUP HEADER -->
  1154	          <tr>
  1155	            <td style="padding-bottom: 20px;">
  1156	              <div style="border-radius: 8px; overflow: hidden; border: 1px solid #2d4160; box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.5);">
  1157	                <a href="${absolutePreviewUrl}" target="_blank" style="display: block; text-decoration: none;">
  1158	                  <img src="${mockupSrc}" data-cid="cid:ambush-mockup-cid" alt="${businessName} Website Concept Mockup" width="600" style="width: 100%; max-width: 600px; display: block; border: 0; outline: none;" />
  1159	                </a>
  1160	              </div>
  1161	              <div style="text-align: center; margin-top: 10px; font-size: 11px; color: #94a3b8; letter-spacing: 0.05em; text-transform: uppercase;">
  1162	                Concept Direction Prepared for ${businessName} • ${location}
  1163	              </div>
  1164	            </td>
  1165	          </tr>
  1166	
  1167	          <!-- BODY COPY -->
  1168	          <tr>
  1169	            <td style="padding: 10px 4px 20px 4px; font-size: 15px; line-height: 1.6; color: #cbd5e1;">
  1170	              <p style="margin: 0 0 16px 0; font-size: 17px; font-weight: 600; color: #f8fafc;">
  1171	                Hello Team at ${businessName},
  1172	              </p>
  1173	              <p style="margin: 0 0 16px 0;">
  1174	                ${cohortOpeningPlainText}
  1175	              </p>
  1176	              <p style="margin: 0 0 16px 0;">
  1177	                ${isCohortB
  1178	                  ? `Rather than sending a generic note, our team put together a bespoke homepage redesign concept mockup tailored around ${businessName}'s real services and branding.`
  1179	                  : `To show what is possible, our team put together a bespoke homepage concept preview tailored specifically for ${businessName}.`}
  1180	              </p>
  1181	              
  1182	              <!-- CTA BUTTON -->
  1183	              <div style="margin: 28px 0; text-align: center;">
  1184	                <a href="${absolutePreviewUrl}" target="_blank" style="background-color: #d4af37; color: #000000; padding: 14px 28px; text-decoration: none; border-radius: 6px; font-weight: 700; display: inline-block; font-size: 15px; box-shadow: 0 4px 14px rgba(212,175,55,0.35);">
  1185	                  View Live Interactive Preview &amp; Kerry AI &rarr;
  1186	                </a>
  1187	              </div>
  1188	
  1189	              <!-- VALUE PROPOSITION & CAROUSEL HIGHLIGHT -->
  1190	              <p style="margin: 0 0 14px 0; font-weight: 600; color: #e2e8f0;">
  1191	                What is included in your live preview:
  1192	              </p>
  1193	              <ul style="margin: 0 0 20px 0; padding-left: 20px; color: #94a3b8; line-height: 1.7;">
  1194	                <li><strong style="color: #cbd5e1;">Responsive Multi-Device Showcase:</strong> Test the site layout on mobile, tablet, and desktop viewports.</li>
  1195	                <li><strong style="color: #cbd5e1;">Kerry AI Receptionist:</strong> 24/7 intelligent triage to capture emergency enquiries and customer details while you are on site.</li>
  1196	                <li><strong style="color: #cbd5e1;">10-Point Growth Infrastructure:</strong> Complete service suite including missed call recovery, review growth, and local SEO.</li>
  1197	              </ul>
  1198	              <p style="margin: 0 0 16px 0;">
  1199	                This concept is completely without obligation. We create these to show local trade leaders what modern web infrastructure looks like before spending a penny.
  1200	              </p>
  1201	              <p style="margin: 0; color: #94a3b8;">
  1202	                Best regards,<br />
  1203	                <strong style="color: #f1f5f9;">Rob &amp; The Entreprenuity Team</strong><br />
  1204	                Entreprenuity AI &amp; Web Design Solutions<br />
  1205	                <a href="https://www.entreprenuitywebdesign.com" style="color: #38bdf8; text-decoration: none;">www.entreprenuitywebdesign.com</a>
  1206	              </p>
  1207	            </td>
  1208	          </tr>
  1209	
  1210	          <!-- FOOTER -->
  1211	          <tr>
  1212	            <td style="border-top: 1px solid #1e2e45; padding-top: 16px; font-size: 11px; color: #64748b; text-align: center;">
  1213	              This is a private concept presentation delivered for preview verification.<br />
  1214	              Entreprenuity AI Solutions • Birmingham &amp; West Midlands<br />
  1215	              To opt out or unsubscribe from future commercial concept previews, reply <strong>UNSUBSCRIBE</strong> and your domain will be immediately added to our suppression ledger.
  1216	            </td>
  1217	          </tr>
  1218	
  1219	        </table>
  1220	      </td>
  1221	    </tr>
  1222	  </table>
  1223	</body>
  1224	</html>`;
  1225	
  1226	  const emailPlainText = [
  1227	    `Hi ${businessName} team,`,
  1228	    "",
  1229	    cohortOpeningPlainText,
  1230	    "",
  1231	    isCohortB
  1232	      ? `Rather than sending a generic note, our team put together a bespoke homepage redesign concept mockup tailored around ${businessName}'s real services and branding.`
  1233	      : `To show what is possible, our team put together a bespoke homepage concept preview tailored specifically for ${businessName}.`,
  1234	    "",
  1235	    `Personalised Concept Preview (with live Kerry AI guide): ${absolutePreviewUrl}`,
  1236	    "",
  1237	    "What is included in the live concept:",
  1238	    "- Responsive Multi-Device Preview: Test the design on mobile, tablet, and desktop views.",
  1239	    "- Kerry AI Receptionist: 24/7 intelligent triage to capture emergency enquiries and customer details while you are on site.",
  1240	    "- 10-Point Growth Infrastructure: Complete service suite including missed call recovery, review growth, and local SEO.",
  1241	    "",
  1242	    "This concept is completely without obligation.",
  1243	    "",
  1244	    "Best regards,",
  1245	    "Rob & The Entreprenuity Team",
  1246	    "Entreprenuity AI & Web Design Solutions",
  1247	    "https://www.entreprenuitywebdesign.com",
  1248	    "---",
  1249	    "Opt-out / Unsubscribe: To opt out of future commercial previews from Entreprenuity Business Solutions, reply UNSUBSCRIBE to this email and your domain will be permanently suppressed.",
  1250	  ].join("\n");
  1251	
  1252	  const htmlSha = createHash("sha256").update(emailHtml, "utf8").digest("hex");
  1253	  const textSha = createHash("sha256").update(emailPlainText, "utf8").digest("hex");
  1254	
  1255	  writeFileSync(path.join(emailDir, `${htmlSha}.html`), emailHtml, "utf8");
  1256	  writeFileSync(path.join(emailDir, `${textSha}.txt`), emailPlainText, "utf8");
  1257	
  1258	  const emailDraft: OfficeEmailArtifactContract = {
  1259	    schema_version: "geminx.ambush.email.v1",
  1260	    draft_id: `draft_${staged.slug}_${htmlSha.slice(0, 8)}`,
  1261	    campaign_id: `digital-ambush-${staged.slug}`,
  1262	    prospect_id: staged.slug,
  1263	    company_scope: "entreprenuity",
  1264	    cohort: staged.cohort as OfficeEmailArtifactContract["cohort"],
  1265	    sender_id: input.sender_id === "gmail" ? "gmail" : "zoho",
  1266	    sender_identity_id: process.env.GEMINX_ZOHO_EMAIL || "info@entreprenuity.co.uk",
  1267	    recipient_type: "EXTERNAL_PROSPECT",
  1268	    subject: emailSubject,
  1269	    html_artifact_sha256: htmlSha,
  1270	    text_artifact_sha256: textSha,
  1271	    image_artifact_sha256: desktopShot?.sha256 || qaReceiptSha256,
  1272	    concept_url: absolutePreviewUrl,
  1273	    approval_state: "PENDING",
  1274	    outbound_idempotency_key: `ambush_${staged.slug}_${htmlSha.slice(0, 8)}`,
  1275	    provider_message_id: null,
  1276	    provider_thread_id: null,
  1277	    sent_at: null,
  1278	  };
  1279	
  1280	  ledger.email_drafts = [
  1281	    ...ledger.email_drafts.filter((d) => d.prospect_id !== staged.slug && !d.draft_id.includes(staged.slug)),
  1282	    emailDraft,
  1283	  ];
  1284	
  1285	  // Step 4: Ensure Kerry approval card exists in office ledger for 1-Tap Founder decision
  1286	  const approvalId = `appr_send_${staged.slug}_${qaReceiptSha256.slice(0, 8)}_${htmlSha.slice(0, 8)}`;
  1287	
  1288	  // Atomically invalidate / remove any existing pending approval for this prospect with differing QA or HTML artifacts
  1289	  ledger.pending_approvals = ledger.pending_approvals.filter(
  1290	    (a) => !(a.prospect_id === staged.slug && a.kind === "EXTERNAL_PROSPECT_SEND" && a.approval_id !== approvalId)
  1291	  );
  1292	
  1293	  const rawAgent = String((input as any).actor_agent ?? (input as any).owner_agent ?? "").trim();
  1294	  const requestedBy: OfficeAgentId = isOfficeAgentId(rawAgent) ? rawAgent : "kerry";
  1295	
  1296	  let pendingItem = ledger.pending_approvals.find((a) => a.approval_id === approvalId);
  1297	  if (!pendingItem) {
  1298	    const dispatchMode: "INTERNAL_CANARY" | "EXTERNAL_PROSPECT" = input.dispatch_mode === "internal_canary" || !input.external_send ? "INTERNAL_CANARY" : "EXTERNAL_PROSPECT";
  1299	    const targetRecipient = input.external_send && input.recipient ? input.recipient : (process.env.GEMINX_ZOHO_APPROVED_RECIPIENT ?? "glowauraboutique@gmail.com");
  1300	
  1301	    const createdApproval = {
  1302	      approval_id: approvalId,
  1303	      company_scope: "entreprenuity" as const,
  1304	      prospect_id: staged.slug,
  1305	      requested_by: requestedBy,
  1306	      gate_owner: "founder" as const,
  1307	      kind: "EXTERNAL_PROSPECT_SEND" as const,
  1308	      status: "PENDING" as const,
  1309	      summary: `[${dispatchMode}] Destination: ${targetRecipient} | HTML concept & email staged for ${input.business_name ?? staged.slug} (${staged.cohort}). 1-Tap Founder approval required.`,
  1310	      action_url: absolutePreviewUrl,
  1311	      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  1312	      dispatch_mode: dispatchMode,
  1313	      target_recipient: targetRecipient,
  1314	      research_provenance: staged.research_provenance,
  1315	      qa_receipt_sha256: qaReceiptSha256,
  1316	      html_artifact_sha256: htmlSha,
  1317	    };
  1318	    ledger.pending_approvals.push(createdApproval);
  1319	    pendingItem = createdApproval;
  1320	  }
  1321	
  1322	  // Also record / update prospect lock
  1323	  const lockIndex = ledger.prospect_locks.findIndex((l) => l.prospect_id === staged.slug);
  1324	  const lockRecord = {
  1325	    prospect_id: staged.slug,
  1326	    slug: staged.slug,
  1327	    business_name: String(input.business_name ?? staged.slug),
  1328	    company_scope: "entreprenuity",
  1329	    cohort: staged.cohort,
  1330	    official_website: input.website ?? null,
  1331	    friction_signals: input.public_findings ?? [],
  1332	    sender_id: input.sender_id === "gmail" ? ("gmail" as const) : ("zoho" as const),
  1333	    sender_cohort_cell: null,
  1334	    owner_agent: validatedNow.actor_agent,
  1335	    lease_token: validatedNow.lease_token,
  1336	    lease_expires_at: validatedNow.lease_expires_at,
  1337	    fenced_tokens: validatedNow.fenced_tokens,
  1338	    stage: "QA_APPROVED" as const,
  1339	    concept_url: absolutePreviewUrl,
  1340	    mockup_artifact_sha256: desktopShot?.sha256 ?? null,
  1341	    mockup_content_url: desktopShot?.sha256 ? `/api/v1/founder/uploads/${desktopShot.sha256}/content` : null,
  1342	    email_artifact_sha256: htmlSha,
  1343	    email_preview_url: absolutePreviewUrl,
  1344	    qa_receipt_id: qaReceiptSha256.slice(0, 16),
  1345	    outbound_receipt_id: null,
  1346	    provider_message_id: null,
  1347	    updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  1348	  };
  1349	
  1350	  if (lockIndex >= 0) {
  1351	    ledger.prospect_locks[lockIndex] = lockRecord;
  1352	  } else {
  1353	    ledger.prospect_locks.push(lockRecord);
  1354	  }
  1355	
  1356	  appendOfficeActivityEvent(ledger, {
  1357	    venture_id: "entreprenuity",
  1358	    actor_agent: validatedNow.actor_agent,
  1359	    phase: "DIGITAL_AMBUSH_DOM_STAGED_AND_QA_PASSED",
  1360	    status: "COMPLETED_PROVEN",
  1361	    label: `Digital Ambush run complete for ${input.business_name ?? staged.slug}: 6-width DOM QA passed, Gemini 3.8 Flash Vision verified, staged for Founder 1-Tap dispatch`,
  1362	  });
  1363	
  1364	  // A six-viewport + Vision run can outlast a lease. Compare the latest
  1365	  // persisted lock immediately before commit and abort if a colleague claimed,
  1366	  // fenced, or expired the lease during the expensive QA work.
  1367	  const latestLock=readOfficeLedger(root).prospect_locks.find(l=>l.prospect_id===staged.slug);
  1368	  if (latestLock?.lease_token !== existingLock?.lease_token ||
  1369	      latestLock?.owner_agent !== existingLock?.owner_agent) {
  1370	    throw new Error("AMBUSH_LEASE_CHANGED_DURING_QA");
  1371	  }
  1372	  if (latestLock) validateDomStageOwnership(latestLock,execution);
  1373	  if (!latestLock && !execution?.authenticated) {
  1374	    throw new Error("AMBUSH_AUTHENTICATED_ACTOR_REQUIRED");
  1375	  }
  1376	  writeOfficeLedger(ledger, root);
  1377	
  1378	  return {
  1379	    ok: true,
  1380	    status: "DOM_QA_PASSED_AWAITING_FOUNDER_APPROVAL",
  1381	    prospect_id: certifiedRecord.prospect_id,
  1382	    slug: certifiedRecord.slug,
  1383	    secure_id: certifiedRecord.secure_id,
  1384	    preview_url: `/outreach-preview/${certifiedRecord.secure_id}`,
  1385	    template_family: staged.template_family,
  1386	    cohort: certifiedRecord.cohort,
  1387	    vision_qa: visionQa,
  1388	    dom_qa_verified: true,
  1389	    widths_verified: [320, 390, 430, 768, 1024, 1440],
  1390	    responsive_screenshots: capturedScreenshots.map((s) => ({
  1391	      width: s.width,
  1392	      height: s.height,
  1393	      byte_length: s.byte_length,
  1394	      sha256: s.sha256,
  1395	    })),
  1396	    approval_id: pendingItem?.approval_id ?? approvalId,
  1397	    approval_status: pendingItem?.status ?? "PENDING",
  1398	    next_action: "Founder review via Founder Office 1-Tap approval (/approvals/decide) triggers immediate Zoho email dispatch.",
  1399	  };
  1400	}
  1401	
  1402	export function getDigitalAmbushStatus(slugOrProspectId: string, root = geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot()) {
  1403	  const store = new SmartSiteStore(root);
  1404	  const ledger = readOfficeLedger(root);
  1405	  const slug = String(slugOrProspectId ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
  1406	  const prospect = store.listProspects(SITE_ID).find((p) => p.slug === slug || p.prospect_id === slug);
  1407	  const lock = ledger.prospect_locks.find((l) => l.slug === slug || l.prospect_id === slug);
  1408	  const approval = ledger.pending_approvals.find((a) => a.prospect_id === slug && a.kind === "EXTERNAL_PROSPECT_SEND");
  1409	  const draft = ledger.email_drafts.find((d) => d.prospect_id === slug || d.draft_id.includes(slug));
  1410	
  1411	  return {
  1412	    ok: true,
  1413	    found: Boolean(prospect || lock),
  1414	    slug,
  1415	    prospect: prospect
  1416	      ? {
  1417	          prospect_id: prospect.prospect_id,
  1418	          secure_id: prospect.secure_id,
  1419	          business_name: prospect.business_name,
  1420	          cohort: prospect.cohort,
  1421	          preview_qa_status: prospect.preview_qa_status,
  1422	          dom_qa: prospect.preview_dom_qa,
  1423	          preview_url: `/outreach-preview/${prospect.secure_id}`,
  1424	        }
  1425	      : null,
  1426	    lock: lock
  1427	      ? {
  1428	          stage: lock.stage,
  1429	          owner_agent: lock.owner_agent,
  1430	          concept_url: lock.concept_url,
  1431	          provider_message_id: lock.provider_message_id,
  1432	          updated_at: lock.updated_at,
  1433	        }
  1434	      : null,
  1435	    approval: approval
  1436	      ? {
  1437	          approval_id: approval.approval_id,
  1438	          status: approval.status,
  1439	          gate_owner: approval.gate_owner,
  1440	          action_url: approval.action_url,
  1441	        }
  1442	      : null,
  1443	    draft: draft
  1444	      ? {
  1445	          draft_id: draft.draft_id,
  1446	          subject: draft.subject,
  1447	          concept_url: draft.concept_url,
  1448	          html_artifact_sha256: draft.html_artifact_sha256,
  1449	          approval_state: draft.approval_state,
  1450	          provider_message_id: draft.provider_message_id,
  1451	          sent_at: draft.sent_at,
  1452	        }
  1453	      : null,
  1454	  };
  1455	}

   101	export function isOfficeAgentId(value: unknown): value is OfficeAgentId {
   102	  const clean = String(value ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
   103	  return (OFFICE_AGENT_IDS as readonly string[]).includes(clean);
   104	}
   105	
   106	export function normalizeOfficeAgentId(
   107	  value: unknown,
   108	  fallback: OfficeAgentId = "neo"
   109	): OfficeAgentId {
   110	  const clean = String(value ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
   111	  if (clean === "adam") return "ads";
   112	  if (clean === "geminex") return "geminx";
   113	  return isOfficeAgentId(clean) ? clean : fallback;
   114	}
   115	
   116	export function normalizeCompanyScope(value: unknown, agentId?: OfficeAgentId): string {
   117	  const raw = String(value ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
   118	  if (raw === "wild_botanics" || raw === "wildbotanics" || raw === "wild-botanics" || raw.includes("botanic")) {
   119	    return "wild-botanics";
   120	  }
   121	  if (
   122	    raw === "entreprenuity" ||
   123	    raw === "entreprenuity-business-solutions" ||
   124	    raw === "entreprenuity_ai"
   125	  ) {
   126	    return "entreprenuity";
   127	  }
   128	  if (raw === "holding" || raw === "all" || raw === "geminx-holding") {
   129	    return "holding";
   130	  }
   131	  if (raw.length > 0 && raw !== "default") {
   132	    return raw.replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]/g, "-").slice(0, 64);
   133	  }
   134	  if (agentId === "sophie") return "entreprenuity";
   135	  if (agentId === "geminx" || agentId === "ag") return "holding";
   136	  return "entreprenuity";
   137	}
