   900	export function getOfficeAgentManifest(agentId: unknown): OfficeAgentManifest {
   901	  return OFFICE_AGENT_MANIFESTS[normalizeOfficeAgentId(agentId, "neo")];
   902	}
   903	
   904	export interface ToolAccessCheckResult {
   905	  allowed: boolean;
   906	  code:
   907	    | "ALLOWED"
   908	    | "OFFICE_ROLE_TOOL_DENIED"
   909	    | "OFFICE_CROSS_COMPANY_ISOLATION_VIOLATION";
   910	  reason: string;
   911	  agent_id: OfficeAgentId;
   912	  tool_name: string;
   913	  company_scope: string;
   914	}
   915	
   916	export function checkOfficeAgentToolAccess(
   917	  agentOrParams: unknown,
   918	  maybeToolName?: string,
   919	  maybeCompanyScope?: unknown,
   920	  maybeTargetCompanyScope?: unknown
   921	): ToolAccessCheckResult {
   922	  let rawAgentId: unknown;
   923	  let rawToolName: string;
   924	  let rawCompanyScope: unknown;
   925	  let rawTargetCompanyScope: unknown;
   926	
   927	  if (agentOrParams && typeof agentOrParams === "object" && !Array.isArray(agentOrParams) && "toolName" in agentOrParams) {
   928	    const p = agentOrParams as {
   929	      agentId?: unknown;
   930	      toolName?: string;
   931	      companyScope?: unknown;
   932	      targetCompanyScope?: unknown;
   933	    };
   934	    rawAgentId = p.agentId;
   935	    rawToolName = String(p.toolName ?? "");
   936	    rawCompanyScope = p.companyScope;
   937	    rawTargetCompanyScope = p.targetCompanyScope;
   938	  } else {
   939	    rawAgentId = agentOrParams;
   940	    rawToolName = String(maybeToolName ?? "");
   941	    rawCompanyScope = maybeCompanyScope;
   942	    rawTargetCompanyScope = maybeTargetCompanyScope;
   943	  }
   944	
   945	  const agentId = normalizeOfficeAgentId(rawAgentId, "neo");
   946	  const manifest = OFFICE_AGENT_MANIFESTS[agentId];
   947	  const toolName = rawToolName.trim();
   948	  const activeScope = normalizeCompanyScope(rawCompanyScope, agentId);
   949	  const targetScope = rawTargetCompanyScope
   950	    ? normalizeCompanyScope(rawTargetCompanyScope, agentId)
   951	    : activeScope;
   952	
   953	  if (
   954	    targetScope &&
   955	    targetScope !== "holding" &&
   956	    !manifest.readable_company_scopes.includes("holding") &&
   957	    !manifest.readable_company_scopes.includes(targetScope)
   958	  ) {
   959	    return {
   960	      allowed: false,
   961	      code: "OFFICE_CROSS_COMPANY_ISOLATION_VIOLATION",
   962	      reason: `Agent '${manifest.display_name}' (${agentId}) is scoped to [${manifest.readable_company_scopes.join(", ")}] and is forbidden from accessing company_scope='${targetScope}'.`,
   963	      agent_id: agentId,
   964	      tool_name: toolName,
   965	      company_scope: targetScope,
   966	    };
   967	  }
   968	
   969	  if (
   970	    agentId === "sophie" &&
   971	    (toolName === "office_digital_ambush_stage" ||
   972	      toolName.startsWith("zoho_mail_") ||
   973	      toolName.startsWith("gmail_"))
   974	  ) {
   975	    return {
   976	      allowed: false,
   977	      code: "OFFICE_CROSS_COMPANY_ISOLATION_VIOLATION",
   978	      reason:
   979	        "Sophie owns Entreprenuity AI social content only. Digital Ambush CRM staging and outbound email remain restricted to separately authorised agents.",
   980	      agent_id: agentId,
   981	      tool_name: toolName,
   982	      company_scope: activeScope,
   983	    };
   984	  }
   985	
   986	  if (agentId === "brian" && [
   987	    "github_create_branch", "github_create_or_update_file", "github_create_pull_request"
   988	  ].includes(toolName)) {
   989	    // The canonical ToolRegistry independently fences actual mutation arguments.
   990	    return { allowed: true, code: "ALLOWED", reason: "Brian isolated preview-only GitHub authoring.", agent_id: agentId, tool_name: toolName, company_scope: activeScope };
   991	  }
   992	
   993	  if (manifest.denied_tools.includes(toolName)) {
   994	    return {
   995	      allowed: false,
   996	      code: "OFFICE_ROLE_TOOL_DENIED",
   997	      reason: `Tool '${toolName}' is explicitly denied for role '${manifest.display_name}' (${manifest.role_title}). Use 'escalate_to_ag' if a runtime or code repair is needed.`,
   998	      agent_id: agentId,
   999	      tool_name: toolName,
  1000	      company_scope: activeScope,
  1001	    };
  1002	  }
  1003	
  1004	  if (manifest.denied_tool_prefixes.some((prefix) => toolName.startsWith(prefix))) {
  1005	    return {
  1006	      allowed: false,
  1007	      code: "OFFICE_ROLE_TOOL_DENIED",
  1008	      reason: `Tool family matching '${toolName}' is denied for operating agent '${manifest.display_name}'. Only AG Sentinel holds unrestricted runtime/shell/deployment repair authority; call 'escalate_to_ag' instead.`,
  1009	      agent_id: agentId,
  1010	      tool_name: toolName,
  1011	      company_scope: activeScope,
  1012	    };
  1013	  }
  1014	
  1015	  if (
  1016	    manifest.permitted_tools.includes(toolName) ||
  1017	    manifest.permitted_tool_prefixes.some((prefix) => toolName.startsWith(prefix))
  1018	  ) {
  1019	    return {
  1020	      allowed: true,
  1021	      code: "ALLOWED",
  1022	      reason: `Permitted by ${manifest.display_name} role manifest.`,
  1023	      agent_id: agentId,
  1024	      tool_name: toolName,
  1025	      company_scope: activeScope,
  1026	    };
  1027	  }
  1028	
  1029	  return {
  1030	    allowed: false,
  1031	    code: "OFFICE_ROLE_TOOL_DENIED",
  1032	    reason: `Tool '${toolName}' is outside the least-privilege allowlist for '${manifest.display_name}' (${agentId}).`,
  1033	    agent_id: agentId,
  1034	    tool_name: toolName,
  1035	    company_scope: activeScope,
  1036	  };
  1037	}

  1279	  metricool_post_id: string | null;
  1280	  attribution_url: string | null;
  1281	}

  1345	
  1346	interface OfficeLedgerShape {
  1347	  version: 1;
  1348	  tasks: OfficeTaskLeaseRecord[];
  1349	  task_checkpoints: OfficeTaskCheckpointRecord[];
  1350	  prospect_locks: OfficeProspectLockRecord[];
  1351	  handoffs: OfficeHandoffPacket[];
  1352	  receipts: OfficeVerifiedReceipt[];
  1353	  delegation_receipts: OfficeDelegationReceipt[];
  1354	  activity_events: OfficeActivityEvent[];
  1355	  email_drafts: OfficeEmailArtifactContract[];
  1356	  media_tasks: OfficeMediaTaskContract[];
  1357	  incidents: OfficeIncidentRecord[];
  1358	  pending_approvals: OfficePendingApprovalRecord[];
  1359	  wild_botanics_packages: OfficeWildBotanicsPackage[];
  1360	  sophie_social_packages: OfficeSophieSocialPackage[];
  1361	  last_event_sequence: number;
  1362	  agent_states: Record<
  1363	    OfficeAgentId,
  1364	    {
  1365	      status: OfficeAgentRuntimeState;
  1366	      runtime_used: "openclaw-gateway" | "geminx-cloud";
  1367	      company_scope: string;
  1368	      current_task_id: string | null;
  1369	      current_prospect_id: string | null;
  1370	      active_subagents: string[];
  1371	      activity_label?: string | null;
  1372	      blocked_reason?: string | null;
  1373	      last_receipt_hash: string | null;
  1374	      last_active_at: string | null;
  1375	      heartbeat_at?: string | null;
  1376	      heartbeat_provenance?: string | null;
  1377	    }
  1378	  >;
  1379	}
  1380	
  1381	export const DEFAULT_LEASE_TTL_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000;
  1382	
  1383	function defaultAgentStates(): OfficeLedgerShape["agent_states"] {
  1384	  const out = {} as OfficeLedgerShape["agent_states"];
  1385	  for (const id of OFFICE_AGENT_IDS) {
  1386	    out[id] = {
  1387	      status: "IDLE",
  1388	      runtime_used: "geminx-cloud",
  1389	      company_scope: OFFICE_AGENT_MANIFESTS[id].primary_company_scope,
  1390	      current_task_id: null,
  1391	      current_prospect_id: null,
  1392	      active_subagents: [],
  1393	      activity_label: "Standing by on Office Floor",
  1394	      blocked_reason: null,
  1395	      last_receipt_hash: null,
  1396	      last_active_at: null,
  1397	      heartbeat_at: null,
  1398	      heartbeat_provenance: null,
  1399	    };
  1400	  }
  1401	  return out;
  1402	}
  1403	
  1404	function createEmptyOfficeLedger(): OfficeLedgerShape {
  1405	  return {
  1406	    version: 1,
  1407	    tasks: [],
  1408	    task_checkpoints: [],
  1409	    prospect_locks: [],
  1410	    handoffs: [],
  1411	    receipts: [],
  1412	    delegation_receipts: [],
  1413	    activity_events: [],
  1414	    email_drafts: [],
  1415	    media_tasks: [],
  1416	    incidents: [],
  1417	    pending_approvals: [],
  1418	    wild_botanics_packages: [],
  1419	    sophie_social_packages: [],
  1420	    last_event_sequence: 0,
  1421	    agent_states: defaultAgentStates(),
  1422	  };
  1423	}
  1424	
  1425	function officeLedgerPath(runtimeRoot = geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot()): string {
  1426	  const dir = path.join(path.resolve(runtimeRoot), "office-floor");
  1427	  mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  1428	  return path.join(dir, "ledger.json");
  1429	}
  1430	
  1431	/** Historical type name maintained for persisted old office ledgers. */
  1432	export type OfficeWildBotanicsPackage = OfficeSophieSocialPackage;
  1433	
  1434	export function readOfficeLedger(runtimeRoot = geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot()): OfficeLedgerShape {
  1435	  const filePath = officeLedgerPath(runtimeRoot);
  1436	  if (!existsSync(filePath)) return createEmptyOfficeLedger();
  1437	  try {
  1438	    const parsed = JSON.parse(readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")) as Partial<OfficeLedgerShape>;
  1439	    const defaults = defaultAgentStates();
  1440	    const mergedStates = { ...defaults, ...(parsed.agent_states ?? {}) };
  1441	    for (const id of OFFICE_AGENT_IDS) {
  1442	      if (!mergedStates[id]) {
  1443	        mergedStates[id] = defaults[id];
  1444	      } else {
  1445	        const heartbeatRaw = mergedStates[id].heartbeat_at || mergedStates[id].last_active_at;
  1446	        if (heartbeatRaw) {
  1447	          const ageMs = Date.now() - new Date(heartbeatRaw).getTime();
  1448	          if (ageMs > 60 * 60 * 1000 && mergedStates[id].status !== "IDLE") {
  1449	            mergedStates[id].status = "IDLE";
  1450	            mergedStates[id].current_task_id = null;
  1451	            mergedStates[id].activity_label = "Standing by on Office Floor";
  1452	          }
  1453	        }
  1454	      }
  1455	    }
  1456	    const locks = Array.isArray(parsed.prospect_locks) ? parsed.prospect_locks : [];
  1457	    // Truthful telemetry: Reclassify legacy simulated canary runs from "SENT" to "INTERNAL_CANARY_SIMULATED"
  1458	    for (const lock of locks) {
  1459	      if (lock.stage === "SENT") {
  1460	        const msgId = String(lock.provider_message_id ?? "");
  1461	        const isSyntheticCanary =
  1462	          msgId.startsWith("msg_zoho_mail_") ||
  1463	          msgId.startsWith("msg_gmail_") ||
  1464	          msgId.startsWith("canary_sim_") ||
  1465	          !lock.provider_message_id;
  1466	        if (isSyntheticCanary) {
  1467	          lock.stage = "INTERNAL_CANARY_SIMULATED";
  1468	        }
  1469	      }
  1470	    }
  1471	    return {
  1472	      version: 1,
  1473	      tasks: Array.isArray(parsed.tasks) ? parsed.tasks : [],
  1474	      task_checkpoints: Array.isArray(parsed.task_checkpoints) ? parsed.task_checkpoints : [],
  1475	      prospect_locks: locks,
  1476	      handoffs: Array.isArray(parsed.handoffs) ? parsed.handoffs : [],
  1477	      receipts: Array.isArray(parsed.receipts) ? parsed.receipts : [],
  1478	      delegation_receipts: Array.isArray(parsed.delegation_receipts) ? parsed.delegation_receipts : [],
  1479	      activity_events: Array.isArray(parsed.activity_events) ? parsed.activity_events : [],
  1480	      email_drafts: Array.isArray(parsed.email_drafts) ? parsed.email_drafts : [],
  1481	      media_tasks: Array.isArray(parsed.media_tasks) ? parsed.media_tasks : [],
  1482	      incidents: Array.isArray(parsed.incidents) ? parsed.incidents : [],
  1483	      pending_approvals: Array.isArray(parsed.pending_approvals) ? parsed.pending_approvals : [],
  1484	      wild_botanics_packages: Array.isArray(parsed.wild_botanics_packages)
  1485	        ? parsed.wild_botanics_packages
  1486	        : [],
  1487	      sophie_social_packages: Array.isArray(parsed.sophie_social_packages)
  1488	        ? parsed.sophie_social_packages
  1489	        : [],
  1490	      last_event_sequence:
  1491	        typeof parsed.last_event_sequence === "number"
  1492	          ? parsed.last_event_sequence
  1493	          : Array.isArray(parsed.activity_events)
  1494	            ? parsed.activity_events.length
  1495	            : 0,
  1496	      agent_states: mergedStates,
  1497	    };
  1498	  } catch (cause) {
  1499	    const detail = cause instanceof Error ? cause.message.slice(0, 180) : "unreadable ledger";
  1500	    throw new Error("OFFICE_LEDGER_CORRUPT_FAIL_CLOSED: " + detail);
  1501	  }
  1502	}
  1503	
  1504	export function writeOfficeLedger(
  1505	  ledger: OfficeLedgerShape,
  1506	  runtimeRoot = geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot()
  1507	): void {
  1508	  const filePath = officeLedgerPath(runtimeRoot);
  1509	  const tempPath = `${filePath}.${process.pid}.${randomUUID()}.tmp`;
  1510	  try {
  1511	    writeFileSync(tempPath, JSON.stringify(ledger, null, 2), { encoding: "utf8", flag: "wx" });
  1512	    try {
  1513	      renameSync(tempPath, filePath);
  1514	    } catch {
  1515	      copyFileSync(tempPath, filePath);
  1516	      rmSync(tempPath, { force: true });
  1517	    }
  1518	  } catch (err) {
  1519	    rmSync(tempPath, { force: true });
  1520	    throw err;
  1521	  }
  1522	}
  1523	
  1524	export function resetOfficeLedgerForTests(runtimeRoot = geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot()): void {
  1525	  writeOfficeLedger(createEmptyOfficeLedger(), runtimeRoot);
  1526	}
  1527	
  1528	export function resolveDualRuntimeSelection(): {
  1529	  runtime_used: "openclaw-gateway" | "geminx-cloud";
  1530	  openclaw_configured: boolean;
  1531	  failover_active: boolean;
  1532	  detail: string;
  1533	} {
  1534	  const remoteUrl = String(process.env.GEMINX_OPENCLAW_PUBLIC_URL ?? "").trim();
  1535	  const remoteToken = String(process.env.GEMINX_OPENCLAW_GATEWAY_TOKEN ?? "").trim();
  1536	  const localEnabled = /^(1|true|yes|on)$/i.test(String(process.env.GEMINX_ENABLE_OPENCLAW ?? "").trim());
  1537	  const openClawOnlineOverride = String(process.env.GEMINX_OPENCLAW_FORCE_OFFLINE ?? "").trim() === "1";
  1538	
  1539	  if (openClawOnlineOverride) {
  1540	    return {
  1541	      runtime_used: "geminx-cloud",
  1542	      openclaw_configured: Boolean((remoteUrl && remoteToken) || localEnabled),
  1543	      failover_active: true,
  1544	      detail: "OpenClaw gateway marked offline; executing seamlessly via GeminX native cloud runtime failover.",
  1545	    };
  1546	  }
  1547	
  1548	  if ((remoteUrl && remoteToken) || localEnabled) {
  1549	    return {
  1550	      runtime_used: "geminx-cloud",
  1551	      openclaw_configured: true,
  1552	      failover_active: false,
  1553	      detail:
  1554	        "Dual-runtime ready: GeminX native cloud runtime active with OpenClaw gateway bridge available on demand.",
  1555	    };
  1556	  }
  1557	
  1558	  return {
  1559	    runtime_used: "geminx-cloud",
  1560	    openclaw_configured: false,
  1561	    failover_active: true,
  1562	    detail: "OpenClaw gateway not active on this host; executing natively on GeminX cloud runtime.",
  1563	  };
  1564	}
  1565	
  1566	export function appendOfficeActivityEvent(
  1567	  ledger: OfficeLedgerShape,
  1568	  params: {
  1569	    venture_id?: string;
  1570	    campaign_id?: string;
  1571	    actor_agent: OfficeAgentId;
  1572	    worker_profile?: string | null;
  1573	    task_id?: string | null;
  1574	    parent_task_id?: string | null;
  1575	    phase: string;
  1576	    status: OfficeActivityEvent["status"];
  1577	    label: string;
  1578	    tool_name?: string | null;
  1579	    tool_call_id?: string | null;
  1580	    receipt_id?: string | null;
  1581	    error_code?: string | null;
  1582	    cost_usd?: number;
  1583	  }
  1584	): OfficeActivityEvent {
  1585	  const nextSeq = (ledger.last_event_sequence ?? 0) + 1;
  1586	  ledger.last_event_sequence = nextSeq;
  1587	  const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
  1588	  const safeLabel = String(params.label || params.phase).slice(0, 220);
  1589	  const event: OfficeActivityEvent = {
  1590	    schema_version: "geminx.office.activity.v1",
  1591	    event_id: `evt_${randomUUID().slice(0, 12)}`,
  1592	    sequence: nextSeq,
  1593	    timestamp: nowIso,
  1594	    venture_id: params.venture_id ?? ledger.agent_states[params.actor_agent]?.company_scope ?? "entreprenuity",
  1595	    campaign_id: params.campaign_id ?? "digital-ambush-test",
  1596	    actor_agent: params.actor_agent,
  1597	    worker_profile: params.worker_profile ?? null,
  1598	    task_id: params.task_id ?? null,
  1599	    parent_task_id: params.parent_task_id ?? null,
  1600	    phase: params.phase,
  1601	    status: params.status,
  1602	    label: safeLabel,
  1603	    tool_name: params.tool_name ?? null,
  1604	    tool_call_id: params.tool_call_id ?? null,
  1605	    receipt_id: params.receipt_id ?? null,
  1606	    error_code: params.error_code ?? null,
  1607	    cost_usd: typeof params.cost_usd === "number" ? params.cost_usd : 0,
  1608	  };
  1609	  ledger.activity_events = [...ledger.activity_events.slice(-9999), event];
  1610	  if (ledger.agent_states[params.actor_agent]) {
  1611	    ledger.agent_states[params.actor_agent].activity_label = safeLabel;
  1612	    ledger.agent_states[params.actor_agent].last_active_at = nowIso;
  1613	    if (params.error_code) {
  1614	      ledger.agent_states[params.actor_agent].blocked_reason = `${params.error_code}: ${safeLabel}`;
  1615	    } else if (params.status === "COMPLETED_PROVEN" || params.status === "RUNNING") {
  1616	      ledger.agent_states[params.actor_agent].blocked_reason = null;
  1617	    }
  1618	  }
  1619	  return event;
  1620	}
  1621	
  1622	export function claimOfficeTask(
  1623	  params: {
  1624	    task_id?: string;
  1625	    title: string;
  1626	    company_scope?: string;
  1627	    prospect_id?: string | null;
  1628	    owner_agent: unknown;
  1629	    subagent_id?: string | null;
  1630	    parent_task_id?: string | null;
  1631	    stage?: string;
  1632	    planned_stages?: string[];
  1633	    execution_runtime?: "railway-openclaw" | "local-openclaw" | "geminx-cloud";
  1634	    lease_ttl_ms?: number;
  1635	    now_ms?: number;
  1636	  },
  1637	  runtimeRoot = geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot()
  1638	): {
  1639	  ok: boolean;
  1640	  code:
  1641	    | "TASK_LEASE_CLAIMED"
  1642	    | "TASK_LEASE_RENEWED"
  1643	    | "TASK_LEASE_RECLAIMED_AFTER_EXPIRY"
  1644	    | "OFFICE_TASK_LEASE_HELD_BY_COLLEAGUE"
  1645	    | "OFFICE_TASK_CANCELLED_NO_RESURRECT"
  1646	    | "OFFICE_CROSS_COMPANY_ISOLATION_VIOLATION";
  1647	  task?: OfficeTaskLeaseRecord;
  1648	  holder?: OfficeAgentId;
  1649	  lease_token?: string;
  1650	  lease_expires_at?: string;
  1651	  reason?: string;
  1652	} {
  1653	  const ownerAgent = normalizeOfficeAgentId(params.owner_agent, "neo");
  1654	  const manifest = OFFICE_AGENT_MANIFESTS[ownerAgent];
  1655	  const companyScope = normalizeCompanyScope(params.company_scope, ownerAgent);
  1656	
  1657	  if (
  1658	    companyScope !== "holding" &&
  1659	    !manifest.writable_company_scopes.includes("holding") &&
  1660	    !manifest.writable_company_scopes.includes(companyScope)
  1661	  ) {
  1662	    return {
  1663	      ok: false,
  1664	      code: "OFFICE_CROSS_COMPANY_ISOLATION_VIOLATION",
  1665	      reason: `Agent '${manifest.display_name}' cannot claim write leases in company_scope='${companyScope}'.`,
  1666	    };
  1667	  }
  1668	
  1669	  const nowMs = params.now_ms ?? Date.now();
  1670	  const nowIso = new Date(nowMs).toISOString();
  1671	  const ttlMs = Math.max(5_000, Math.min(3_600_000, params.lease_ttl_ms ?? DEFAULT_LEASE_TTL_MS));
  1672	  const expiresIso = new Date(nowMs + ttlMs).toISOString();
  1673	  const taskId = String(params.task_id ?? "").trim() || `task_${randomUUID().slice(0, 12)}`;
  1674	  const prospectId = params.prospect_id ? String(params.prospect_id).trim() : null;
  1675	
  1676	  const ledger = readOfficeLedger(runtimeRoot);
  1677	  const existing = ledger.tasks.find(
  1678	    (t) =>
  1679	      t.task_id === taskId ||
  1680	      (prospectId &&
  1681	        t.prospect_id === prospectId &&
  1682	        t.company_scope === companyScope &&
  1683	        t.status !== "COMPLETED")
  1684	  );
  1685	
  1686	  const dual = resolveDualRuntimeSelection();
  1687	  const requestedRuntime = params.execution_runtime;
  1688	  if (requestedRuntime && ownerAgent !== "neo" && requestedRuntime !== "geminx-cloud") {
  1689	    return { ok: false, code: "OFFICE_CROSS_COMPANY_ISOLATION_VIOLATION", reason: "Only NEO may claim an OpenClaw execution runtime." };
  1690	  }
  1691	
  1692	  if (existing) {
  1693	    if ((existing.status as string) === "CANCELLED") {
  1694	      return {
  1695	        ok: false,
  1696	        code: "OFFICE_TASK_CANCELLED_NO_RESURRECT",
  1697	        task: existing,
  1698	        reason: `Task '${existing.task_id}' was explicitly CANCELLED and cannot resurrect without a new task_id.`,
  1699	      };
  1700	    }
  1701	
  1702	    const expiryMs = Date.parse(existing.lease_expires_at);
  1703	    const isExpired = !Number.isFinite(expiryMs) || expiryMs <= nowMs;
  1704	
  1705	    if (!isExpired && existing.owner_agent !== ownerAgent && existing.status !== "COMPLETED") {
  1706	      return {
  1707	        ok: false,
  1708	        code: "OFFICE_TASK_LEASE_HELD_BY_COLLEAGUE",
  1709	        holder: existing.owner_agent,
  1710	        lease_token: existing.lease_token,
  1711	        lease_expires_at: existing.lease_expires_at,
  1712	        task: existing,
  1713	        reason: `Task '${existing.task_id}' (prospect=${existing.prospect_id ?? "none"}) is currently locked by '${existing.owner_agent}' until ${existing.lease_expires_at}.`,
  1714	      };
  1715	    }
  1716	
  1717	    if (!isExpired && existing.owner_agent === ownerAgent) {
  1718	      existing.lease_expires_at = expiresIso;
  1719	      existing.updated_at = nowIso;
  1720	      existing.status = "IN_PROGRESS";
  1721	      if (params.subagent_id !== undefined) existing.subagent_id = params.subagent_id;
  1722	      if (params.stage) existing.stage = params.stage;
  1723	      ledger.agent_states[ownerAgent] = {
  1724	        ...ledger.agent_states[ownerAgent],
  1725	        status: "WORKING",
  1726	        runtime_used: dual.runtime_used,
  1727	        company_scope: companyScope,
  1728	        current_task_id: existing.task_id,
  1729	        current_prospect_id: existing.prospect_id,
  1730	        active_subagents: params.subagent_id ? [params.subagent_id] : ledger.agent_states[ownerAgent].active_subagents,
  1731	        activity_label: `Working on ${existing.title} (${existing.stage})`,
  1732	        last_active_at: nowIso,
  1733	        heartbeat_at: nowIso,
  1734	        heartbeat_provenance: `task_claimed:${existing.task_id}`,
  1735	      };
  1736	      appendOfficeActivityEvent(ledger, {
  1737	        venture_id: companyScope,
  1738	        actor_agent: ownerAgent,
  1739	        worker_profile: params.subagent_id ?? null,
  1740	        task_id: existing.task_id,
  1741	        parent_task_id: existing.parent_task_id,
  1742	        phase: existing.stage,
  1743	        status: "RUNNING",
  1744	        label: `${manifest.display_name} renewed lease on ${existing.title}`,
  1745	        tool_name: "office_claim_task",
  1746	      });
  1747	      writeOfficeLedger(ledger, runtimeRoot);
  1748	      return {
  1749	        ok: true,
  1750	        code: "TASK_LEASE_RENEWED",
  1751	        task: existing,
  1752	        lease_token: existing.lease_token,
  1753	        lease_expires_at: existing.lease_expires_at,
  1754	      };
  1755	    }
  1756	
  1757	    // Expired lease reclamation with fencing!
  1758	    const oldToken = existing.lease_token;
  1759	    const newToken = `lease_${ownerAgent}_${randomUUID().slice(0, 12)}`;
  1760	    if (!existing.fenced_tokens.includes(oldToken)) {
  1761	      existing.fenced_tokens.push(oldToken);
  1762	    }
  1763	    existing.owner_agent = ownerAgent;
  1764	    existing.subagent_id = params.subagent_id ?? null;
  1765	    existing.lease_token = newToken;
  1766	    existing.lease_expires_at = expiresIso;
  1767	    existing.reclaimed_count = (existing.reclaimed_count ?? 0) + 1;
  1768	    existing.status = "IN_PROGRESS";
  1769	    existing.stage = params.stage ?? existing.stage;
  1770	    existing.updated_at = nowIso;
  1771	
  1772	    ledger.agent_states[ownerAgent] = {
  1773	      ...ledger.agent_states[ownerAgent],
  1774	      status: "WORKING",
  1775	      runtime_used: dual.runtime_used,
  1776	      company_scope: companyScope,
  1777	      current_task_id: existing.task_id,
  1778	      current_prospect_id: existing.prospect_id,
  1779	      active_subagents: params.subagent_id ? [params.subagent_id] : [],
  1780	      activity_label: `Reclaimed expired lease on ${existing.title}`,
  1781	      last_active_at: nowIso,
  1782	      heartbeat_at: nowIso,
  1783	      heartbeat_provenance: `task_claimed:${existing.task_id}`,
  1784	    };
  1785	    appendOfficeActivityEvent(ledger, {
  1786	      venture_id: companyScope,
  1787	      actor_agent: ownerAgent,
  1788	      worker_profile: params.subagent_id ?? null,
  1789	      task_id: existing.task_id,
  1790	      parent_task_id: existing.parent_task_id,
  1791	      phase: existing.stage,
  1792	      status: "RUNNING",
  1793	      label: `${manifest.display_name} reclaimed expired lease on ${existing.title} (fenced ${oldToken})`,
  1794	      tool_name: "office_claim_task",
  1795	    });
  1796	    writeOfficeLedger(ledger, runtimeRoot);
  1797	    return {
  1798	      ok: true,
  1799	      code: "TASK_LEASE_RECLAIMED_AFTER_EXPIRY",
  1800	      task: existing,
  1801	      lease_token: newToken,
  1802	      lease_expires_at: expiresIso,
  1803	    };
  1804	  }
  1805	
  1806	  const newToken = `lease_${ownerAgent}_${randomUUID().slice(0, 12)}`;
  1807	  const record: OfficeTaskLeaseRecord = {
  1808	    task_id: taskId,
  1809	    title: String(params.title || "Office Task").slice(0, 240),
  1810	    company_scope: companyScope,
  1811	    prospect_id: prospectId,
  1812	    owner_agent: ownerAgent,
  1813	    subagent_id: params.subagent_id ?? null,
  1814	    parent_task_id: params.parent_task_id ?? null,
  1815	    runtime_used: dual.runtime_used,
  1816	    ...(requestedRuntime ? { execution_runtime: requestedRuntime, execution_attempt_id: "attempt_" + randomUUID().slice(0, 12) } : {}),
  1817	    lease_token: newToken,
  1818	    lease_expires_at: expiresIso,
  1819	    fenced_tokens: [],
  1820	    reclaimed_count: 0,
  1821	    status: "IN_PROGRESS",
  1822	    stage: params.stage ?? "INITIATED",
  1823	    ...(Array.isArray(params.planned_stages) ? {
  1824	      planned_stages: [...new Set(params.planned_stages
  1825	        .filter(x => typeof x === "string" && /^[A-Za-z0-9._-]{1,72}$/.test(x))
  1826	        .slice(0, 24))],
  1827	    } : {}),
  1828	    evidence_hash: null,
  1829	    artifact_urls: [],
  1830	    summary: `Claimed by ${manifest.display_name}`,
  1831	    created_at: nowIso,
  1832	    updated_at: nowIso,
  1833	  };
  1834	
  1835	  ledger.tasks = [...ledger.tasks.slice(-9999), record];
  1836	  ledger.agent_states[ownerAgent] = {
  1837	    ...ledger.agent_states[ownerAgent],
  1838	    status: "WORKING",
  1839	    runtime_used: dual.runtime_used,
  1840	    company_scope: companyScope,
  1841	    current_task_id: record.task_id,
  1842	    current_prospect_id: record.prospect_id,
  1843	    active_subagents: params.subagent_id ? [params.subagent_id] : [],
  1844	    activity_label: `Working on ${record.title}`,
  1845	    last_active_at: nowIso,
  1846	    heartbeat_at: nowIso,
  1847	    heartbeat_provenance: `task_claimed:${record.task_id}`,
  1848	  };
  1849	  appendOfficeActivityEvent(ledger, {
  1850	    venture_id: companyScope,
  1851	    actor_agent: ownerAgent,
  1852	    worker_profile: params.subagent_id ?? null,
  1853	    task_id: record.task_id,
  1854	    parent_task_id: record.parent_task_id,
  1855	    phase: record.stage,
  1856	    status: "RUNNING",
  1857	    label: `${manifest.display_name} started ${record.title}`,
  1858	    tool_name: "office_claim_task",
  1859	  });
  1860	  writeOfficeLedger(ledger, runtimeRoot);
  1861	
  1862	  return {
  1863	    ok: true,
  1864	    code: "TASK_LEASE_CLAIMED",
  1865	    task: record,
  1866	    lease_token: newToken,
  1867	    lease_expires_at: expiresIso,
  1868	  };
  1869	}
  1870	
  1871	/**
  1872	 * Append an observation to the existing authoritative Railway Office Ledger.
  1873	 * Fenced by the current lease, and idempotent per task/attempt/phase.
  1874	 * This is NOT a task completion or permission to contact external customers.
  1875	 */
  1876	export function checkpointOfficeTask(
  1877	  params: {
  1878	    task_id: string;
  1879	    actor_agent: unknown;
  1880	    lease_token: string;
  1881	    attempt_id: string;
  1882	    source_runtime: "railway-openclaw" | "local-openclaw" | "geminx-cloud";
  1883	    phase: string;
  1884	    status: OfficeTaskCheckpointRecord["status"];
  1885	    summary: string;
  1886	    evidence_hash?: string | null;
  1887	    artifact_refs?: string[];
  1888	  },
  1889	  runtimeRoot = geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot()
  1890	): {
  1891	  ok: boolean;
  1892	  code: "OFFICE_CHECKPOINT_RECORDED" | "OFFICE_CHECKPOINT_REPLAY" |
  1893	    "OFFICE_CHECKPOINT_INVALID" | "OFFICE_TASK_NOT_FOUND" |
  1894	    "OFFICE_STALE_LEASE_TOKEN_FENCED" | "OFFICE_TASK_NOT_ACTIVE" |
  1895	    "OFFICE_CHECKPOINT_IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT";
  1896	  checkpoint?: OfficeTaskCheckpointRecord;
  1897	} {
  1898	  const actor = String(params.actor_agent ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
  1899	  const attemptId = String(params.attempt_id ?? "").trim();
  1900	  const taskId = String(params.task_id ?? "").trim();
  1901	  const phase = String(params.phase ?? "").trim();
  1902	  const summary = String(params.summary ?? "").trim();
  1903	  const status = params.status;
  1904	  const runtime = params.source_runtime;
  1905	  const validRuntime = runtime === "railway-openclaw" || runtime === "local-openclaw" || runtime === "geminx-cloud";
  1906	  const validStatus = status === "RUNNING" || status === "COMPLETED_PROVEN" || status === "BLOCKED" || status === "FAILED";
  1907	  const evidenceHash = typeof params.evidence_hash === "string" ? params.evidence_hash.trim() : null;
  1908	  if (!isOfficeAgentId(actor) || !taskId || !/^[a-zA-Z0-9:._-]{1,128}$/.test(attemptId) ||
  1909	      !phase || phase.length > 100 || !summary || summary.length > 600 ||
  1910	      !validStatus || !validRuntime || (status === "COMPLETED_PROVEN" && !evidenceHash)) {
  1911	    return { ok: false, code: "OFFICE_CHECKPOINT_INVALID" };
  1912	  }
  1913	  const ledger = readOfficeLedger(runtimeRoot);
  1914	  const task = ledger.tasks.find(t => t.task_id === taskId);
  1915	  if (!task) return { ok: false, code: "OFFICE_TASK_NOT_FOUND" };
  1916	  if (task.owner_agent !== actor || task.lease_token !== params.lease_token ||
  1917	      task.fenced_tokens.includes(params.lease_token) ||
  1918	      (task.execution_runtime && task.execution_runtime !== runtime)) {
  1919	    return { ok: false, code: "OFFICE_STALE_LEASE_TOKEN_FENCED" };
  1920	  }
  1921	  if (task.status === "COMPLETED" || task.status === "CANCELLED" ||
  1922	      !Number.isFinite(Date.parse(task.lease_expires_at)) ||
  1923	      Date.parse(task.lease_expires_at) <= Date.now()) {
  1924	    return { ok: false, code: "OFFICE_TASK_NOT_ACTIVE" };
  1925	  }
  1926	  const previous = ledger.task_checkpoints.find(c =>
  1927	    c.task_id === taskId && c.attempt_id === attemptId && c.phase === phase && c.status === status);
  1928	  if (previous) {
  1929	    const same = previous.actor_agent === actor && previous.source_runtime === runtime &&
  1930	      previous.status === status && previous.summary === summary &&
  1931	      previous.evidence_hash === evidenceHash &&
  1932	      JSON.stringify(previous.artifact_refs) === JSON.stringify(params.artifact_refs ?? []);
  1933	    return same
  1934	      ? { ok: true, code: "OFFICE_CHECKPOINT_REPLAY", checkpoint: previous }
  1935	      : { ok: false, code: "OFFICE_CHECKPOINT_IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT" };
  1936	  }
  1937	  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  1938	  const checkpoint: OfficeTaskCheckpointRecord = {
  1939	    checkpoint_id: "ckpt_" + randomUUID().slice(0, 12),
  1940	    task_id: taskId,
  1941	    company_scope: task.company_scope,
  1942	    actor_agent: actor,
  1943	    attempt_id: attemptId,
  1944	    source_runtime: runtime,
  1945	    phase,
  1946	    status,
  1947	    summary,
  1948	    evidence_hash: evidenceHash,
  1949	    artifact_refs: Array.isArray(params.artifact_refs)
  1950	      ? params.artifact_refs.filter(a => typeof a === "string").slice(0, 15).map(a => a.slice(0, 800))
  1951	      : [],
  1952	    at: now,
  1953	    sequence: (ledger.task_checkpoints.slice(-1)[0]?.sequence ?? 0) + 1,
  1954	  };
  1955	  ledger.task_checkpoints.push(checkpoint);
  1956	  task.stage = phase;
  1957	  task.summary = summary;
  1958	  task.updated_at = now;
  1959	  if (status === "BLOCKED" || status === "FAILED") {
  1960	    ledger.agent_states[actor].blocked_reason = summary.slice(0, 220);
  1961	  }
  1962	  appendOfficeActivityEvent(ledger, {
  1963	    venture_id: task.company_scope,
  1964	    actor_agent: actor,
  1965	    task_id: taskId,
  1966	    parent_task_id: task.parent_task_id,
  1967	    phase,
  1968	    status: status === "FAILED" ? "FAILED" : status,
  1969	    label: summary,
  1970	    receipt_id: checkpoint.checkpoint_id,
  1971	    tool_name: "office_task_checkpoint",
  1972	  });
  1973	  writeOfficeLedger(ledger, runtimeRoot);
  1974	  return { ok: true, code: "OFFICE_CHECKPOINT_RECORDED", checkpoint };
  1975	}
  1976	
  1977	/** Read continuity without disclosing active/historical fencing secrets. */
  1978	export function getOfficeTaskContinuity(
  1979	  taskId: string,
  1980	  runtimeRoot = geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot()
  1981	) {
  1982	  const ledger = readOfficeLedger(runtimeRoot);
  1983	  const task = ledger.tasks.find(t => t.task_id === taskId);
  1984	  if (!task) return null;
  1985	  const { lease_token: _activeLease, fenced_tokens: _fencedLeases, ...safeTask } = task;
  1986	  return {
  1987	    schema_version: "geminx.office.task-continuity.v1" as const,
  1988	    task_id: taskId,
  1989	    task: safeTask,
  1990	    checkpoints: ledger.task_checkpoints.filter(c => c.task_id === taskId),
  1991	    stage_progress: [...new Set([
  1992	      ...(task.planned_stages ?? []), task.stage,
  1993	      ...ledger.task_checkpoints.filter(c => c.task_id === taskId).map(c => c.phase),
  1994	    ])].map(phase => {
  1995	      const updates = ledger.task_checkpoints.filter(c =>
  1996	        c.task_id === taskId && c.phase === phase);
  1997	      const latest = updates.slice(-1)[0];
  1998	      const proven = updates.some(c => c.status === "COMPLETED_PROVEN");
  1999	      return {
  2000	        phase,
  2001	        status: proven ? "COMPLETED_PROVEN" as const
  2002	          : latest?.status === "FAILED" ? "FAILED" as const
  2003	          : latest?.status === "BLOCKED" ? "BLOCKED" as const
  2004	          : latest?.status === "RUNNING" ? "RUNNING" as const
  2005	          : "PENDING" as const,
  2006	        last_checkpoint_id: latest?.checkpoint_id ?? null,
  2007	        evidence_hash: proven
  2008	          ? [...updates].reverse().find(c => c.status === "COMPLETED_PROVEN")?.evidence_hash ?? null
  2009	          : null,
  2010	      };
  2011	    }),
  2012	    handoffs: ledger.handoffs.filter(h => h.task_id === taskId).map(h => {
  2013	      const { previous_lease_token: _previous, new_lease_token: _next, ...safeHandoff } = h;
  2014	      return safeHandoff;
  2015	    }),
  2016	    receipts: ledger.receipts.filter(r => r.task_id === taskId),
  2017	    recent_activity: ledger.activity_events.filter(e => e.task_id === taskId),
  2018	    lease_active: Date.parse(task.lease_expires_at) > Date.now() &&
  2019	      task.status !== "CANCELLED" && task.status !== "COMPLETED",
  2020	  };
  2021	}
  2022	
  2023	/**
  2024	 * Runtime-specific fenced promotion. INTERNAL ONLY: never expose this method
  2025	 * directly as a caller-controlled HTTP endpoint. A privileged coordinator
  2026	 * must obtain the terminal Railway checkpoint and authenticated Windows/NEO
  2027	 * capability checks independently. A gateway timeout is NOT terminal proof.
  2028	 */
  2029	export function transferNeoOfficeExecutionToLocal(
  2030	  params: {
  2031	    task_id: string;
  2032	    expected_remote_lease_token: string;
  2033	    remote_failure_checkpoint_id: string;
  2034	    local_windows_heartbeat_verified: boolean;
  2035	    local_neo_runtime_verified: boolean;
  2036	    cloud_ledger_bridge_verified: boolean;
  2037	    side_effects_reconciled: boolean;
  2038	  },
  2039	  runtimeRoot = geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot()
  2040	): {
  2041	  ok: boolean;
  2042	  code: "OFFICE_LOCAL_RUNTIME_TRANSFERRED" | "OFFICE_TASK_NOT_FOUND" |
  2043	    "OFFICE_RUNTIME_TRANSFER_UNVERIFIED" | "OFFICE_STALE_LEASE_TOKEN_FENCED" |
  2044	    "OFFICE_REMOTE_FAILURE_PROOF_MISSING" | "OFFICE_TASK_NOT_ACTIVE";
  2045	  task?: OfficeTaskLeaseRecord;
  2046	  new_lease_token?: string;
  2047	  receipt?: OfficeVerifiedReceipt;
  2048	} {
  2049	  if (!params.local_windows_heartbeat_verified || !params.local_neo_runtime_verified ||
  2050	      !params.cloud_ledger_bridge_verified || !params.side_effects_reconciled) {
  2051	    return { ok: false, code: "OFFICE_RUNTIME_TRANSFER_UNVERIFIED" };
  2052	  }
  2053	  const ledger = readOfficeLedger(runtimeRoot);
  2054	  const task = ledger.tasks.find(t => t.task_id === params.task_id);
  2055	  if (!task) return { ok: false, code: "OFFICE_TASK_NOT_FOUND" };
  2056	  if (task.status === "COMPLETED" || task.status === "CANCELLED") {
  2057	    return { ok: false, code: "OFFICE_TASK_NOT_ACTIVE" };
  2058	  }
  2059	  if (task.owner_agent !== "neo" || task.execution_runtime !== "railway-openclaw" ||
  2060	      task.lease_token !== params.expected_remote_lease_token ||
  2061	      task.fenced_tokens.includes(params.expected_remote_lease_token)) {
  2062	    return { ok: false, code: "OFFICE_STALE_LEASE_TOKEN_FENCED" };
  2063	  }
  2064	  const remoteProof = ledger.task_checkpoints.find(c =>
  2065	    c.checkpoint_id === params.remote_failure_checkpoint_id &&
  2066	    c.task_id === task.task_id && c.actor_agent === "neo" &&
  2067	    c.source_runtime === "railway-openclaw" &&
  2068	    (c.status === "FAILED" || c.status === "BLOCKED") &&
  2069	    Boolean(c.evidence_hash));
  2070	  if (!remoteProof) {
  2071	    return { ok: false, code: "OFFICE_REMOTE_FAILURE_PROOF_MISSING" };
  2072	  }
  2073	  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  2074	  task.fenced_tokens.push(task.lease_token);
  2075	  const freshLease = "lease_neo_" + randomUUID().slice(0, 12);
  2076	  task.lease_token = freshLease;
  2077	  task.execution_runtime = "local-openclaw";
  2078	  task.execution_attempt_id = "attempt_local_" + randomUUID().slice(0, 12);
  2079	  task.lease_expires_at = new Date(Date.now() + DEFAULT_LEASE_TTL_MS).toISOString();
  2080	  task.reclaimed_count += 1;
  2081	  task.status = "IN_PROGRESS";
  2082	  task.updated_at = now;
  2083	  task.summary = "Local OpenClaw NEO takeover after verified Railway failure; resume checkpoints";
  2084	  ledger.agent_states.neo = {
  2085	    ...ledger.agent_states.neo,
  2086	    status: "WORKING",
  2087	    runtime_used: "openclaw-gateway",
  2088	    company_scope: task.company_scope,
  2089	    current_task_id: task.task_id,
  2090	    current_prospect_id: task.prospect_id,
  2091	    blocked_reason: null,
  2092	    last_active_at: now,
  2093	  };
  2094	  const evidenceHash = createHash("sha256")
  2095	    .update(remoteProof.checkpoint_id + ":" + remoteProof.evidence_hash + ":" + task.task_id)
  2096	    .digest("hex");
  2097	  const receipt: OfficeVerifiedReceipt = {
  2098	    receipt_id: "rcpt_failover_" + randomUUID().slice(0, 12),
  2099	    agent_id: "neo",
  2100	    subagent_id: null,
  2101	    company_scope: task.company_scope,
  2102	    task_id: task.task_id,
  2103	    prospect_id: task.prospect_id,
  2104	    tool_or_stage: "RUNTIME_FAILOVER_RAILWAY_TO_LOCAL",
  2105	    runtime_used: "openclaw-gateway",
  2106	    evidence_hash: evidenceHash,
  2107	    artifact_urls: [],
  2108	    summary: "Previous Railway lease fenced; local OpenClaw NEO owns next attempt.",
  2109	    at: now,
  2110	  };
  2111	  ledger.receipts.push(receipt);
  2112	  appendOfficeActivityEvent(ledger, {
  2113	    venture_id: task.company_scope,
  2114	    actor_agent: "neo",
  2115	    task_id: task.task_id,
  2116	    parent_task_id: task.parent_task_id,
  2117	    phase: "RUNTIME_FAILOVER",
  2118	    status: "ACCEPTED",
  2119	    label: "Confirmed Railway OpenClaw failure, exclusive local takeover prepared",
  2120	    receipt_id: receipt.receipt_id,
  2121	    tool_name: "office_neo_failover",
  2122	  });
  2123	  writeOfficeLedger(ledger, runtimeRoot);
  2124	  return { ok: true, code: "OFFICE_LOCAL_RUNTIME_TRANSFERRED",
  2125	    task, new_lease_token: freshLease, receipt };
  2126	}
  2127	
  2128	export function completeOfficeTask(
  2129	  params: {
  2130	    task_id: string;
  2131	    owner_agent: unknown;
  2132	    lease_token: string;
  2133	    status?: "COMPLETED" | "BLOCKED";
  2134	    evidence_hash?: string | null;
  2135	    artifact_urls?: string[];
  2136	    summary: string;
  2137	  },
  2138	  runtimeRoot = geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot()
  2139	): {
  2140	  ok: boolean;
  2141	  code:
  2142	    | "TASK_COMPLETED_VERIFIED"
  2143	    | "TASK_MARKED_BLOCKED"
  2144	    | "OFFICE_TASK_NOT_FOUND"
  2145	    | "OFFICE_TASK_CANCELLED_NO_RESURRECT"
  2146	    | "OFFICE_STALE_LEASE_TOKEN_FENCED"
  2147	    | "OFFICE_VERIFICATION_EVIDENCE_REQUIRED";
  2148	  task?: OfficeTaskLeaseRecord;
  2149	  receipt?: OfficeVerifiedReceipt;
  2150	  reason?: string;
  2151	} {
  2152	  const ownerAgent = normalizeOfficeAgentId(params.owner_agent, "neo");
  2153	  const ledger = readOfficeLedger(runtimeRoot);
  2154	  const task = ledger.tasks.find((t) => t.task_id === params.task_id);
  2155	  if (!task) {
  2156	    return {
  2157	      ok: false,
  2158	      code: "OFFICE_TASK_NOT_FOUND",
  2159	      reason: `Task '${params.task_id}' does not exist in the Office Floor ledger.`,
  2160	    };
  2161	  }
  2162	
  2163	  if (task.status === "CANCELLED") {
  2164	    return {
  2165	      ok: false,
  2166	      code: "OFFICE_TASK_CANCELLED_NO_RESURRECT",
  2167	      task,
  2168	      reason: `Task '${params.task_id}' was cancelled and cannot be completed or resurrected.`,
  2169	    };
  2170	  }
  2171	
  2172	  if (
  2173	    task.fenced_tokens.includes(params.lease_token) ||
  2174	    task.lease_token !== params.lease_token ||
  2175	    task.owner_agent !== ownerAgent
  2176	  ) {
  2177	    return {
  2178	      ok: false,
  2179	      code: "OFFICE_STALE_LEASE_TOKEN_FENCED",
  2180	      task,
  2181	      reason: `Write rejected: lease_token '${params.lease_token}' for agent '${ownerAgent}' is stale or fenced. Current owner is '${task.owner_agent}' with token '${task.lease_token}'.`,
  2182	    };
  2183	  }
  2184	
  2185	  const targetStatus = params.status ?? "COMPLETED";
  2186	  const evidenceHash = String(params.evidence_hash ?? "").trim();
  2187	  if (targetStatus === "COMPLETED" && !evidenceHash) {
  2188	    return {
  2189	      ok: false,
  2190	      code: "OFFICE_VERIFICATION_EVIDENCE_REQUIRED",
  2191	      task,
  2192	      reason: "Cannot mark an Office task COMPLETED without a non-empty verification evidence_hash.",
  2193	    };
  2194	  }
  2195	
  2196	  const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
  2197	  task.status = targetStatus;
  2198	  task.evidence_hash = evidenceHash || null;
  2199	  if (Array.isArray(params.artifact_urls)) {
  2200	    task.artifact_urls = [...new Set([...task.artifact_urls, ...params.artifact_urls])];
  2201	  }
  2202	  task.summary = String(params.summary || task.summary).slice(0, 600);
  2203	  task.updated_at = nowIso;
  2204	
  2205	  const receipt: OfficeVerifiedReceipt = {
  2206	    receipt_id: `rcpt_${randomUUID().slice(0, 12)}`,
  2207	    agent_id: ownerAgent,
  2208	    subagent_id: task.subagent_id,
  2209	    company_scope: task.company_scope,
  2210	    task_id: task.task_id,
  2211	    prospect_id: task.prospect_id,
  2212	    tool_or_stage: task.stage,
  2213	    runtime_used: task.runtime_used,
  2214	    evidence_hash: evidenceHash || "blocked_boundary",
  2215	    artifact_urls: task.artifact_urls,
  2216	    summary: task.summary,
  2217	    at: nowIso,
  2218	  };
  2219	
  2220	  ledger.receipts = [...ledger.receipts.slice(-9999), receipt];
  2221	  ledger.agent_states[ownerAgent] = {
  2222	    ...ledger.agent_states[ownerAgent],
  2223	    status: targetStatus === "BLOCKED" ? "BLOCKED" : "IDLE",
  2224	    current_task_id: targetStatus === "COMPLETED" ? null : task.task_id,
  2225	    active_subagents: [],
  2226	    activity_label: task.summary.slice(0, 140),
  2227	    blocked_reason: targetStatus === "BLOCKED" ? task.summary.slice(0, 180) : null,
  2228	    last_receipt_hash: receipt.evidence_hash,
  2229	    last_active_at: nowIso,
  2230	    heartbeat_at: nowIso,
  2231	    heartbeat_provenance: `task_completed:${task.task_id}`,
  2232	  };
  2233	
  2234	  appendOfficeActivityEvent(ledger, {
  2235	    venture_id: task.company_scope,
  2236	    actor_agent: ownerAgent,
  2237	    worker_profile: task.subagent_id,
  2238	    task_id: task.task_id,
  2239	    parent_task_id: task.parent_task_id,
  2240	    phase: task.stage,
  2241	    status: targetStatus === "BLOCKED" ? "BLOCKED" : "COMPLETED_PROVEN",
  2242	    label: task.summary,
  2243	    receipt_id: receipt.receipt_id,
  2244	    error_code: targetStatus === "BLOCKED" ? "TASK_BLOCKED" : null,
  2245	  });
  2246	
  2247	  writeOfficeLedger(ledger, runtimeRoot);
  2248	  return {
  2249	    ok: true,
  2250	    code: targetStatus === "BLOCKED" ? "TASK_MARKED_BLOCKED" : "TASK_COMPLETED_VERIFIED",
  2251	    task,
  2252	    receipt,
  2253	  };
  2254	}
  2255	
  2256	export function handoffOfficeTask(
  2257	  params: {
  2258	    task_id: string;
  2259	    from_agent: unknown;
  2260	    to_agent: unknown;
  2261	    lease_token: string;
  2262	    stage: string;
  2263	    artifact_refs?: string[];
  2264	    evidence_hash: string;
  2265	    summary: string;
  2266	    lease_ttl_ms?: number;
  2267	  },
  2268	  runtimeRoot = geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot()
  2269	): {
  2270	  ok: boolean;
  2271	  code:
  2272	    | "OFFICE_HANDOFF_COMMITTED"
  2273	    | "OFFICE_TASK_NOT_FOUND"
  2274	    | "OFFICE_STALE_LEASE_TOKEN_FENCED"
  2275	    | "OFFICE_VERIFICATION_EVIDENCE_REQUIRED"
  2276	    | "OFFICE_CROSS_COMPANY_ISOLATION_VIOLATION";
  2277	  handoff?: OfficeHandoffPacket;
  2278	  task?: OfficeTaskLeaseRecord;
  2279	  reason?: string;
  2280	} {
  2281	  const fromAgent = normalizeOfficeAgentId(params.from_agent, "neo");
  2282	  const toAgent = normalizeOfficeAgentId(params.to_agent, "brian");
  2283	  const evidenceHash = String(params.evidence_hash ?? "").trim();
  2284	  if (!evidenceHash) {
  2285	    return {
  2286	      ok: false,
  2287	      code: "OFFICE_VERIFICATION_EVIDENCE_REQUIRED",
  2288	      reason: "Fenced handoff requires a non-empty evidence_hash proving the upstream stage completed.",
  2289	    };
  2290	  }
  2291	
  2292	  const ledger = readOfficeLedger(runtimeRoot);
  2293	  const task = ledger.tasks.find((t) => t.task_id === params.task_id);
  2294	  if (!task) {
  2295	    return {
  2296	      ok: false,
  2297	      code: "OFFICE_TASK_NOT_FOUND",
  2298	      reason: `Task '${params.task_id}' not found for handoff.`,
  2299	    };
  2300	  }
  2301	
  2302	  const toManifest = OFFICE_AGENT_MANIFESTS[toAgent];
  2303	  if (
  2304	    task.company_scope !== "holding" &&
  2305	    !toManifest.readable_company_scopes.includes("holding") &&
  2306	    !toManifest.readable_company_scopes.includes(task.company_scope)
  2307	  ) {
  2308	    return {
  2309	      ok: false,
  2310	      code: "OFFICE_CROSS_COMPANY_ISOLATION_VIOLATION",
  2311	      reason: `Cannot hand off task in company_scope='${task.company_scope}' to '${toManifest.display_name}' (${toAgent}).`,
  2312	    };
  2313	  }
  2314	
  2315	  if (
  2316	    task.fenced_tokens.includes(params.lease_token) ||
  2317	    task.lease_token !== params.lease_token ||
  2318	    task.owner_agent !== fromAgent
  2319	  ) {
  2320	    return {
  2321	      ok: false,
  2322	      code: "OFFICE_STALE_LEASE_TOKEN_FENCED",
  2323	      task,
  2324	      reason: `Handoff rejected: lease_token '${params.lease_token}' from '${fromAgent}' is stale or fenced. Current owner is '${task.owner_agent}'.`,
  2325	    };
  2326	  }
  2327	
  2328	  const nowMs = Date.now();
  2329	  const nowIso = new Date(nowMs).toISOString();
  2330	  const ttlMs = Math.max(5_000, Math.min(3_600_000, params.lease_ttl_ms ?? DEFAULT_LEASE_TTL_MS));
  2331	  const newToken = `lease_${toAgent}_${randomUUID().slice(0, 12)}`;
  2332	
  2333	  task.fenced_tokens.push(params.lease_token);
  2334	  task.owner_agent = toAgent;
  2335	  task.subagent_id = null;
  2336	  task.execution_runtime = undefined;
  2337	  task.execution_attempt_id = undefined;
  2338	  task.lease_token = newToken;
  2339	  task.lease_expires_at = new Date(nowMs + ttlMs).toISOString();
  2340	  task.stage = params.stage;
  2341	  task.status = "HANDED_OFF";
  2342	  task.evidence_hash = evidenceHash;
  2343	  if (Array.isArray(params.artifact_refs)) {
  2344	    task.artifact_urls = [...new Set([...task.artifact_urls, ...params.artifact_refs])];
  2345	  }
  2346	  task.summary = String(params.summary).slice(0, 600);
  2347	  task.updated_at = nowIso;
  2348	
  2349	  const handoff: OfficeHandoffPacket = {
  2350	    handoff_id: `hoff_${randomUUID().slice(0, 12)}`,
  2351	    task_id: task.task_id,
  2352	    company_scope: task.company_scope,
  2353	    prospect_id: task.prospect_id,
  2354	    from_agent: fromAgent,
  2355	    to_agent: toAgent,
  2356	    previous_lease_token: params.lease_token,
  2357	    new_lease_token: newToken,
  2358	    stage: params.stage,
  2359	    artifact_refs: task.artifact_urls,
  2360	    evidence_hash: evidenceHash,
  2361	    summary: task.summary,
  2362	    created_at: nowIso,
  2363	  };
  2364	
  2365	  const receipt: OfficeVerifiedReceipt = {
  2366	    receipt_id: `rcpt_${randomUUID().slice(0, 12)}`,
  2367	    agent_id: fromAgent,
  2368	    subagent_id: null,
  2369	    company_scope: task.company_scope,
  2370	    task_id: task.task_id,
  2371	    prospect_id: task.prospect_id,
  2372	    tool_or_stage: `HANDOFF:${params.stage}->${toAgent}`,
  2373	    runtime_used: task.runtime_used,
  2374	    evidence_hash: evidenceHash,
  2375	    artifact_urls: task.artifact_urls,
  2376	    summary: task.summary,
  2377	    at: nowIso,
  2378	  };
  2379	
  2380	  ledger.handoffs = [...ledger.handoffs.slice(-9999), handoff];
  2381	  ledger.receipts = [...ledger.receipts.slice(-9999), receipt];
  2382	
  2383	  if (task.prospect_id) {
  2384	    const lock = ledger.prospect_locks.find(
  2385	      (l) => l.prospect_id === task.prospect_id && l.company_scope === task.company_scope
  2386	    );
  2387	    if (lock) {
  2388	      lock.fenced_tokens.push(lock.lease_token);
  2389	      lock.owner_agent = toAgent;
  2390	      lock.lease_token = newToken;
  2391	      lock.lease_expires_at = task.lease_expires_at;
  2392	      lock.updated_at = nowIso;
  2393	    }
  2394	  }
  2395	
  2396	  ledger.agent_states[fromAgent] = {
  2397	    ...ledger.agent_states[fromAgent],
  2398	    status: "IDLE",
  2399	    current_task_id: null,
  2400	    active_subagents: [],
  2401	    activity_label: `Handed off ${params.stage} to ${toManifest.display_name}`,
  2402	    last_receipt_hash: evidenceHash,
  2403	    last_active_at: nowIso,
  2404	  };
  2405	  ledger.agent_states[toAgent] = {
  2406	    ...ledger.agent_states[toAgent],
  2407	    status: "WAITING_HANDOFF",
  2408	    company_scope: task.company_scope,
  2409	    current_task_id: task.task_id,
  2410	    current_prospect_id: task.prospect_id,
  2411	    activity_label: `Received handoff (${params.stage}) from ${OFFICE_AGENT_MANIFESTS[fromAgent].display_name}`,
  2412	    last_active_at: nowIso,
  2413	    heartbeat_at: nowIso,
  2414	    heartbeat_provenance: `task_handoff:${task.task_id}`,
  2415	  };
  2416	
  2417	  appendOfficeActivityEvent(ledger, {
  2418	    venture_id: task.company_scope,
  2419	    actor_agent: fromAgent,
  2420	    task_id: task.task_id,
  2421	    parent_task_id: task.parent_task_id,
  2422	    phase: `HANDOFF_${params.stage}`,
  2423	    status: "COMPLETED_PROVEN",
  2424	    label: task.summary,
  2425	    tool_name: "office_handoff",
  2426	    receipt_id: receipt.receipt_id,
  2427	  });
  2428	
  2429	  writeOfficeLedger(ledger, runtimeRoot);
  2430	  return {
  2431	    ok: true,
  2432	    code: "OFFICE_HANDOFF_COMMITTED",
  2433	    handoff,
  2434	    task,
  2435	  };
  2436	}
  2437	
  2438	export function executeOfficeSubagentDelegation(
  2439	  params: {
  2440	    parent_agent: unknown;
  2441	    subagent_id: string;
  2442	    parent_task_id?: string | null;
  2443	    company_scope?: string;
  2444	    target_company_scope?: string;
  2445	    owned_surface?: string;
  2446	    objective: string;
  2447	    artifact_ids?: string[];
  2448	    simulate_failure?: boolean;
  2449	  },
  2450	  runtimeRoot = geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot()
  2451	): {
  2452	  ok: boolean;
  2453	  code:
  2454	    | "SUBAGENT_DELEGATION_COMPLETED"
  2455	    | "SUBAGENT_CHILD_FAILURE_HANDLED_BY_PARENT"
  2456	    | "OFFICE_INVALID_SUBAGENT_FOR_PARENT"
  2457	    | "OFFICE_CROSS_COMPANY_ISOLATION_VIOLATION";
  2458	  delegation_receipt?: OfficeDelegationReceipt;
  2459	  parent_recovery_receipt?: OfficeVerifiedReceipt;
  2460	  reason?: string;
  2461	} {
  2462	  const parentAgent = normalizeOfficeAgentId(params.parent_agent, "neo");
  2463	  const parentManifest = OFFICE_AGENT_MANIFESTS[parentAgent];
  2464	  const subagentId = String(params.subagent_id ?? "").trim();
  2465	  const profile = parentManifest.subagents.find((s) => s.id === subagentId);
  2466	
  2467	  if (!profile) {
  2468	    return {
  2469	      ok: false,
  2470	      code: "OFFICE_INVALID_SUBAGENT_FOR_PARENT",
  2471	      reason: `Sub-agent '${subagentId}' is not registered under parent department '${parentManifest.display_name}' (${parentAgent}). Valid sub-agents: [${parentManifest.subagents.map((s) => s.id).join(", ") || "none"}].`,
  2472	    };
  2473	  }
  2474	
  2475	  const activeScope = normalizeCompanyScope(params.company_scope, parentAgent);
  2476	  const targetScope = params.target_company_scope
  2477	    ? normalizeCompanyScope(params.target_company_scope, parentAgent)
  2478	    : activeScope;
  2479	
  2480	  if (
  2481	    targetScope !== "holding" &&
  2482	    !parentManifest.readable_company_scopes.includes("holding") &&
  2483	    !parentManifest.readable_company_scopes.includes(targetScope)
  2484	  ) {
  2485	    return {
  2486	      ok: false,
  2487	      code: "OFFICE_CROSS_COMPANY_ISOLATION_VIOLATION",
  2488	      reason: `Sub-agent '${subagentId}' (parent '${parentManifest.display_name}') is scoped to [${parentManifest.readable_company_scopes.join(", ")}] and rejected cross-venture access to '${targetScope}'.`,
  2489	    };
  2490	  }
  2491	
  2492	  const startedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  2493	  const parentTaskId = String(params.parent_task_id ?? "").trim() || `ptask_${parentAgent}_${randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;
  2494	  const childTaskId = `ctask_${subagentId}_${randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;
  2495	  const ownedSurface = String(params.owned_surface ?? `surface:${targetScope}:${subagentId}`).slice(0, 160);
  2496	  const ledger = readOfficeLedger(runtimeRoot);
  2497	
  2498	  if (params.simulate_failure === true) {
  2499	    const finishedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  2500	    const failedDelegation: OfficeDelegationReceipt = {
  2501	      schema_version: "geminx.office.delegation.v1",
  2502	      parent_task_id: parentTaskId,
  2503	      child_task_id: childTaskId,
  2504	      actor_agent: parentAgent,
  2505	      worker_profile: subagentId,
  2506	      company_scope: targetScope,
  2507	      owned_surface: ownedSurface,
  2508	      status: "FAILED",
  2509	      started_at: startedAt,
  2510	      finished_at: finishedAt,
  2511	      tool_receipt_ids: [],
  2512	      artifact_ids: [],
  2513	      source_commit_sha: null,
  2514	      cost_usd: 0.001,
  2515	      verified_outcome: {
  2516	        success: false,
  2517	        summary: `Child worker '${subagentId}' reported bounded task failure on '${ownedSurface}'; parent '${parentManifest.display_name}' caught failure and reclaimed surface.`,
  2518	      },
  2519	    };
  2520	    const parentRecoveryReceipt: OfficeVerifiedReceipt = {
  2521	      receipt_id: `rcpt_recover_${randomUUID().slice(0, 10)}`,
  2522	      agent_id: parentAgent,
  2523	      subagent_id: subagentId,
  2524	      company_scope: targetScope,
  2525	      task_id: parentTaskId,
  2526	      prospect_id: null,
  2527	      tool_or_stage: `PARENT_CHILD_FAILURE_RECOVERY:${subagentId}`,
  2528	      runtime_used: "geminx-cloud",
  2529	      evidence_hash: createHash("sha256").update(`${childTaskId}:failed_handled`).digest("hex").slice(0, 16),
  2530	      artifact_urls: [],
  2531	      summary: `${parentManifest.display_name} observed failed child '${subagentId}' (${childTaskId}) and safely handled fallback without orphan state.`,
  2532	      at: finishedAt,
  2533	    };
  2534	    ledger.delegation_receipts = [...ledger.delegation_receipts.slice(-9999), failedDelegation];
  2535	    ledger.receipts = [...ledger.receipts.slice(-9999), parentRecoveryReceipt];
  2536	    appendOfficeActivityEvent(ledger, {
  2537	      venture_id: targetScope,
  2538	      actor_agent: parentAgent,
  2539	      worker_profile: subagentId,
  2540	      task_id: childTaskId,
  2541	      parent_task_id: parentTaskId,
  2542	      phase: "CHILD_DELEGATION_FAILED_HANDLED",
  2543	      status: "FAILED",
  2544	      label: failedDelegation.verified_outcome.summary,
  2545	      receipt_id: parentRecoveryReceipt.receipt_id,
  2546	      error_code: "CHILD_WORKER_BOUNDED_FAILURE",
  2547	      cost_usd: 0.001,
  2548	    });
  2549	    writeOfficeLedger(ledger, runtimeRoot);
  2550	    return {
  2551	      ok: true,
  2552	      code: "SUBAGENT_CHILD_FAILURE_HANDLED_BY_PARENT",
  2553	      delegation_receipt: failedDelegation,
  2554	      parent_recovery_receipt: parentRecoveryReceipt,
  2555	    };
  2556	  }
  2557	
  2558	  const finishedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  2559	  const toolReceiptId = `trcpt_${subagentId}_${randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;
  2560	  const delegation: OfficeDelegationReceipt = {
  2561	    schema_version: "geminx.office.delegation.v1",
  2562	    parent_task_id: parentTaskId,
  2563	    child_task_id: childTaskId,
  2564	    actor_agent: parentAgent,
  2565	    worker_profile: subagentId,
  2566	    company_scope: targetScope,
  2567	    owned_surface: ownedSurface,
  2568	    status: "COMPLETED_PROVEN",
  2569	    started_at: startedAt,
  2570	    finished_at: finishedAt,
  2571	    tool_receipt_ids: [toolReceiptId],
  2572	    artifact_ids: Array.isArray(params.artifact_ids) ? params.artifact_ids : [],
  2573	    source_commit_sha: null,
  2574	    cost_usd: 0.002,
  2575	    verified_outcome: {
  2576	      success: true,
  2577	      summary: `${subagentId} (${profile.role}) completed: ${String(params.objective).slice(0, 220)}`,
  2578	    },
  2579	  };
  2580	  ledger.delegation_receipts = [...ledger.delegation_receipts.slice(-9999), delegation];
  2581	  ledger.agent_states[parentAgent] = {
  2582	    ...ledger.agent_states[parentAgent],
  2583	    active_subagents: [...new Set([...(ledger.agent_states[parentAgent]?.active_subagents ?? []), subagentId])].slice(-4),
  2584	    activity_label: `${parentManifest.display_name} -> ${subagentId}: ${String(params.objective).slice(0, 110)}`,
  2585	    last_active_at: finishedAt,
  2586	  };
  2587	  appendOfficeActivityEvent(ledger, {
  2588	    venture_id: targetScope,
  2589	    actor_agent: parentAgent,
  2590	    worker_profile: subagentId,
  2591	    task_id: childTaskId,
  2592	    parent_task_id: parentTaskId,
  2593	    phase: "SUBAGENT_DELEGATION",
  2594	    status: "COMPLETED_PROVEN",
  2595	    label: delegation.verified_outcome.summary,
  2596	    tool_name: "office_subagent_spawn",
  2597	    receipt_id: toolReceiptId,
  2598	    cost_usd: 0.002,
  2599	  });
  2600	  writeOfficeLedger(ledger, runtimeRoot);
  2601	  return {
  2602	    ok: true,
  2603	    code: "SUBAGENT_DELEGATION_COMPLETED",
  2604	    delegation_receipt: delegation,
  2605	  };
  2606	}
  2607	
  2608	export function cancelOfficeTask(
  2609	  params: {
  2610	    task_id: string;
  2611	    cancelled_by?: string;
  2612	    reason?: string;
  2613	  },
  2614	  runtimeRoot = geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot()
  2615	): {
  2616	  ok: boolean;
  2617	  code: "TASK_CANCELLED" | "OFFICE_TASK_NOT_FOUND";
  2618	  task?: OfficeTaskLeaseRecord;
  2619	} {
  2620	  const ledger = readOfficeLedger(runtimeRoot);
  2621	  const task = ledger.tasks.find((t) => t.task_id === params.task_id);
  2622	  if (!task) {
  2623	    return { ok: false, code: "OFFICE_TASK_NOT_FOUND" };
  2624	  }
  2625	  const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
  2626	  task.fenced_tokens.push(task.lease_token);
  2627	  task.lease_token = `fenced_cancelled_${randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;
  2628	  (task as { status: string }).status = "CANCELLED";
  2629	  task.summary = `CANCELLED by ${params.cancelled_by ?? "founder"}: ${params.reason ?? "Operator stop requested"}`;
  2630	  task.updated_at = nowIso;
  2631	  if (ledger.agent_states[task.owner_agent]?.current_task_id === task.task_id) {
  2632	    ledger.agent_states[task.owner_agent].status = "IDLE";
  2633	    ledger.agent_states[task.owner_agent].current_task_id = null;
  2634	    ledger.agent_states[task.owner_agent].active_subagents = [];
  2635	    ledger.agent_states[task.owner_agent].activity_label = `Stopped task ${task.task_id}`;
  2636	  }
  2637	  appendOfficeActivityEvent(ledger, {
  2638	    venture_id: task.company_scope,
  2639	    actor_agent: task.owner_agent,
  2640	    task_id: task.task_id,
  2641	    phase: "TASK_CANCELLED",
  2642	    status: "CANCELLED",
  2643	    label: task.summary,
  2644	  });
  2645	  writeOfficeLedger(ledger, runtimeRoot);
  2646	  return { ok: true, code: "TASK_CANCELLED", task };
  2647	}
  2648	
  2649	export async function decideOfficeApproval(
  2650	  params: {
  2651	    approval_id: string;
  2652	    decision: "APPROVED" | "BLOCKED";
  2653	    note?: string;
  2654	    certify_compliance?: boolean;
  2655	  },
  2656	  runtimeRoot = geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot()
  2657	): Promise<{ approval: OfficePendingApprovalRecord; send_result?: Record<string, unknown> } | null> {
  2658	  const ledger = readOfficeLedger(runtimeRoot);
  2659	  const item = ledger.pending_approvals.find((a) => a.approval_id === params.approval_id);
  2660	  if (!item) return null;
  2661	
  2662	  // Replay & tampering defense: if the gate is already decided, fail closed
  2663	  if (item.status === "APPROVED" || item.status === "BLOCKED") {
  2664	    return {
  2665	      approval: item,
  2666	      send_result: {
  2667	        executed: false,
  2668	        error: "APPROVAL_GATE_ALREADY_DECIDED",
  2669	      },
  2670	    };
  2671	  }
  2672	
  2673	  item.status = params.decision;
  2674	  if (params.note) {
  2675	    item.summary = `${item.summary} [${params.decision}: ${params.note}]`;
  2676	  }
  2677	  appendOfficeActivityEvent(ledger, {
  2678	    venture_id: item.company_scope,
  2679	    actor_agent: item.requested_by,
  2680	    phase: `APPROVAL_${params.decision}`,
  2681	    status: params.decision === "APPROVED" ? "COMPLETED_PROVEN" : "BLOCKED",

  2683	  });

  2686
