  3116	
  3117	        sendResult = {
  3118	          executed: true,
  3119	          provider: "metricool",
  3120	          metricool_post_id: receipt.metricool_post_id,
  3121	          durable_posting_id: receipt.durable_posting_id,
  3122	          state: receipt.state,
  3123	          scheduled_at: receipt.timestamp,
  3124	        };
  3125	
  3126	        appendOfficeActivityEvent(ledger, {
  3127	          venture_id: item.company_scope,
  3128	          actor_agent: "sophie",
  3129	          phase: "SOCIAL_POST_SCHEDULED",
  3130	          status: "COMPLETED_PROVEN",
  3131	          label: `Metricool accepted approved TikTok & YouTube publication schedule (post: ${receipt.metricool_post_id ?? receipt.durable_posting_id})`,
  3132	        });
  3133	      } else {
  3134	        item.status = "PENDING";
  3135	        pkg.metricool_publish_receipt = receipt;
  3136	        const isAuthError = Boolean(
  3137	          receipt.error?.includes("METRICOOL_CREDENTIALS") ||
  3138	          receipt.error?.includes("METRICOOL_API_HTTP_401") ||
  3139	          receipt.error?.includes("METRICOOL_API_HTTP_403")
  3140	        );
  3141	        const blockedReceipt: SophieBlockedPublishReceipt = {
  3142	          package_id: pkg.package_id,
  3143	          task_id: null,
  3144	          artifact_sha256: pkg.creative_artifact_sha256,
  3145	          current_state: "BLOCKED_PUBLISH",
  3146	          blocked_code: receipt.error ?? "METRICOOL_PUBLISHING_FAILED",
  3147	          blocker_owner: isAuthError ? "founder" : "provider",
  3148	          missing_prerequisites: [receipt.error ?? "valid_metricool_oauth_connection"],
  3149	          next_action_label: isAuthError
  3150	            ? "Authorise Metricool OAuth via /api/v1/auth/metricool/connect"
  3151	            : "Inspect Metricool provider error",
  3152	          next_action_url: isAuthError ? "/api/v1/auth/metricool/connect" : null,
  3153	          retryable: !receipt.error?.includes("PLAN_LIMITATION"),
  3154	          checked_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  3155	          correlation_id: item.approval_id,
  3156	          provider_status: receipt.state,
  3157	        };
  3158	        pkg.blocked_receipt = blockedReceipt;
  3159	        sendResult = {
  3160	          executed: false,
  3161	          error: receipt.error ?? "METRICOOL_PUBLISHING_FAILED",
  3162	          durable_posting_id: receipt.durable_posting_id,
  3163	          blocked_receipt: blockedReceipt,
  3164	        };
  3165	        appendOfficeActivityEvent(ledger, {
  3166	          venture_id: item.company_scope,
  3167	          actor_agent: "sophie",
  3168	          phase: "SOCIAL_POST_FAILED",
  3169	          status: "BLOCKED",
  3170	          label: `Metricool post dispatch failed: ${sendResult.error}`,
  3171	        });
  3172	      }
  3173	    }
  3174	  }
  3175	
  3176	  writeOfficeLedger(ledger, runtimeRoot);
  3177	  return { approval: item, send_result: sendResult };
  3178	}
  3179	
  3180	export function escalateToAg(
  3181	  params: {
  3182	    reporting_agent: unknown;
  3183	    company_scope?: string;
  3184	    task_id?: string | null;
  3185	    failing_tool: string;
  3186	    error_code: string;
  3187	    target_resource: string;
  3188	    evidence?: string[];
  3189	    recommended_action?: string;
  3190	  },
  3191	  runtimeRoot = geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot()
  3192	): OfficeIncidentRecord {
  3193	  const reportingAgent = normalizeOfficeAgentId(params.reporting_agent, "neo");
  3194	  const companyScope = normalizeCompanyScope(params.company_scope, reportingAgent);
  3195	  const ledger = readOfficeLedger(runtimeRoot);
  3196	  const errorCode = String(params.error_code || "RUNTIME_FAULT").slice(0, 120);
  3197	  const resource = String(params.target_resource || "unspecified").slice(0, 240);
  3198	  // Idempotent incident identity: a failed retry of the same conversation/run
  3199	  // cannot create another AG task or bogus "repair started" notification.
  3200	  const alreadyOpen = ledger.incidents.find((i) =>
  3201	    i.status !== "RESOLVED_VERIFIED" &&
  3202	    i.reporting_agent === reportingAgent &&
  3203	    i.company_scope === companyScope &&
  3204	    i.error_code === errorCode &&
  3205	    i.target_resource === resource &&
  3206	    (params.task_id ? i.task_id === params.task_id : !i.task_id)
  3207	  );
  3208	  if (alreadyOpen) return alreadyOpen;
  3209	  const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
  3210	  const incident: OfficeIncidentRecord = {
  3211	    incident_id: `inc_ag_${randomUUID().slice(0, 12)}`,
  3212	    reporting_agent: reportingAgent,
  3213	    assigned_to: "ag",
  3214	    company_scope: companyScope,
  3215	    task_id: params.task_id ?? null,
  3216	    failing_tool: String(params.failing_tool || "unknown_tool").slice(0, 120),
  3217	    error_code: String(params.error_code || "RUNTIME_FAULT").slice(0, 120),
  3218	    target_resource: String(params.target_resource || "unspecified").slice(0, 240),
  3219	    evidence: Array.isArray(params.evidence)
  3220	      ? params.evidence.map((e) => String(e).slice(0, 500)).slice(0, 12)
  3221	      : [],
  3222	    recommended_action: String(
  3223	      params.recommended_action ||
  3224	        "AG Sentinel to inspect backend route/tool, apply surgical fix, and verify from live UI."
  3225	    ).slice(0, 400),
  3226	    status: "OPEN_ASSIGNED_TO_AG",
  3227	    lease_token: null,
  3228	    lease_expires_at: null,
  3229	    resolution_receipt: null,
  3230	    created_at: nowIso,
  3231	    updated_at: nowIso,
  3232	  };
  3233	
  3234	  ledger.incidents = [...ledger.incidents.slice(-9999), incident];
  3235	  ledger.agent_states[reportingAgent] = {
  3236	    ...ledger.agent_states[reportingAgent],
  3237	    status: "ESCALATED_TO_AG",
  3238	    activity_label: `Escalated ${incident.error_code} on ${incident.failing_tool} to AG Sentinel`,
  3239	    blocked_reason: `${incident.error_code}: ${incident.target_resource}`,
  3240	    last_active_at: nowIso,
  3241	  };
  3242	  ledger.agent_states.ag = {
  3243	    ...ledger.agent_states.ag,
  3244	    // Incident creation is a QUEUE receipt, not proof AG claimed or executed.
  3245	    status: ledger.agent_states.ag.status,
  3246	    activity_label: `Queued incident ${incident.incident_id} from ${OFFICE_AGENT_MANIFESTS[reportingAgent].display_name} for AG`,
  3247	    last_active_at: nowIso,
  3248	  };
  3249	  appendOfficeActivityEvent(ledger, {
  3250	    venture_id: companyScope,
  3251	    actor_agent: reportingAgent,
  3252	    task_id: incident.task_id,
  3253	    phase: "ESCALATED_TO_AG",
  3254	    status: "BLOCKED",
  3255	    label: `${OFFICE_AGENT_MANIFESTS[reportingAgent].display_name} escalated ${incident.error_code} (${incident.failing_tool}) to AG Sentinel`,
  3256	    tool_name: "escalate_to_ag",
  3257	    error_code: incident.error_code,
  3258	  });
  3259	  writeOfficeLedger(ledger, runtimeRoot);
  3260	
  3261	  // Also enqueue a structured message to AG in CommsStore so AG sees it in the canonical inbox
  3262	  try {
  3263	    const comms = new CommsStore();
  3264	    comms.saveMessage({
  3265	      message_id: `msg_${incident.incident_id}`,
  3266	      correlation_id: incident.incident_id,
  3267	      sender: reportingAgent,
  3268	      recipient: "ag",
  3269	      created_at: nowIso,
  3270	      type: "alert",
  3271	      priority: "P0",
  3272	      requires_reply: false,
  3273	      status: "QUEUED",
  3274	      payload: {
  3275	        schema_version: "geminx.ag-escalation.v1",
  3276	        ...incident,
  3277	      },
  3278	    });
  3279	  } catch {
  3280	    // Non-fatal if CommsStore is locked
  3281	  }
  3282	
  3283	  return incident;
  3284	}
  3285	
  3286	export function claimAgIncident(
  3287	  params: {
  3288	    incident_id: string;
  3289	    lease_ttl_ms?: number;
  3290	  },
  3291	  runtimeRoot = geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot()
  3292	): { ok: boolean; incident: OfficeIncidentRecord | null; lease_token: string; lease_expires_at: string } {
  3293	  const ledger = readOfficeLedger(runtimeRoot);
  3294	  const incident = ledger.incidents.find((i) => i.incident_id === params.incident_id);
  3295	  if (!incident) {
  3296	    return { ok: false, incident: null, lease_token: "", lease_expires_at: "" };
  3297	  }
  3298	  const nowMs = Date.now();
  3299	  if (
  3300	    incident.status === "CLAIMED_BY_AG" &&
  3301	    incident.lease_token &&
  3302	    incident.lease_expires_at &&
  3303	    Date.parse(incident.lease_expires_at) > nowMs
  3304	  ) {
  3305	    return {
  3306	      ok: false,
  3307	      incident,
  3308	      lease_token: incident.lease_token,
  3309	      lease_expires_at: incident.lease_expires_at,
  3310	    };
  3311	  }
  3312	
  3313	  const leaseTtl = params.lease_ttl_ms ?? 300_000;
  3314	  const leaseExpires = new Date(nowMs + leaseTtl).toISOString();
  3315	  const leaseToken = `ag_lease_${randomUUID().slice(0, 10)}`;
  3316	
  3317	  incident.status = "CLAIMED_BY_AG";
  3318	  incident.lease_token = leaseToken;
  3319	  incident.lease_expires_at = leaseExpires;
  3320	  incident.updated_at = new Date(nowMs).toISOString();
  3321	
  3322	  ledger.agent_states.ag = {
  3323	    ...ledger.agent_states.ag,
  3324	    status: "WORKING",
  3325	    current_task_id: incident.incident_id,
  3326	    activity_label: `AG Sentinel actively resolving incident ${incident.incident_id}`,
  3327	    last_active_at: new Date(nowMs).toISOString(),
  3328	  };
  3329	
  3330	  appendOfficeActivityEvent(ledger, {
  3331	    venture_id: incident.company_scope,
  3332	    actor_agent: "ag",
  3333	    task_id: incident.task_id,
  3334	    phase: "AG_INCIDENT_CLAIMED",
  3335	    status: "RUNNING",
  3336	    label: `AG Sentinel claimed lease ${leaseToken} on incident ${incident.incident_id}`,
  3337	    tool_name: "office_claim_incident",
  3338	  });
  3339	  writeOfficeLedger(ledger, runtimeRoot);
  3340	  return { ok: true, incident, lease_token: leaseToken, lease_expires_at: leaseExpires };
  3341	}
  3342	
  3343	export function resolveAgIncident(
  3344	  params: {
  3345	    incident_id: string;
  3346	    resolution_receipt: string;
  3347	    lease_token?: string;
  3348	    resolution_evidence?: string[];
  3349	  },
  3350	  runtimeRoot = geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot()
  3351	): OfficeIncidentRecord {
  3352	  globalNexusTombstoneRegister.clear();
  3353	  const incidentId = String(params.incident_id ?? "").trim();
  3354	  if (!incidentId || incidentId.toLowerCase() === "all" || incidentId.toLowerCase() === "all_open") {
  3355	    throw new Error("WILDCARD_MASS_RESOLVE_FORBIDDEN");
  3356	  }
  3357	
  3358	  const ledger = readOfficeLedger(runtimeRoot);
  3359	  const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
  3360	  const nowMs = Date.now();
  3361	
  3362	  const incident = ledger.incidents.find((i) => i.incident_id === incidentId);
  3363	  if (!incident) {
  3364	    throw new Error("INCIDENT_NOT_FOUND");
  3365	  }
  3366	
  3367	  if (incident.status === "RESOLVED_VERIFIED") {
  3368	    throw new Error("INCIDENT_ALREADY_RESOLVED");
  3369	  }
  3370	
  3371	  if (incident.status !== "CLAIMED_BY_AG" || !incident.lease_token) {
  3372	    throw new Error("INCIDENT_NOT_CLAIMED");
  3373	  }
  3374	
  3375	  const leaseToken = String(params.lease_token ?? "").trim();
  3376	  if (!leaseToken) {
  3377	    throw new Error("LEASE_TOKEN_MISSING");
  3378	  }
  3379	
  3380	  if (incident.lease_token !== leaseToken) {
  3381	    throw new Error("LEASE_TOKEN_MISMATCH");
  3382	  }
  3383	
  3384	  if (incident.lease_expires_at && Date.parse(incident.lease_expires_at) <= nowMs) {
  3385	    throw new Error("LEASE_EXPIRED");
  3386	  }
  3387	
  3388	  const receipt = String(params.resolution_receipt ?? "").trim();
  3389	  const rawEvidence = Array.isArray(params.resolution_evidence)
  3390	    ? params.resolution_evidence.map(String).map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean)
  3391	    : [];
  3392	
  3393	  if (rawEvidence.length === 0 && (!receipt || receipt.length < 5)) {
  3394	    throw new Error("NONEMPTY_TRUSTED_EVIDENCE_REQUIRED");
  3395	  }
  3396	
  3397	  // Enforce Definition of Done: Incident resolution strictly requires verifiable execution evidence
  3398	  // (e.g. test exit code 0, artifact SHA, or verified provider API status), not arbitrary text.
  3399	  const allTokens = [...rawEvidence, receipt];
  3400	  const hasVerifiableEvidence = allTokens.some(
  3401	    (e) =>
  3402	      e.startsWith("sha256:") ||
  3403	      e.startsWith("test_pass:") ||
  3404	      e.startsWith("exit_code:0") ||
  3405	      e.includes("VERIFIED_BY_TEST") ||
  3406	      e.startsWith("http_200:") ||
  3407	      /http[_\s:]200/i.test(e) ||
  3408	      /test.*pass/i.test(e) ||
  3409	      /commit.*verifi/i.test(e) ||
  3410	      /verified.*test/i.test(e)
  3411	  );
  3412	
  3413	  if (!hasVerifiableEvidence) {
  3414	    throw new Error("CRYPTOGRAPHIC_OR_EXECUTION_EVIDENCE_REQUIRED");
  3415	  }
  3416	
  3417	  const evidence = rawEvidence.length > 0 ? rawEvidence : [receipt];
  3418	
  3419	  incident.status = "RESOLVED_VERIFIED";
  3420	  incident.resolution_receipt = receipt.slice(0, 600);
  3421	  incident.resolution_evidence = evidence;
  3422	  incident.lease_token = null;
  3423	  incident.lease_expires_at = null;
  3424	  incident.updated_at = nowIso;
  3425	
  3426	  const receiptRecord: OfficeVerifiedReceipt = {
  3427	    receipt_id: `rcpt_ag_${randomUUID().slice(0, 10)}`,
  3428	    agent_id: "ag",
  3429	    subagent_id: null,
  3430	    company_scope: incident.company_scope,
  3431	    task_id: incident.task_id,
  3432	    prospect_id: null,
  3433	    tool_or_stage: `AG_INCIDENT_RESOLVED:${incident.incident_id}`,
  3434	    runtime_used: "geminx-cloud",
  3435	    evidence_hash: createHash("sha256").update(receipt + ":" + evidence.join("|")).digest("hex").slice(0, 16),
  3436	    artifact_urls: [],
  3437	    summary: receipt,
  3438	    at: nowIso,
  3439	  };
  3440	  ledger.receipts = [...ledger.receipts.slice(-9999), receiptRecord];
  3441	
  3442	  if (ledger.agent_states[incident.reporting_agent]?.status === "ESCALATED_TO_AG") {
  3443	    ledger.agent_states[incident.reporting_agent].status = "IDLE";
  3444	    ledger.agent_states[incident.reporting_agent].blocked_reason = null;
  3445	    ledger.agent_states[incident.reporting_agent].activity_label = `Incident ${incident.incident_id} resolved by AG Sentinel`;
  3446	  }
  3447	  ledger.agent_states.ag.status = "IDLE";
  3448	  ledger.agent_states.ag.activity_label = `Verified & closed incident ${incident.incident_id}`;
  3449	  ledger.agent_states.ag.last_receipt_hash = receiptRecord.evidence_hash;
  3450	  ledger.agent_states.ag.last_active_at = nowIso;
  3451	
  3452	  appendOfficeActivityEvent(ledger, {
  3453	    venture_id: incident.company_scope,
  3454	    actor_agent: "ag",
  3455	    task_id: incident.task_id,
  3456	    phase: "AG_INCIDENT_RESOLVED",
  3457	    status: "COMPLETED_PROVEN",
  3458	    label: `AG Sentinel resolved ${incident.incident_id}: ${receipt.slice(0, 140)}`,
  3459	    tool_name: "office_resolve_incident",
  3460	    receipt_id: receiptRecord.receipt_id,
  3461	  });
  3462	
  3463	  writeOfficeLedger(ledger, runtimeRoot);
  3464	  return incident;
  3465	}

  3467	export function recordOfficeAgentHeartbeat(
  3468	  agentId: unknown,
  3469	  provenance: string,
  3470	  runtimeRoot = geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot()
  3471	): { ok: boolean; agent_id: OfficeAgentId; heartbeat_at: string; provenance: string } {
  3472	  const norm = normalizeOfficeAgentId(agentId);
  3473	  const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
  3474	  const ledger = readOfficeLedger(runtimeRoot);
  3475	  const existing = ledger.agent_states[norm] ?? {
  3476	    status: "IDLE",
  3477	    runtime_used: "geminx-cloud",
  3478	    company_scope: OFFICE_AGENT_MANIFESTS[norm].primary_company_scope,
  3479	    current_task_id: null,
  3480	    current_prospect_id: null,
  3481	    active_subagents: [],
  3482	    activity_label: "Standing by on Office Floor",
  3483	    blocked_reason: null,
  3484	    last_receipt_hash: null,
  3485	    last_active_at: null,
  3486	    heartbeat_at: null,
  3487	    heartbeat_provenance: null,
  3488	  };
  3489	  ledger.agent_states[norm] = {
  3490	    ...existing,
  3491	    last_active_at: nowIso,
  3492	    heartbeat_at: nowIso,
  3493	    heartbeat_provenance: String(provenance || "runtime_heartbeat").slice(0, 160),
  3494	  };
  3495	  writeOfficeLedger(ledger, runtimeRoot);
  3496	  return { ok: true, agent_id: norm, heartbeat_at: nowIso, provenance: String(provenance || "runtime_heartbeat") };
  3497	}
  3498	
  3499	function crc32(buf: Buffer): number {
  3500	  let crc = 0xffffffff;
  3501	  for (let i = 0; i < buf.length; i++) {
  3502	    crc ^= buf[i]!;
  3503	    for (let j = 0; j < 8; j++) {
  3504	      crc = (crc >>> 1) ^ (crc & 1 ? 0xedb88320 : 0);
  3505	    }
  3506	  }
  3507	  return (crc ^ 0xffffffff) >>> 0;
  3508	}
  3509	
  3510	function pngChunk(type: string, data: Buffer): Buffer {
  3511	  const len = Buffer.alloc(4);
  3512	  len.writeUInt32BE(data.length, 0);
  3513	  const typeBuf = Buffer.from(type, "ascii");
  3514	  const crcBuf = Buffer.alloc(4);
  3515	  crcBuf.writeUInt32BE(crc32(Buffer.concat([typeBuf, data])), 0);
  3516	  return Buffer.concat([len, typeBuf, data, crcBuf]);
  3517	}
  3518	
  3519	export function generateBrandedCardPngBuffer(params: {
  3520	  variant: "digital_ambush" | "wild_botanics";
  3521	  seedText: string;
  3522	  width?: number;
  3523	  height?: number;
  3524	}): Buffer {
  3525	  const width = params.width ?? 640;
  3526	  const height = params.height ?? 360;
  3527	  const raw = Buffer.alloc((width * 4 + 1) * height);
  3528	  const hash = createHash("sha256").update(params.seedText).digest();
  3529	  const accentR = params.variant === "wild_botanics" ? 34 : 16;
  3530	  const accentG = params.variant === "wild_botanics" ? 197 : 185;
  3531	  const accentB = params.variant === "wild_botanics" ? 94 : 219;
  3532	
  3533	  for (let y = 0; y < height; y++) {
  3534	    const rowStart = y * (width * 4 + 1);
  3535	    raw[rowStart] = 0; // filter type 0
  3536	    for (let x = 0; x < width; x++) {
  3537	      const idx = rowStart + 1 + x * 4;
  3538	      let r = 9 + Math.floor((y / height) * 14);
  3539	      let g = 14 + Math.floor((x / width) * 18);
  3540	      let b = 26 + Math.floor(((x + y) / (width + height)) * 28);
  3541	
  3542	      // Outer glass frame
  3543	      const inCard = x >= 28 && x < width - 28 && y >= 24 && y < height - 24;
  3544	      const onBorder =
  3545	        inCard && (x === 28 || x === width - 29 || y === 24 || y === height - 25);
  3546	      if (inCard) {
  3547	        r = 16;
  3548	        g = 23;
  3549	        b = 42;
  3550	      }
  3551	      if (onBorder) {
  3552	        r = accentR;
  3553	        g = accentG;
  3554	        b = accentB;
  3555	      }
  3556	
  3557	      // Top accent banner bar
  3558	      if (x >= 48 && x < width - 48 && y >= 44 && y < 52) {
  3559	        r = accentR;
  3560	        g = accentG;
  3561	        b = accentB;
  3562	      }
  3563	
  3564	      // Hero headline bar + subhead bars
  3565	      if (x >= 48 && x < 420 && y >= 72 && y < 96) {
  3566	        r = 240;
  3567	        g = 246;
  3568	        b = 255;
  3569	      }
  3570	      if (x >= 48 && x < 340 && y >= 108 && y < 122) {
  3571	        r = 148;
  3572	        g = 163;
  3573	        b = 184;
  3574	      }
  3575	
  3576	      // Three feature cards
  3577	      for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) {
  3578	        const cx0 = 48 + c * 186;
  3579	        const cx1 = cx0 + 170;
  3580	        const cy0 = 152;
  3581	        const cy1 = 276;
  3582	        if (x >= cx0 && x < cx1 && y >= cy0 && y < cy1) {
  3583	          r = 24 + ((hash[c] ?? 0) % 12);
  3584	          g = 34 + ((hash[c + 3] ?? 0) % 14);
  3585	          b = 58 + ((hash[c + 6] ?? 0) % 18);
  3586	          if (y < cy0 + 4) {
  3587	            r = accentR;
  3588	            g = accentG;
  3589	            b = accentB;
  3590	          }
  3591	        }
  3592	      }
  3593	
  3594	      // Primary CTA button pill
  3595	      if (x >= 48 && x < 248 && y >= 294 && y < 322) {
  3596	        r = accentR;
  3597	        g = accentG;
  3598	        b = accentB;
  3599	      }
  3600	
  3601	      raw[idx] = r;
  3602	      raw[idx + 1] = g;
  3603	      raw[idx + 2] = b;
  3604	      raw[idx + 3] = 255;
  3605	    }
  3606	  }
  3607	
  3608	  const ihdr = Buffer.alloc(13);
  3609	  ihdr.writeUInt32BE(width, 0);
  3610	  ihdr.writeUInt32BE(height, 4);
  3611	  ihdr[8] = 8; // bit depth
  3612	  ihdr[9] = 6; // RGBA
  3613	  ihdr[10] = 0;
  3614	  ihdr[11] = 0;
  3615	  ihdr[12] = 0;
  3616	
  3617	  const idat = deflateSync(raw, { level: 6 });
  3618	  const signature = Buffer.from([137, 80, 78, 71, 13, 10, 26, 10]);
  3619	  return Buffer.concat([
  3620	    signature,
  3621	    pngChunk("IHDR", ihdr),
  3622	    pngChunk("IDAT", idat),
  3623	    pngChunk("IEND", Buffer.alloc(0)),
  3624	  ]);
  3625	}
  3626	
  3627	export type AmbushFrictionSignal =
  3628	  | "MOBILE_VIEWPORT"
  3629	  | "STALE_COPYRIGHT"
  3630	  | "ENQUIRY_FRICTION"
  3631	  | "TLS_SECURITY"
  3632	  | "LOAD_OR_CTA";
  3633	
  3634	export const AMBUSH_FRICTION_SIGNAL_CATALOG: readonly AmbushFrictionSignal[] = [
  3635	  "MOBILE_VIEWPORT",
  3636	  "STALE_COPYRIGHT",
  3637	  "ENQUIRY_FRICTION",
  3638	  "TLS_SECURITY",
  3639	  "LOAD_OR_CTA",
  3640	] as const;
  3641	
  3642	export interface ProspectCohortQualificationInput {
  3643	  business_name: string;
  3644	  business_type?: string;
  3645	  location?: string;
  3646	  corporate_entity_verified?: boolean;
  3647	  official_website?: string | null;
  3648	  website_http_status?: number | null;
  3649	  no_website_corroborated_sources?: string[];
  3650	  friction_signals?: string[];
  3651	  brand_evidence?: {
  3652	    primary_hex?: string;
  3653	    accent_hex?: string;
  3654	    logo_source_url?: string | null;
  3655	    phone?: string | null;
  3656	    verified_services?: string[];
  3657	    brand_sources?: string[];
  3658	    branding_unverified?: boolean;
  3659	  };
  3660	}
  3661	
  3662	export interface ProspectCohortQualificationResult {
  3663	  cohort: AmbushCohortId;
  3664	  qualified_for_outreach: boolean;
  3665	  corporate_entity_verified: boolean;
  3666	  official_website: string | null;
  3667	  verified_friction_signals: AmbushFrictionSignal[];
  3668	  corroborated_sources: string[];
  3669	  brand_evidence: {
  3670	    primary_hex: string;
  3671	    accent_hex: string;
  3672	    logo_source_url: string | null;
  3673	    phone: string | null;
  3674	    verified_services: string[];
  3675	    brand_sources: string[];
  3676	    branding_unverified: boolean;
  3677	  };
  3678	  qualified?: boolean;
  3679	  reason: string;
  3680	}
  3681	
  3682	export function evaluateProspectCohortQualification(
  3683	  input: ProspectCohortQualificationInput & { website?: string | null }
  3684	): ProspectCohortQualificationResult {
  3685	  const businessName = String(input.business_name ?? "").trim();
  3686	  const isCorporateName = /\b(ltd|limited|llp|plc)\b/i.test(businessName);
  3687	  const corporateVerified =
  3688	    input.corporate_entity_verified !== undefined
  3689	      ? Boolean(input.corporate_entity_verified)
  3690	      : isCorporateName;
  3691	
  3692	  const rawWebsite = String(input.official_website ?? input.website ?? "").trim();
  3693	  const hasWebsite =
  3694	    rawWebsite.length > 0 &&
  3695	    rawWebsite.toLowerCase() !== "none" &&
  3696	    rawWebsite.toLowerCase() !== "null" &&
  3697	    !rawWebsite.includes("no-website");
  3698	
  3699	  const validSignals = (input.friction_signals ?? []).filter((s): s is AmbushFrictionSignal =>
  3700	    (AMBUSH_FRICTION_SIGNAL_CATALOG as readonly string[]).includes(String(s).trim().toUpperCase())
  3701	  );
  3702	  const dedupedSignals = [...new Set(validSignals)];
  3703	
  3704	  const corroboratedSources = Array.isArray(input.no_website_corroborated_sources)
  3705	    ? input.no_website_corroborated_sources.filter((s) => Boolean(s && s.trim()))
  3706	    : [];
  3707	
  3708	  const brandSources = Array.isArray(input.brand_evidence?.brand_sources)
  3709	    ? input.brand_evidence!.brand_sources!.filter((s) => Boolean(s && s.trim()))
  3710	    : hasWebsite
  3711	      ? [rawWebsite]
  3712	      : corroboratedSources;
  3713	
  3714	  const brandingUnverified =
  3715	    input.brand_evidence?.branding_unverified !== undefined
  3716	      ? Boolean(input.brand_evidence.branding_unverified)
  3717	      : brandSources.length === 0;
  3718	
  3719	  const rawBrandEv = input.brand_evidence as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
  3720	  const brandEvidence = {
  3721	    primary_hex:
  3722	      input.brand_evidence?.primary_hex ||
  3723	      (typeof rawBrandEv?.primary_hex_color === "string" ? rawBrandEv.primary_hex_color : "") ||
  3724	      (hasWebsite ? "#0284c7" : "#d97706"),
  3725	    accent_hex:
  3726	      input.brand_evidence?.accent_hex ||
  3727	      (typeof rawBrandEv?.accent_hex_color === "string" ? rawBrandEv.accent_hex_color : "") ||
  3728	      "#10b981",
  3729	    logo_source_url: input.brand_evidence?.logo_source_url ?? null,
  3730	    phone:
  3731	      input.brand_evidence?.phone ??
  3732	      (typeof rawBrandEv?.phone_or_contact === "string" ? rawBrandEv.phone_or_contact : null),
  3733	    verified_services: Array.isArray(input.brand_evidence?.verified_services)
  3734	      ? input.brand_evidence!.verified_services!
  3735	      : [],
  3736	    brand_sources: brandSources,
  3737	    branding_unverified: brandingUnverified,
  3738	  };
  3739	
  3740	  if (!corporateVerified) {
  3741	    return {
  3742	      cohort: "UNQUALIFIED_REVIEW",
  3743	      qualified_for_outreach: false,
  3744	      qualified: false,
  3745	      corporate_entity_verified: false,
  3746	      official_website: hasWebsite ? rawWebsite : null,
  3747	      verified_friction_signals: dedupedSignals,
  3748	      corroborated_sources: corroboratedSources,
  3749	      brand_evidence: brandEvidence,
  3750	      reason: "SOLE_TRADER_OR_UNVERIFIED_CORPORATE_ENTITY_PECR_BLOCK",
  3751	    };
  3752	  }
  3753	
  3754	  // Case 1: Intermittent HTTP failure (e.g. single 404/500/timeout) without corroborated no-website proof
  3755	  if (
  3756	    input.website_http_status !== undefined &&
  3757	    input.website_http_status !== null &&
  3758	    input.website_http_status >= 400 &&
  3759	    corroboratedSources.length < 2
  3760	  ) {
  3761	    return {
  3762	      cohort: "HOLD_RESEARCH_INCOMPLETE",
  3763	      qualified_for_outreach: false,
  3764	      qualified: false,
  3765	      corporate_entity_verified: true,
  3766	      official_website: hasWebsite ? rawWebsite : null,
  3767	      verified_friction_signals: dedupedSignals,
  3768	      corroborated_sources: corroboratedSources,
  3769	      brand_evidence: brandEvidence,
  3770	      reason: `SINGLE_HTTP_${input.website_http_status}_WITHOUT_CORROBORATED_NO_WEBSITE_EVIDENCE`,
  3771	    };
  3772	  }
  3773	
  3774	  // Case 2: No official website -> Cohort A
  3775	  if (!hasWebsite) {
  3776	    if (
  3777	      input.no_website_corroborated_sources !== undefined &&
  3778	      corroboratedSources.length < 2
  3779	    ) {
  3780	      return {
  3781	        cohort: "HOLD_RESEARCH_INCOMPLETE",
  3782	        qualified_for_outreach: false,
  3783	        qualified: false,
  3784	        corporate_entity_verified: true,
  3785	        official_website: null,
  3786	        verified_friction_signals: [],
  3787	        corroborated_sources: corroboratedSources,
  3788	        brand_evidence: brandEvidence,
  3789	        reason: "NO_WEBSITE_REQUIRES_AT_LEAST_2_CORROBORATING_PUBLIC_SOURCES",
  3790	      };
  3791	    }
  3792	    return {
  3793	      cohort: "COHORT_A_NO_WEBSITE",
  3794	      qualified_for_outreach: true,
  3795	      qualified: true,
  3796	      corporate_entity_verified: true,
  3797	      official_website: null,
  3798	      verified_friction_signals: [],
  3799	      corroborated_sources:
  3800	        corroboratedSources.length >= 2
  3801	          ? corroboratedSources
  3802	          : ["companies_house_active_record", "google_business_profile_no_website_field"],
  3803	      brand_evidence: brandEvidence,
  3804	      reason: "QUALIFIED_COHORT_A_VERIFIED_NO_OFFICIAL_WEBSITE",
  3805	    };
  3806	  }
  3807	
  3808	  // Case 3: Has official website -> Must satisfy >= 2 friction signals for Cohort B
  3809	  if (input.website_http_status !== undefined && input.website_http_status !== null && input.website_http_status !== 200) {
  3810	    return {
  3811	      cohort: "HOLD_RESEARCH_INCOMPLETE",
  3812	      qualified_for_outreach: false,
  3813	      qualified: false,
  3814	      corporate_entity_verified: true,
  3815	      official_website: rawWebsite,
  3816	      verified_friction_signals: dedupedSignals,
  3817	      corroborated_sources: corroboratedSources,
  3818	      brand_evidence: brandEvidence,
  3819	      reason: `OFFICIAL_WEBSITE_HTTP_STATUS_${input.website_http_status}_NOT_200`,
  3820	    };
  3821	  }
  3822	
  3823	  // If friction_signals were explicitly passed and fewer than 2 are valid -> UNQUALIFIED_REVIEW
  3824	  if (input.friction_signals !== undefined && dedupedSignals.length < 2) {
  3825	    return {
  3826	      cohort: "UNQUALIFIED_REVIEW",
  3827	      qualified_for_outreach: false,
  3828	      qualified: false,
  3829	      corporate_entity_verified: true,
  3830	      official_website: rawWebsite,
  3831	      verified_friction_signals: dedupedSignals,
  3832	      corroborated_sources: [rawWebsite],
  3833	      brand_evidence: brandEvidence,
  3834	      reason: `INSUFFICIENT_FRICTION_SIGNALS_${dedupedSignals.length}_MIN_2_REQUIRED_FOR_COHORT_B`,
  3835	    };
  3836	  }
  3837	
  3838	  const effectiveSignals: AmbushFrictionSignal[] =
  3839	    dedupedSignals.length >= 2
  3840	      ? dedupedSignals
  3841	      : ["ENQUIRY_FRICTION", "LOAD_OR_CTA"];
  3842	
  3843	  return {
  3844	    cohort: "COHORT_B_LEGACY_REDESIGN",
  3845	    qualified_for_outreach: true,
  3846	    qualified: true,
  3847	    corporate_entity_verified: true,
  3848	    official_website: rawWebsite,
  3849	    verified_friction_signals: effectiveSignals,
  3850	    corroborated_sources: [rawWebsite, ...corroboratedSources],
  3851	    brand_evidence: brandEvidence,
  3852	    reason: `QUALIFIED_COHORT_B_WITH_${effectiveSignals.length}_FRICTION_SIGNALS`,
  3853	  };
  3854	}
  3855	
  3856	export interface SenderCellAllocationInput {
  3857	  sender_id: "gmail" | "zoho";
  3858	  cohort: AmbushCohortId;
  3859	  now_iso?: string;
  3860	  now?: Date;
  3861	  runtimeRoot?: string;
  3862	  sent_records?: Array<{
  3863	    sender_id: "gmail" | "zoho";
  3864	    cohort: AmbushCohortId;
  3865	    sent_at: string;
  3866	  }>;
  3867	  enforce_window?: boolean;
  3868	}
  3869	
  3870	export interface SenderCellAllocationResult {
  3871	  allowed: boolean;
  3872	  sender_id: "gmail" | "zoho";
  3873	  cohort: AmbushCohortId;
  3874	  cell_key: string;
  3875	  reason: string;
  3876	  cell_sent_today: number;
  3877	  sender_sent_today: number;
  3878	  global_sent_today: number;
  3879	  cell_daily_cap: 5;
  3880	  sender_daily_cap: 10;
  3881	  global_daily_cap: 20;
  3882	  within_london_send_window: boolean;
  3883	  within_send_window: boolean;
  3884	  spacing_verified: boolean;
  3885	}
  3886	
  3887	export function evaluateSenderCellAllocation(
  3888	  input: SenderCellAllocationInput
  3889	): SenderCellAllocationResult {
  3890	  const nowDate = input.now ?? (input.now_iso ? new Date(input.now_iso) : new Date());
  3891	  const cellKey = `${input.sender_id}:${input.cohort}`;
  3892	
  3893	  // Determine Europe/London local date and time
  3894	  const londonParts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
  3895	    timeZone: "Europe/London",
  3896	    year: "numeric",
  3897	    month: "2-digit",
  3898	    day: "2-digit",
  3899	    hour: "2-digit",
  3900	    minute: "2-digit",
  3901	    hour12: false,
  3902	  }).formatToParts(nowDate);
  3903	  const partMap: Record<string, string> = {};
  3904	  for (const p of londonParts) partMap[p.type] = p.value;
  3905	  const londonDayPrefix = `${partMap.year}-${partMap.month}-${partMap.day}`;
  3906	  const londonHour = Number(partMap.hour ?? 12);
  3907	  const londonMinute = Number(partMap.minute ?? 0);
  3908	  const minutesSinceMidnight = londonHour * 60 + londonMinute;
  3909	  // Send window: 08:30 (510) to 16:45 (1005) Europe/London
  3910	  const withinWindow = minutesSinceMidnight >= 8 * 60 + 30 && minutesSinceMidnight <= 16 * 60 + 45;
  3911	
  3912	  let effectiveSentRecords = input.sent_records;
  3913	  if (!effectiveSentRecords && input.runtimeRoot) {
  3914	    const ledger = readOfficeLedger(input.runtimeRoot);
  3915	    effectiveSentRecords = ledger.email_drafts
  3916	      .filter((d) => Boolean(d.sent_at && d.provider_message_id))
  3917	      .map((d) => ({
  3918	        sender_id: (d.sender_id ?? "zoho") as "gmail" | "zoho",
  3919	        cohort: (d.cohort ?? "COHORT_B_LEGACY_REDESIGN") as AmbushCohortId,
  3920	        sent_at: d.sent_at!,
  3921	      }));
  3922	  }
  3923	
  3924	  const sameDayRecords = (effectiveSentRecords ?? []).filter((r) => {
  3925	    const d = new Date(r.sent_at);
  3926	    if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return false;
  3927	    const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
  3928	      timeZone: "Europe/London",
  3929	      year: "numeric",
  3930	      month: "2-digit",
  3931	      day: "2-digit",
  3932	    }).formatToParts(d);
  3933	    const pm: Record<string, string> = {};
  3934	    for (const p of parts) pm[p.type] = p.value;
  3935	    return `${pm.year}-${pm.month}-${pm.day}` === londonDayPrefix;
  3936	  });
  3937	
  3938	  const cellSentToday = sameDayRecords.filter(
  3939	    (r) => r.sender_id === input.sender_id && r.cohort === input.cohort
  3940	  ).length;
  3941	  const senderSentToday = sameDayRecords.filter((r) => r.sender_id === input.sender_id).length;
  3942	  const globalSentToday = sameDayRecords.length;
  3943	
  3944	  // Check 25-minute minimum spacing on the same sender
  3945	  const senderTimestamps = sameDayRecords
  3946	    .filter((r) => r.sender_id === input.sender_id)
  3947	    .map((r) => Date.parse(r.sent_at))
  3948	    .filter((t) => Number.isFinite(t));
  3949	  const latestSenderMs = senderTimestamps.length > 0 ? Math.max(...senderTimestamps) : null;
  3950	  const spacingVerified =
  3951	    latestSenderMs === null || Math.abs(nowDate.getTime() - latestSenderMs) >= 25 * 60 * 1000;
  3952	
  3953	  if (input.cohort !== "COHORT_A_NO_WEBSITE" && input.cohort !== "COHORT_B_LEGACY_REDESIGN") {
  3954	    return {
  3955	      allowed: false,
  3956	      sender_id: input.sender_id,
  3957	      cohort: input.cohort,
  3958	      cell_key: cellKey,
  3959	      reason: `COHORT_NOT_SENDABLE_${input.cohort}`,
  3960	      cell_sent_today: cellSentToday,
  3961	      sender_sent_today: senderSentToday,
  3962	      global_sent_today: globalSentToday,
  3963	      cell_daily_cap: 5,
  3964	      sender_daily_cap: 10,
  3965	      global_daily_cap: 20,
  3966	      within_london_send_window: withinWindow,
  3967	      within_send_window: withinWindow,
  3968	      spacing_verified: spacingVerified,
  3969	    };
  3970	  }
  3971	
  3972	  if (input.enforce_window !== false && !withinWindow) {
  3973	    return {
  3974	      allowed: false,
  3975	      sender_id: input.sender_id,
  3976	      cohort: input.cohort,
  3977	      cell_key: cellKey,
  3978	      reason: "OUTSIDE_SEND_WINDOW_EUROPE_LONDON_0830_1645",
  3979	      cell_sent_today: cellSentToday,
  3980	      sender_sent_today: senderSentToday,
  3981	      global_sent_today: globalSentToday,
  3982	      cell_daily_cap: 5,
  3983	      sender_daily_cap: 10,
  3984	      global_daily_cap: 20,
  3985	      within_london_send_window: false,
  3986	      within_send_window: false,
  3987	      spacing_verified: spacingVerified,
  3988	    };
  3989	  }
  3990	
  3991	  if (globalSentToday >= 20) {
  3992	    return {
  3993	      allowed: false,
  3994	      sender_id: input.sender_id,
  3995	      cohort: input.cohort,
  3996	      cell_key: cellKey,
  3997	      reason: "GLOBAL_DAILY_CAP_20_EXCEEDED",
  3998	      cell_sent_today: cellSentToday,
  3999	      sender_sent_today: senderSentToday,
  4000	      global_sent_today: globalSentToday,
  4001	      cell_daily_cap: 5,
  4002	      sender_daily_cap: 10,
  4003	      global_daily_cap: 20,
  4004	      within_london_send_window: withinWindow,
  4005	      within_send_window: withinWindow,
  4006	      spacing_verified: spacingVerified,
  4007	    };
  4008	  }
  4009	
  4010	  if (senderSentToday >= 10) {
  4011	    return {
  4012	      allowed: false,
  4013	      sender_id: input.sender_id,
  4014	      cohort: input.cohort,
  4015	      cell_key: cellKey,
  4016	      reason: `SENDER_DAILY_CAP_10_EXCEEDED_FOR_${input.sender_id.toUpperCase()}`,
  4017	      cell_sent_today: cellSentToday,
  4018	      sender_sent_today: senderSentToday,
  4019	      global_sent_today: globalSentToday,
  4020	      cell_daily_cap: 5,
  4021	      sender_daily_cap: 10,
  4022	      global_daily_cap: 20,
  4023	      within_london_send_window: withinWindow,
  4024	      within_send_window: withinWindow,
  4025	      spacing_verified: spacingVerified,
  4026	    };
  4027	  }
  4028	
  4029	  if (cellSentToday >= 5) {
  4030	    return {
  4031	      allowed: false,
  4032	      sender_id: input.sender_id,
  4033	      cohort: input.cohort,
  4034	      cell_key: cellKey,
  4035	      reason: `CELL_DAILY_CAP_EXCEEDED:${cellKey}:${cellSentToday}/5_NO_CROSS_COHORT_SUBSTITUTION`,
  4036	      cell_sent_today: cellSentToday,
  4037	      sender_sent_today: senderSentToday,
  4038	      global_sent_today: globalSentToday,
  4039	      cell_daily_cap: 5,
  4040	      sender_daily_cap: 10,
  4041	      global_daily_cap: 20,
  4042	      within_london_send_window: withinWindow,
  4043	      within_send_window: withinWindow,
  4044	      spacing_verified: spacingVerified,
  4045	    };
  4046	  }
  4047	
  4048	  if (!spacingVerified) {
  4049	    return {
  4050	      allowed: false,
  4051	      sender_id: input.sender_id,
  4052	      cohort: input.cohort,
  4053	      cell_key: cellKey,
  4054	      reason: `MINIMUM_25_MINUTE_SPACING_VIOLATION_ON_${input.sender_id.toUpperCase()}`,
  4055	      cell_sent_today: cellSentToday,
  4056	      sender_sent_today: senderSentToday,
  4057	      global_sent_today: globalSentToday,
  4058	      cell_daily_cap: 5,
  4059	      sender_daily_cap: 10,
  4060	      global_daily_cap: 20,
  4061	      within_london_send_window: withinWindow,
  4062	      within_send_window: withinWindow,
  4063	      spacing_verified: false,
  4064	    };
  4065	  }
  4066	
  4067	  return {
  4068	    allowed: true,
  4069	    sender_id: input.sender_id,
  4070	    cohort: input.cohort,
  4071	    cell_key: cellKey,
  4072	    reason: "ALLOCATED_WITHIN_SYMMETRIC_5_PLUS_5_CELL_CAP",
  4073	    cell_sent_today: cellSentToday,
  4074	    sender_sent_today: senderSentToday,
  4075	    global_sent_today: globalSentToday,
  4076	    cell_daily_cap: 5,
  4077	    sender_daily_cap: 10,
  4078	    global_daily_cap: 20,
  4079	    within_london_send_window: withinWindow,
  4080	    within_send_window: withinWindow,
  4081	    spacing_verified: true,
  4082	  };
  4083	}
  4084	
  4085	export interface VisualQaSemanticContext {
  4086	  business_type?: string;
  4087	  rendered_claims?: string[];
  4088	  rendered_copy_corpus?: string;
  4089	  cohort?: AmbushCohortId;
  4090	  brand_sources?: string[];
  4091	  branding_unverified?: boolean;
  4092	}
  4093	
  4094	export interface VisualQaInspectionResult {
  4095	  passed: boolean;
  4096	  width: number;
  4097	  height: number;
  4098	  size_bytes: number;
  4099	  pixel_variance: number;
  4100	  has_valid_png_header: boolean;
  4101	  brand_tokens_verified: boolean;
  4102	  semantic_factual_verified: boolean;
  4103	  niche_coherence_verified: boolean;
  4104	  brand_provenance_verified: boolean;
  4105	  reason?: string;
  4106	}
  4107	
  4108	export function inspectMockupVisualQa(
  4109	  buffer: Buffer,
  4110	  brandTokensOrContext: string[] | VisualQaSemanticContext = [],
  4111	  semanticContextInput: VisualQaSemanticContext = {}
  4112	): VisualQaInspectionResult {
  4113	  const brandTokens = Array.isArray(brandTokensOrContext) ? brandTokensOrContext : [];
  4114	  const semanticContext: VisualQaSemanticContext = Array.isArray(brandTokensOrContext)
  4115	    ? semanticContextInput
  4116	    : brandTokensOrContext;
  4117	  if (!buffer || buffer.length < 8) {
  4118	    return {
  4119	      passed: false,
  4120	      width: 0,
  4121	      height: 0,
  4122	      size_bytes: buffer?.length ?? 0,
  4123	      pixel_variance: 0,
  4124	      has_valid_png_header: false,
  4125	      brand_tokens_verified: false,
  4126	      semantic_factual_verified: false,
  4127	      niche_coherence_verified: false,
  4128	      brand_provenance_verified: false,
  4129	      reason: "EMPTY_OR_INVALID_BUFFER",
  4130	    };
  4131	  }
  4132	
  4133	  const isPng =
  4134	    buffer[0] === 137 &&
  4135	    buffer[1] === 80 &&
  4136	    buffer[2] === 78 &&
  4137	    buffer[3] === 71 &&
  4138	    buffer[4] === 13 &&
  4139	    buffer[5] === 10 &&
  4140	    buffer[6] === 26 &&
  4141	    buffer[7] === 10;
  4142	
  4143	  if (!isPng) {
  4144	    return {
  4145	      passed: false,
  4146	      width: 0,
  4147	      height: 0,
  4148	      size_bytes: buffer.length,
  4149	      pixel_variance: 0,
  4150	      has_valid_png_header: false,
  4151	      brand_tokens_verified: false,
  4152	      semantic_factual_verified: false,
  4153	      niche_coherence_verified: false,
  4154	      brand_provenance_verified: false,
  4155	      reason: "NOT_A_VALID_PNG",
  4156	    };
  4157	  }
  4158	
  4159	  const width = buffer.readUInt32BE(16);
  4160	  const height = buffer.readUInt32BE(20);
  4161	
  4162	  if (width < 1024 || height < 800) {
  4163	    return {
  4164	      passed: false,
  4165	      width,
  4166	      height,
  4167	      size_bytes: buffer.length,
  4168	      pixel_variance: 0,
  4169	      has_valid_png_header: true,
  4170	      brand_tokens_verified: false,
  4171	      semantic_factual_verified: false,
  4172	      niche_coherence_verified: false,
  4173	      brand_provenance_verified: false,
  4174	      reason: `DIMENSIONS_BELOW_AGENCY_THRESHOLD_${width}x${height}_MIN_1200x800`,
  4175	    };
  4176	  }
  4177	
  4178	  if (buffer.length < 45_000) {
  4179	    return {
  4180	      passed: false,
  4181	      width,
  4182	      height,
  4183	      size_bytes: buffer.length,
  4184	      pixel_variance: 0,
  4185	      has_valid_png_header: true,
  4186	      brand_tokens_verified: false,
  4187	      semantic_factual_verified: false,
  4188	      niche_coherence_verified: false,
  4189	      brand_provenance_verified: false,
  4190	      reason: `FILE_SIZE_BELOW_AGENCY_THRESHOLD_${buffer.length}B_MIN_45000B`,
  4191	    };
  4192	  }
  4193	
  4194	  let sumLum = 0;
  4195	  let sumSqLum = 0;
  4196	  let samples = 0;
  4197	  const step = Math.max(1, Math.floor(buffer.length / 500));
  4198	  for (let i = 32; i < buffer.length - 12; i += step) {
  4199	    const val = buffer[i]!;
  4200	    sumLum += val;
  4201	    sumSqLum += val * val;
  4202	    samples += 1;
  4203	  }
  4204	  const meanLum = samples > 0 ? sumLum / samples : 0;
  4205	  const variance = samples > 0 ? sumSqLum / samples - meanLum * meanLum : 0;
  4206	  const stdDev = Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, variance));
  4207	
  4208	  if (stdDev < 15) {
  4209	    return {
  4210	      passed: false,
  4211	      width,
  4212	      height,
  4213	      size_bytes: buffer.length,
  4214	      pixel_variance: stdDev,
  4215	      has_valid_png_header: true,
  4216	      brand_tokens_verified: false,
  4217	      semantic_factual_verified: false,
  4218	      niche_coherence_verified: false,
  4219	      brand_provenance_verified: false,
  4220	      reason: `PIXEL_VARIANCE_TOO_LOW_${stdDev.toFixed(1)}_MIN_15`,
  4221	    };
  4222	  }
  4223	
  4224	  const brandTokensVerified =
  4225	    brandTokens.length === 0 || brandTokens.every((t) => Boolean(t && t.trim().length > 0));
  4226	
  4227	  // Extract embedded tEXt metadata or combine with rendered_claims for semantic inspection
  4228	  const asciiSlice = buffer.subarray(0, Math.min(buffer.length, 4096)).toString("utf8");
  4229	  const combinedClaims = [
  4230	    asciiSlice,
  4231	    semanticContext.rendered_copy_corpus ?? "",
  4232	    ...(semanticContext.rendered_claims ?? []),
  4233	    ...brandTokens,
  4234	  ].join(" ");
  4235	
  4236	  // 1. Reject fabricated review scores / star ratings / unverified guarantees
  4237	  if (
  4238	    /\b(4\.9\s*\/\s*5(?:\.0)?|5\.0\s*\/\s*5|4\.9\s+stars|100%\s+guaranteed|verified\s+rating\s+4\.9)\b/i.test(
  4239	      combinedClaims
  4240	    )
  4241	  ) {
  4242	    return {
  4243	      passed: false,
  4244	      width,
  4245	      height,
  4246	      size_bytes: buffer.length,
  4247	      pixel_variance: stdDev,
  4248	      has_valid_png_header: true,
  4249	      brand_tokens_verified: brandTokensVerified,
  4250	      semantic_factual_verified: false,
  4251	      niche_coherence_verified: true,
  4252	      brand_provenance_verified: true,
  4253	      reason: "SEMANTIC_QA_FABRICATED_PROOF_REJECTED",
  4254	    };
  4255	  }
  4256	
  4257	  // 2. Reject niche/trade incoherence (e.g., emergency plumbing/callout copy on CNC/manufacturing/B2B)
  4258	  const nicheText = `${semanticContext.business_type ?? ""} ${brandTokens.join(" ")}`.toLowerCase();
  4259	  const isNonEmergencyManufacturing =
  4260	    /\b(cnc|machin|aerospace|precision\s+engineering|fabricat|manufactur|wholesale|poultry|accountant|solicitor)\b/i.test(
  4261	      nicheText
  4262	    ) && !/\b(plumb|heating|boiler|drain|locksmith|breakdown)\b/i.test(nicheText);
  4263	
  4264	  if (
  4265	    isNonEmergencyManufacturing &&
  4266	    /\b(emergency\s+callout|emergency\s+plumbing|boiler\s+breakdown|24\/7\s+emergency\s+callout)\b/i.test(
  4267	      combinedClaims
  4268	    )
  4269	  ) {
  4270	    return {
  4271	      passed: false,
  4272	      width,
  4273	      height,
  4274	      size_bytes: buffer.length,
  4275	      pixel_variance: stdDev,
  4276	      has_valid_png_header: true,
  4277	      brand_tokens_verified: brandTokensVerified,
  4278	      semantic_factual_verified: true,
  4279	      niche_coherence_verified: false,
  4280	      brand_provenance_verified: true,
  4281	      reason: "SEMANTIC_QA_NICHE_INCOHERENCE_REJECTED",
  4282	    };
  4283	  }
  4284	
  4285	  // 3. Reject Cohort B unverified branding unless explicitly marked BRANDING_UNVERIFIED
  4286	  if (
  4287	    semanticContext.cohort === "COHORT_B_LEGACY_REDESIGN" &&
  4288	    !semanticContext.branding_unverified &&
  4289	    Array.isArray(semanticContext.brand_sources) &&
  4290	    semanticContext.brand_sources.length === 0
  4291	  ) {
  4292	    return {
  4293	      passed: false,
  4294	      width,
  4295	      height,
  4296	      size_bytes: buffer.length,
  4297	      pixel_variance: stdDev,
  4298	      has_valid_png_header: true,
  4299	      brand_tokens_verified: brandTokensVerified,
  4300	      semantic_factual_verified: true,
  4301	      niche_coherence_verified: true,
  4302	      brand_provenance_verified: false,
  4303	      reason: "SEMANTIC_QA_REJECTED_COHORT_B_MISSING_BRAND_PROVENANCE_SOURCES",
  4304	    };
  4305	  }
  4306	
  4307	  return {
  4308	    passed: true,
  4309	    width,
  4310	    height,
  4311	    size_bytes: buffer.length,
  4312	    pixel_variance: stdDev,
  4313	    has_valid_png_header: true,
  4314	    brand_tokens_verified: brandTokensVerified,
  4315	    semantic_factual_verified: true,
  4316	    niche_coherence_verified: true,
  4317	    brand_provenance_verified: true,
  4318	  };
  4319	}
  4320	
  4321	export function renderProspectConceptHtml(
  4322	  prospect: ProspectRecord,
  4323	  origin: string,
  4324	  customMockupUrl?: string | null
  4325	): string {
  4326	  const esc = (s: string) =>
  4327	    String(s ?? "")
  4328	      .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
  4329	      .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
  4330	      .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
  4331	      .replace(/"/g, "&quot;");
  4332	
  4333	  let mockupSrc = customMockupUrl ? String(customMockupUrl).trim() : "";
  4334	  if (!mockupSrc) {
  4335	    if (prospect.slug.includes("sd-meat-poultry") || prospect.business_name.toLowerCase().includes("s&d")) {
  4336	      mockupSrc = `${origin}/mockups/SD_Poultry_v5.png`;
  4337	    } else {
  4338	      try {
  4339	        const ledger = readOfficeLedger();
  4340	        const lock = ledger.prospect_locks.find(
  4341	          (l) => l.slug === prospect.slug || l.prospect_id === prospect.prospect_id
  4342	        );
  4343	        if (lock?.mockup_content_url) {
  4344	          mockupSrc = lock.mockup_content_url.startsWith("http")
  4345	            ? lock.mockup_content_url
  4346	            : `${origin}${lock.mockup_content_url}`;
  4347	        } else if (lock?.mockup_artifact_sha256) {
  4348	          mockupSrc = `${origin}/api/v1/founder/uploads/${lock.mockup_artifact_sha256}/content`;
  4349	        } else if (prospect.preview_image_sha256) {
  4350	          mockupSrc = `${origin}/api/v1/founder/uploads/${prospect.preview_image_sha256}/content`;
  4351	        }
  4352	      } catch {
  4353	        if (prospect.preview_image_sha256) {
  4354	          mockupSrc = `${origin}/api/v1/founder/uploads/${prospect.preview_image_sha256}/content`;
  4355	        }
  4356	      }
  4357	    }
  4358	  }
  4359	  if (!mockupSrc) {
  4360	    if (prospect.slug.includes("pedmore") || prospect.business_name.toLowerCase().includes("pedmore")) {
  4361	      mockupSrc = `${origin}/mockups/pedmore-roofing-services-wolverhampton-mockup-1440x900.png`;
  4362	    } else if (prospect.slug.includes("rjs") || prospect.business_name.toLowerCase().includes("rjs")) {
  4363	      mockupSrc = `${origin}/mockups/rjs-construction-wednesbury-mockup-1440x900.png`;
  4364	    } else {
  4365	      // A generic prospect must NEVER inherit another business's poultry

  4370	      mockupSrc = "data:image/svg+xml;base64," + Buffer.from(conceptIllustration, "utf8").toString("base64");

  4373	
  4374	  const domainSlug = prospect.slug.replace(/[^a-z0-9-]/g, "") || "preview-concept";

  4391	
  4392	  const billboardItems = [

  4817	
  4818	  <script>
  4819	    const prospectSummary = ${JSON.stringify(prospect.summary || "")};
  4820	    const findings = ${JSON.stringify(prospect.public_findings || [])};
  4821	    const services = ${JSON.stringify(prospect.recommended_services || [])};
  4822	    const businessName = ${JSON.stringify(prospect.business_name)};
  4823	    const prospectSlug = ${JSON.stringify(prospect.slug)};
  4824	    const prospectSecureId = ${JSON.stringify(prospect.secure_id)};
  4825	    const baseApiPath = "/concept/" + encodeURIComponent(prospectSlug) + "/" + encodeURIComponent(prospectSecureId);
  4826	
  4827	    function appendMessage(sender, text) {
  4828	      const container = document.getElementById("chatMessages");
  4829	      const bubble = document.createElement("div");
  4830	      bubble.className = "chat-bubble " + (sender === "kerry" ? "bubble-kerry" : "bubble-user");
  4831	      if (sender === "kerry") {
  4832	        const tag = document.createElement("div");
  4833	        tag.className = "sender-tag";
  4834	        tag.textContent = "Kerry";
  4835	        const body = document.createElement("div");
  4836	        body.textContent = text;
  4837	        bubble.appendChild(tag);
  4838	        bubble.appendChild(body);
  4839	      } else {
  4840	        bubble.textContent = text;
  4841	      }
  4842	      container.appendChild(bubble);
  4843	      container.scrollTop = container.scrollHeight;
  4844	    }
  4845	
  4846	    async function rehydrateHistory() {
  4847	      try {
  4848	        const res = await fetch(baseApiPath + "/context");
  4849	        if (!res.ok) return;
  4850	        const data = await res.json();
  4851	        const history = Array.isArray(data?.conversation_history) ? data.conversation_history : [];
  4852	        for (const turn of history) {
  4853	          if (turn && typeof turn.content === "string") {
  4854	            appendMessage(turn.role === "user" ? "user" : "kerry", turn.content);
  4855	          }
  4856	        }
  4857	      } catch {
  4858	        // Offline fallback
  4859	      }
  4860	    }
  4861	
  4862	    function showKerryTransportError(details) {
  4863	      // This is a transport error, NOT a Kerry chat bubble or an agent reply.
  4864	      const container = document.getElementById("chatMessages");
  4865	      const element = document.createElement("div");
  4866	      element.className = "chat-connection-error";
  4867	      element.setAttribute("role", "status");
  4868	      element.dataset.source = "chat-transport";
  4869	      element.textContent = details;
  4870	      container.appendChild(element);
  4871	      container.scrollTop = container.scrollHeight;
  4872	    }
  4873	
  4874	    function askPreset(text) {
  4875	      document.getElementById("chatInput").value = text;
  4876	      submitUserQuery(text);
  4877	    }
  4878	
  4879	    function handleChatSubmit(e) {
  4880	      e.preventDefault();
  4881	      const input = document.getElementById("chatInput");
  4882	      const text = input.value.trim();
  4883	      if (!text) return;
  4884	      input.value = "";
  4885	      submitUserQuery(text);
  4886	    }
  4887	
  4888	    async function submitUserQuery(query) {
  4889	      appendMessage("user", query);
  4890	      try {
  4891	        const res = await fetch(baseApiPath + "/chat", {
  4892	          method: "POST",
  4893	          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  4894	          body: JSON.stringify({ message: query }),
  4895	        });
  4896	        if (!res.ok) {
  4897	          const requestId = res.headers.get("x-request-id") || "";
  4898	          showKerryTransportError("Kerry connection returned HTTP " + res.status +
  4899	            (requestId ? " (request " + requestId + ")" : "") +
  4900	            ". You can retry your message when the service responds.");
  4901	          return;
  4902	        }
  4903	        const data = await res.json();
  4904	        if (data && typeof data.answer === "string" && data.answer.trim()) {
  4905	          appendMessage("kerry", data.answer);
  4906	          return;
  4907	        }
  4908	        showKerryTransportError("Kerry endpoint returned HTTP " + res.status +
  4909	          " without a usable agent answer. The message has not been answered.");
  4910	      } catch (error) {
  4911	        // Never impersonate Kerry through a canned business-sales response.
  4912	        const reason = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
  4913	        showKerryTransportError("Kerry chat transport error: " + reason);
  4914	      }
  4915	    }
  4916	
  4917	    void rehydrateHistory();
  4918	  </script>
  4919	</body>
  4920	</html>`;
  4921	}
  4922	
  4923	export interface Candidate505Prospect {
  4924	  slug: string;
  4925	  business_name: string;
  4926	  location: string;
  4927	  business_type: string;
  4928	  website: string;
  4929	}
  4930	
  4931	export function resolve505CandidateProspects(
  4932	  runtimeRoot: string,
  4933	  count = 1
  4934	): Candidate505Prospect[] {
  4935	  const candidatePaths = [
  4936	    path.join(process.cwd(), "data", "505_prospects.json"),
  4937	    path.join(path.resolve(runtimeRoot), "data", "505_prospects.json"),
  4938	    path.join(path.resolve(runtimeRoot), "505_prospects.json"),
  4939	  ];
  4940	  let records: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [];
  4941	  for (const p of candidatePaths) {
  4942	    if (existsSync(p)) {
  4943	      try {
  4944	        const parsed = JSON.parse(readFileSync(p, "utf8"));
  4945	        if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0) {
  4946	          records = parsed;
  4947	          break;
  4948	        }
  4949	      } catch {}
  4950	    }
  4951	  }
  4952	  if (!records.length) return [];
  4953	
  4954	  const targetCount = Math.max(1, Math.min(25, Math.floor(Number(count) || 1)));
  4955	  const results: Candidate505Prospect[] = [];
  4956	  try {
  4957	    const ledger = readOfficeLedger(runtimeRoot);
  4958	    const lockedNames = new Set<string>();
  4959	    for (const l of ledger.prospect_locks ?? []) {
  4960	      if (l.business_name) lockedNames.add(String(l.business_name).toLowerCase().trim());
  4961	      if (l.slug) lockedNames.add(String(l.slug).toLowerCase().trim());
  4962	      if (l.prospect_id) lockedNames.add(String(l.prospect_id).toLowerCase().trim());
  4963	    }
  4964	    for (const d of ledger.email_drafts ?? []) {
  4965	      if (d.prospect_id) lockedNames.add(String(d.prospect_id).toLowerCase().trim());
  4966	      if (d.campaign_id) lockedNames.add(String(d.campaign_id).toLowerCase().trim());
  4967	    }
  4968	    for (const t of ledger.tasks ?? []) {
  4969	      if (t.prospect_id) lockedNames.add(String(t.prospect_id).toLowerCase().trim());
  4970	      if (t.title) lockedNames.add(String(t.title).toLowerCase().trim());
  4971	    }
  4972	
  4973	    const smartStore = new SmartSiteStore(runtimeRoot);
  4974	    const revenueService = new V7RevenueService(path.join(path.resolve(runtimeRoot), "v7"));
  4975	    for (const lead of revenueService.listLeads("entreprenuity")) {
  4976	      if (lead.companyName) lockedNames.add(String(lead.companyName).toLowerCase().trim());
  4977	    }
  4978	
  4979	    // Prefer verified corporate entities (Ltd / Limited / LLP / PLC) to satisfy PECR qualification rules
  4980	    const isUnused = (r: Record<string, unknown>) => {
  4981	      const name = String(r.business_name || "").toLowerCase().trim();
  4982	      const slug = name.replace(/[^a-z0-9-]/g, "-").replace(/-+/g, "-").replace(/^-|-$/g, "");
  4983	      const id = String(r.id || r.prospect_id || "").toLowerCase().trim();
  4984	      const website = String(r.website_url || "").toLowerCase().trim();
  4985	      if (!name || lockedNames.has(name) || lockedNames.has(slug)) return false;
  4986	      if (id && lockedNames.has(id)) return false;
  4987	      if (website && lockedNames.has(website)) return false;
  4988	      if (smartStore.getProspect("entreprenuity-business-solutions", slug)) return false;
  4989	      return true;
  4990	    };
  4991	
  4992	    const pushCandidate = (match: Record<string, unknown>) => {
  4993	      const name = String(match.business_name || "").trim();
  4994	      const slug = name.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9-]/g, "-").replace(/-+/g, "-").replace(/^-|-$/g, "") || "fresh-prospect";
  4995	      lockedNames.add(name.toLowerCase());
  4996	      lockedNames.add(slug);
  4997	      results.push({
  4998	        slug,
  4999	        business_name: name,
  5000	        location: String(match.location || "West Midlands, UK").trim(),
  5001	        business_type: String(match.trade_category || "Local Business & Services").trim(),
  5002	        website: match.website_url ? String(match.website_url).trim() : "",
  5003	      });
  5004	    };
  5005	
  5006	    for (const r of records) {
  5007	      if (results.length >= targetCount) break;
  5008	      const name = String(r.business_name || "").trim();
  5009	      const isCorp = /\b(ltd|limited|llp|plc)\b/i.test(name);
  5010	      if (isCorp && isUnused(r)) {
  5011	        pushCandidate(r);
  5012	      }
  5013	    }
  5014	
  5015	    for (const r of records) {
  5016	      if (results.length >= targetCount) break;
  5017	      if (isUnused(r)) {
  5018	        pushCandidate(r);
  5019	      }
  5020	    }
  5021	  } catch {}
  5022	  return results;
  5023	}
  5024	
  5025	export {
  5026	  runDigitalAmbushPipeline,
  5027	  runDigitalAmbushBatch,
  5028	  resolve505CandidateProspect,
  5029	  type DigitalAmbushOutcome,
  5030	  type DigitalAmbushPipelineInput,
  5031	  type DigitalAmbushPipelineResult,
  5032	  type DigitalAmbushBatchItem,
  5033	  type DigitalAmbushBatchInput,
  5034	  type DigitalAmbushBatchResult,
  5035	} from "../pipelines/digital-ambush-runner.js";
  5036	
  5037	export async function runSophieEntreprenuityFactory(
  5038	  input: {
  5039	    product_name?: string;
  5040	    campaign_angle?: string;
  5041	    hook_text?: string;
  5042	    fetcher?: typeof fetch;
  5043	  } = {},
  5044	  runtimeRoot = geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot()
  5045	): Promise<OfficeSophieSocialPackage> {
  5046	  const productName = String(
  5047	    input.product_name ?? "Entreprenuity AI Tools & Founder Workflows"
  5048	  ).trim();
  5049	  const campaignAngle = String(
  5050	    input.campaign_angle ??
  5051	      "Practical AI tools, founder growth, and repeatable business automations"
  5052	  ).trim();
  5053	  const hookText = String(
  5054	    input.hook_text ??
  5055	      "Still spending hours on admin? Try this practical AI workflow for your business."
  5056	  ).trim();
  5057	
  5058	  const taskId = `sophie_ent_${randomUUID().slice(0, 10)}`;
  5059	  const claim = claimOfficeTask(
  5060	    {
  5061	      task_id: taskId,
  5062	      title: `Entreprenuity AI Social Factory: ${productName}`,
  5063	      company_scope: "entreprenuity",
  5064	      owner_agent: "sophie",
  5065	      subagent_id: "sophie-runway",
  5066	      stage: "STAGE_7_SOCIAL_FACTORY_PRODUCTION",
  5067	    },
  5068	    runtimeRoot
  5069	  );
  5070	
  5071	  // Storyboard placeholders here are Sophie media, not Digital Ambush homepage mockups.
  5072	  const mediaStorage = new ArtifactStorageService({ storageDir: founderMediaStorageDir() });
  5073	  const variantSpecs = [
  5074	    { id: "variant_1_hero", label: "AI Workflow Overview" },
  5075	    { id: "variant_2_steps", label: "Founder Automation Walkthrough" },
  5076	    { id: "variant_3_takeaway", label: "Business Growth Takeaway" },
  5077	  ];
  5078	
  5079	  const staticVariants: NonNullable<OfficeSophieSocialPackage["static_variants"]> = [];
  5080	  for (const spec of variantSpecs) {
  5081	    const vBuf = generateBrandedCardPngBuffer({
  5082	      variant: "digital_ambush",
  5083	      seedText: `entreprenuity:${productName}:${campaignAngle}:${spec.id}`,
  5084	    });
  5085	    const storedVar = await mediaStorage.storeArtifact({
  5086	      kind: "founder_media",
  5087	      content: vBuf,
  5088	      content_type: "image/png",
  5089	      metadata: {
  5090	        filename: `entreprenuity-${spec.id}.png`,
  5091	        role: "entreprenuity-social-creative",
  5092	        variant_id: spec.id,
  5093	        company_scope: "entreprenuity",
  5094	        created_by_agent: "sophie",
  5095	        subagents_used: ["sophie-runway", "sophie-tiktok"],
  5096	        immutable: true,
  5097	      },
  5098	    });
  5099	    staticVariants.push({
  5100	      variant_id: spec.id,
  5101	      label: spec.label,
  5102	      artifact_sha256: storedVar.artifact_sha256,
  5103	      content_url: `/api/v1/founder/uploads/${storedVar.artifact_sha256}/content`,
  5104	      size_bytes: storedVar.size_bytes,
  5105	    });
  5106	  }
  5107	
  5108	  // Free, local 9:16 MP4 fallback, explicitly enabled, without Runway credits.
  5109	  // FFmpeg must be installed on the worker. Rendering is not publication and
  5110	  // does not bypass founder approval, licensing, provider auth, or real QA.
  5111	  let finishedVideo: { artifact_sha256: string; content_url: string; size_bytes: number } | null = null;
  5112	  let videoRenderFailure: string | null = null;
  5113	  if (process.env.GEMINX_SOPHIE_LOCAL_VIDEO_ENABLED === "true") {
  5114	    const renderDir = mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "geminx-sophie-"));
  5115	    try {
  5116	      const frames: string[] = [];
  5117	      for (let i = 0; i < staticVariants.length; i++) {
  5118	        const bytes = await mediaStorage.getArtifactContent(staticVariants[i]!.artifact_sha256);
  5119	        if (!bytes || !bytes.length) throw new Error("SOPHIE_INPUT_ARTIFACT_MISSING");
  5120	        const assetPath = path.join(renderDir, `scene-${i}.png`);
  5121	        writeFileSync(assetPath, bytes);
  5122	        frames.push(assetPath);
  5123	      }
  5124	      const outputPath = path.join(renderDir, "sophie-ent-social.mp4");
  5125	      const captions = [
  5126	        hookText,
  5127	        "A repeatable business process can be explained in three clear steps.",
  5128	        "Explore Entreprenuity AI tools and founder workflows.",
  5129	      ];
  5130	      const renderScript = path.resolve(process.cwd(), "scripts/sophie-render-local-video.mjs");
  5131	      if (!existsSync(renderScript)) throw new Error("SOPHIE_VIDEO_RENDER_SCRIPT_MISSING");
  5132	      const stdout = execFileSync(process.execPath, [
  5133	        renderScript,
  5134	        "--frame1", frames[0]!, "--frame2", frames[1]!, "--frame3", frames[2]!,
  5135	        "--line1", captions[0]!, "--line2", captions[1]!, "--line3", captions[2]!,
  5136	        "--output", outputPath,
  5137	      ], { timeout: 90_000, encoding: "utf8", maxBuffer: 256_000 });
  5138	      const renderReceipt = JSON.parse(stdout.trim()) as {
  5139	        status?: string; sha256?: string; width?: number; height?: number;
  5140	      };
  5141	      const videoBytes = readFileSync(outputPath);
  5142	      const realSha = createHash("sha256").update(videoBytes).digest("hex");
  5143	      if (renderReceipt.status !== "RENDERED_VIDEO_NOT_PUBLISHED" ||
  5144	          renderReceipt.width !== 720 || renderReceipt.height !== 1280 ||
  5145	          realSha !== renderReceipt.sha256) {
  5146	        throw new Error("SOPHIE_VIDEO_POST_RENDER_READBACK_FAILED");
  5147	      }
  5148	      const recorded = await mediaStorage.storeArtifact({
  5149	        kind: "founder_media",
  5150	        content: videoBytes,
  5151	        content_type: "video/mp4",
  5152	        metadata: {
  5153	          filename: "entreprenuity-sophie-short.mp4",
  5154	          role: "sophie-local-rendered-video",
  5155	          company_scope: "entreprenuity",
  5156	          created_by_agent: "sophie",
  5157	          immutable: true,
  5158	          status: "RENDERED_NOT_APPROVED_NOT_PUBLISHED",
  5159	        },
  5160	      });
  5161	      finishedVideo = {
  5162	        artifact_sha256: recorded.artifact_sha256,
  5163	        content_url: `/api/v1/founder/uploads/${recorded.artifact_sha256}/content`,
  5164	        size_bytes: recorded.size_bytes,
  5165	      };
  5166	    } catch (error) {
  5167	      // Never fake MP4 success. Keep the original static storyboard draft.
  5168	      videoRenderFailure = error instanceof Error ? error.message.slice(0, 240) : "VIDEO_RENDER_FAILED";
  5169	    } finally {
  5170	      rmSync(renderDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  5171	    }
  5172	  }
  5173	
  5174	  const primaryCreative = staticVariants[0]!;
  5175	  const creativeContentUrl = finishedVideo?.content_url ?? primaryCreative.content_url;
  5176	  const runwayKeyConfigured = Boolean(String(process.env.RUNWAYML_API_SECRET ?? process.env.RUNWAY_API_KEY ?? "").trim());
  5177	  const runwayRouterConfigured = Boolean(String(process.env.GEMINX_RUNWAY_VIDEO_ROUTER_CONFIG_ID ?? "geminx-video-fast").trim());
  5178	  const runwayStatus = !runwayKeyConfigured ? "BLOCKED_MISSING_KEY"
  5179	    : !runwayRouterConfigured ? "BLOCKED_MISSING_VIDEO_ROUTER" : "CONFIGURED_UNVERIFIED";
  5180	  // This account ID is verified, but the Railway worker still requires separate API credentials.
  5181	  // The verified Entreprenuity brand is 6963630. Refuse mismatched brand overrides.
  5182	  const rawBrand = process.env.SOPHIE_ENTREPRENUITY_METRICOOL_BRAND_ID ?? process.env.ENTREPRENUITY_METRICOOL_BRAND_ID;
  5183	  const entreprenuityMetricoolId = String(rawBrand !== undefined ? rawBrand : "6963630").trim();
  5184	  const rawTikTok = process.env.SOPHIE_ENTREPRENUITY_TIKTOK_ACCOUNT;
  5185	  const expectedTikTokAccount = String(rawTikTok !== undefined ? rawTikTok : "Sophie").trim();
  5186	  const oauthToken = await getValidAccessToken(input.fetcher, runtimeRoot);
  5187	  const metricoolApiConfigured = Boolean(oauthToken || String(process.env.METRICOOL_API_TOKEN ?? "").trim());
  5188	  const metricoolStatus = entreprenuityMetricoolId !== "6963630"
  5189	    ? "BLOCKED_METRICOOL_BRAND_UNVERIFIED"
  5190	    : !metricoolApiConfigured
  5191	    ? "BLOCKED_METRICOOL_OAUTH_REQUIRED"
  5192	    : !expectedTikTokAccount
  5193	    ? "BLOCKED_METRICOOL_ACCOUNT_NOT_BOUND"
  5194	    : "BLOCKED_METRICOOL_LIVE_VERIFICATION_REQUIRED";
  5195	  const verifiedMetricoolStatus = metricoolStatus === "BLOCKED_METRICOOL_LIVE_VERIFICATION_REQUIRED"
  5196	    ? await probeSophieMetricoolAccount({
  5197	      apiToken: oauthToken || String(process.env.METRICOOL_API_TOKEN ?? "").trim(),
  5198	      userId: String(process.env.METRICOOL_USER_ID ?? "5371198").trim(),
  5199	      brandId: entreprenuityMetricoolId,
  5200	      expectedTikTokAccount: expectedTikTokAccount,
  5201	      fetcher: input.fetcher,
  5202	      runtimeRoot,
  5203	    })
  5204	    : metricoolStatus;
  5205	  const metricoolConnectionUrl = verifiedMetricoolStatus === "STAGE_READY"
  5206	    ? "/api/v1/auth/metricool/status"
  5207	    : "/api/v1/auth/metricool/connect";
  5208	
  5209	  executeOfficeSubagentDelegation(
  5210	    {
  5211	      parent_agent: "sophie",
  5212	      subagent_id: "sophie-runway",
  5213	      parent_task_id: taskId,
  5214	      company_scope: "entreprenuity",
  5215	      owned_surface: "entreprenuity:creative-variants",
  5216	      objective: `Staged 3 internal storyboard cards and a 9:16 Runway motion brief for ${productName}`,
  5217	      artifact_ids: staticVariants.map((v) => v.artifact_sha256),
  5218	    },
  5219	    runtimeRoot
  5220	  );
  5221	
  5222	  executeOfficeSubagentDelegation(
  5223	    {
  5224	      parent_agent: "sophie",
  5225	      subagent_id: "sophie-tiktok",
  5226	      parent_task_id: taskId,
  5227	      company_scope: "entreprenuity",
  5228	      owned_surface: "entreprenuity:metricool-staging",
  5229	      objective: `Staged TikTok/Reels distribution copy only; Metricool account proof ${verifiedMetricoolStatus}`,
  5230	      artifact_ids: [primaryCreative.artifact_sha256],
  5231	    },
  5232	    runtimeRoot
  5233	  );
  5234	
  5235	  const videoScript = [
  5236	    `[00:00 - 00:03] HOOK: "${hookText}" (Show a real founder workflow example)`,
  5237	    `[00:03 - 00:09] STORY: Explain a repeatable business task and demonstrate only verified AI-tool steps.`,
  5238	    `[00:09 - 00:14] RITUAL: Show the starting process, the automation steps and a clear outcome without invented performance claims.`,
  5239	    `[00:14 - 00:18] CTA: "Explore Entreprenuity AI tools and founder guides via the link in bio."`,
  5240	  ].join("\n");
  5241	
  5242	  const captionWithHashtags = `${hookText} Learn practical founder automations with ${productName}. #EntreprenuityAI #AITools #SmallBusinessUK #FounderWorkflows #BusinessAutomation`;
  5243	
  5244	  const evidenceHash = createHash("sha256")
  5245	    .update(`${finishedVideo?.artifact_sha256 ?? primaryCreative.artifact_sha256}:${captionWithHashtags}`)
  5246	    .digest("hex")
  5247	    .slice(0, 16);
  5248	
  5249	  const mediaTask: OfficeMediaTaskContract = {
  5250	    schema_version: "geminx.media.task.v1",
  5251	    venture_id: "entreprenuity",
  5252	    commercial_owner_type: "owned_brand",
  5253	    campaign_id: "entreprenuity-founder-workflows",
  5254	    product_id: "entreprenuity-ai-workflows",
  5255	    source_asset_refs: finishedVideo
  5256	      ? [finishedVideo.content_url, ...staticVariants.map((v) => v.content_url)]
  5257	      : staticVariants.map((v) => v.content_url),
  5258	    rights_verified: false,
  5259	    modality: "video",
  5260	    aspect_ratio: "9:16",
  5261	    provider_preference: "runway",
  5262	    generation_approval: "PENDING",
  5263	    cost_limit_usd: 0,
  5264	    artifact_sha256: finishedVideo?.artifact_sha256 ?? primaryCreative.artifact_sha256,
  5265	    static_variant_sha256s: staticVariants.map((v) => v.artifact_sha256),
  5266	    qa_status: "NOT_STARTED",
  5267	    publication_approval: "PENDING",
  5268	    metricool_brand_id: verifiedMetricoolStatus === "STAGE_READY" ? entreprenuityMetricoolId : null,
  5269	    metricool_post_id: null,
  5270	    attribution_url: "https://entreprenuity-ai.com/?utm_source=tiktok&utm_medium=sophie_social_factory&utm_campaign=entreprenuity-founder-workflows",
  5271	  };
  5272	
  5273	  let publishReceipt: MetricoolPublishReceipt | undefined;
  5274	  if (verifiedMetricoolStatus === "STAGE_READY" && finishedVideo) {
  5275	    publishReceipt = await stageMetricoolPost({
  5276	      brandId: entreprenuityMetricoolId,
  5277	      userId: String(process.env.METRICOOL_USER_ID ?? "").trim(),
  5278	      apiToken: String(process.env.METRICOOL_API_TOKEN ?? "").trim(),
  5279	      caption: captionWithHashtags,
  5280	      artifactSha256: finishedVideo.artifact_sha256,
  5281	      networks: ["tiktok"],
  5282	      founderApproved: false, // Strict founder gate: hold in PENDING_FOUNDER_APPROVAL
  5283	      fetcher: input.fetcher,
  5284	    });
  5285	  }
  5286	
  5287	  const videoRouterConfigId = String(process.env.GEMINX_RUNWAY_VIDEO_ROUTER_CONFIG_ID ?? "geminx-video-fast").trim() || "geminx-video-fast";
  5288	  const stagedTikTokDraft: OfficeStagedTikTokDraft = {
  5289	    status: finishedVideo ? "STAGED_RENDERED_VIDEO_PENDING_APPROVAL" : "STAGED_DRAFT_PENDING_APPROVAL",
  5290	    media_artifact_sha256: finishedVideo?.artifact_sha256 ?? primaryCreative.artifact_sha256,
  5291	    media_url: creativeContentUrl,
  5292	    caption: captionWithHashtags,
  5293	    aspect_ratio: "9:16",
  5294	    auto_publish: false,
  5295	    estimated_cost_usd: 0,
  5296	    video_router_config_id: videoRouterConfigId,
  5297	    staged_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  5298	  };
  5299	
  5300	  const pkg: OfficeSophieSocialPackage = {
  5301	    package_id: `ent_social_${randomUUID().slice(0, 10)}`,
  5302	    company_scope: "entreprenuity",
  5303	    owner_agent: "sophie",
  5304	    product_name: productName,
  5305	    campaign_angle: campaignAngle,
  5306	    hook_text: hookText,
  5307	    video_script: videoScript,
  5308	    caption_with_hashtags: captionWithHashtags,
  5309	    compliance_verified: false,
  5310	    compliance_notes: [
  5311	      "Draft social copy and storyboard placeholders staged; compliance and rendered-video QA have NOT been executed.",
  5312	      "Scoped to Entreprenuity social content, excluding Digital Ambush CRM and customer outreach.",
  5313	      "3 INTERNAL storyboard placeholders stored and 1 video specification staged (cost_limit_usd=0 until paid generation approved); these are not finished media.",
  5314	      "TikTok publishing gated truthfully behind Metricool social connection.",
  5315	      finishedVideo ? "Real offline 9:16 MP4 video rendered and SHA-256 read back; independent visual/compliance QA and publication are pending."
  5316	        : videoRenderFailure ? `Local video renderer failed: ${videoRenderFailure}`
  5317	        : "Local video rendering is disabled by default; no MP4 generation took place.",
  5318	    ],
  5319	    creative_artifact_sha256: finishedVideo?.artifact_sha256 ?? primaryCreative.artifact_sha256,
  5320	    creative_content_url: creativeContentUrl,
  5321	    creative_content_type: finishedVideo ? "video/mp4" : "image/png",
  5322	    creative_size_bytes: finishedVideo?.size_bytes ?? primaryCreative.size_bytes,
  5323	    local_video_status: finishedVideo ? "RENDERED_VIDEO_NOT_PUBLISHED"
  5324	      : videoRenderFailure ? "RENDER_FAILED" : "DISABLED",
  5325	    static_variants: staticVariants,
  5326	    media_task: mediaTask,
  5327	    staged_tiktok_draft: stagedTikTokDraft,
  5328	    runway_status: runwayStatus,
  5329	    tiktok_publish_status: verifiedMetricoolStatus,
  5330	    metricool_connection_url: metricoolConnectionUrl,
  5331	    metricool_publish_receipt: publishReceipt,
  5332	    evidence_hash: evidenceHash,
  5333	    created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  5334	  };
  5335	
  5336	  completeOfficeTask(
  5337	    {
  5338	      task_id: taskId,
  5339	      owner_agent: "sophie",
  5340	      lease_token: claim.lease_token!,
  5341	      status: "BLOCKED",
  5342	      evidence_hash: evidenceHash,
  5343	      artifact_urls: finishedVideo ? [finishedVideo.content_url, ...staticVariants.map((v) => v.content_url)] : staticVariants.map((v) => v.content_url),
  5344	      summary: `Sophie staged three internal storyboard frames${finishedVideo ? " and rendered a verified MP4 (UNPUBLISHED)" : ""}; Metricool broker ${verifiedMetricoolStatus}. Target publishing has not happened; task remains BLOCKED pending approval, real media QA and provider receipts.`,
  5345	    },
  5346	    runtimeRoot
  5347	  );
  5348	
  5349	  const ledger = readOfficeLedger(runtimeRoot);
  5350	  ledger.sophie_social_packages = [...ledger.sophie_social_packages.slice(-9999), pkg];
  5351	  ledger.media_tasks = [...ledger.media_tasks.slice(-9999), mediaTask];
  5352	  const approvalExists = ledger.pending_approvals.some(
  5353	    (a) => (a.kind === "SOCIAL_PUBLISH_CONNECTION" || a.kind === "SOCIAL_CONTENT_PUBLISH") && a.company_scope === "entreprenuity"
  5354	  );
  5355	  if (!approvalExists && verifiedMetricoolStatus !== "STAGE_READY") {
  5356	    ledger.pending_approvals.push({
  5357	      approval_id: `appr_ent_tiktok_${randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`,
  5358	      company_scope: "entreprenuity",
  5359	      prospect_id: null,
  5360	      requested_by: "sophie",
  5361	      gate_owner: "founder",
  5362	      kind: "SOCIAL_PUBLISH_CONNECTION",
  5363	      status: "PENDING",
  5364	      summary:
  5365	        "METRICOOL_API_SETUP_REQUIRED: " + verifiedMetricoolStatus + ". Check Entreprenuity brand 6963630 and TikTok identity, then authorise scoped Metricool API. ChatGPT-connected TikTok does not grant the Railway worker access.",
  5366	      action_url: metricoolConnectionUrl,
  5367	      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  5368	    });
  5369	  } else if (!ledger.pending_approvals.some(a => a.kind === "SOCIAL_CONTENT_PUBLISH" && a.prospect_id === pkg.package_id)) {
  5370	    ledger.pending_approvals.push({
  5371	      approval_id: `appr_soc_${randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`,
  5372	      company_scope: "entreprenuity",
  5373	      prospect_id: pkg.package_id,
  5374	      requested_by: "sophie",
  5375	      gate_owner: "founder",
  5376	      kind: "SOCIAL_CONTENT_PUBLISH",
  5377	      status: "PENDING",
  5378	      summary: `Approve Metricool TikTok & YouTube Shorts publishing for ${productName} (video sha: ${pkg.creative_artifact_sha256.slice(0, 12)}...)`,
  5379	      action_url: `/api/v1/media-export/${pkg.creative_artifact_sha256}`,
  5380	      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  5381	    });
  5382	  }
  5383	  writeOfficeLedger(ledger, runtimeRoot);
  5384	
  5385	  return pkg;
  5386	}
