  5391	export interface CertifySophieSocialPackageInput {
  5392	  package_id: string;
  5393	  reviewer_agent?: string;
  5394	  visual_qa_notes?: string;
  5395	  rights_confirmed: boolean;
  5396	  compliance_passed: boolean;
  5397	  runtimeRoot?: string;
  5398	}
  5399	
  5400	export function certifySophieSocialPackage(input: CertifySophieSocialPackageInput): {
  5401	  ok: boolean;
  5402	  package?: OfficeSophieSocialPackage;
  5403	  error?: string;
  5404	  blocked_receipt?: SophieBlockedPublishReceipt;
  5405	} {
  5406	  const ledger = readOfficeLedger(input.runtimeRoot);
  5407	  const pkg = ledger.sophie_social_packages.find((p) => p.package_id === input.package_id);
  5408	
  5409	  if (!pkg) {
  5410	    return {
  5411	      ok: false,
  5412	      error: "SOCIAL_PACKAGE_NOT_FOUND",
  5413	      blocked_receipt: {
  5414	        package_id: input.package_id,
  5415	        task_id: null,
  5416	        artifact_sha256: null,
  5417	        current_state: "NOT_FOUND",
  5418	        blocked_code: "SOCIAL_PACKAGE_NOT_FOUND",
  5419	        blocker_owner: "engineer",
  5420	        missing_prerequisites: ["valid_package_id"],
  5421	        next_action_label: "Stage social package before certifying",
  5422	        next_action_url: null,
  5423	        retryable: false,
  5424	        checked_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  5425	        correlation_id: `certify_${input.package_id}`,
  5426	        provider_status: null,
  5427	      },
  5428	    };
  5429	  }
  5430	
  5431	  const missing: string[] = [];
  5432	  if (pkg.creative_content_type !== "video/mp4") {
  5433	    missing.push("creative_content_type must be video/mp4");
  5434	  }
  5435	  if (!pkg.creative_artifact_sha256 || !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(pkg.creative_artifact_sha256)) {
  5436	    missing.push("valid 64-char hex creative_artifact_sha256 required");
  5437	  }
  5438	  if ((pkg.creative_size_bytes ?? 0) <= 0) {
  5439	    missing.push("creative_size_bytes must be positive");
  5440	  }
  5441	  if (!input.rights_confirmed) {
  5442	    missing.push("commercial rights confirmation required");
  5443	  }
  5444	  if (!input.compliance_passed) {
  5445	    missing.push("copy compliance verification required");
  5446	  }
  5447	
  5448	  if (missing.length > 0) {
  5449	    const blockedReceipt: SophieBlockedPublishReceipt = {
  5450	      package_id: pkg.package_id,
  5451	      task_id: pkg.runway_task_id ?? null,
  5452	      artifact_sha256: pkg.creative_artifact_sha256,
  5453	      current_state: "QA_REJECTED",
  5454	      blocked_code: "QA_CERTIFICATION_FAILED",
  5455	      blocker_owner: "compliance",
  5456	      missing_prerequisites: missing,
  5457	      next_action_label: "Resolve missing compliance and media requirements before re-certifying",
  5458	      next_action_url: null,
  5459	      retryable: true,
  5460	      checked_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  5461	      correlation_id: `certify_${pkg.package_id}`,
  5462	      provider_status: null,
  5463	    };
  5464	    pkg.blocked_receipt = blockedReceipt;
  5465	    writeOfficeLedger(ledger, input.runtimeRoot);
  5466	    appendOfficeActivityEvent(ledger, {
  5467	      venture_id: pkg.company_scope,
  5468	      actor_agent: "sophie",
  5469	      phase: "SOCIAL_POST_FAILED",
  5470	      status: "BLOCKED",
  5471	      label: `Compliance QA certification rejected for ${pkg.package_id}: ${missing.join(", ")}`,
  5472	    });
  5473	    return { ok: false, error: "QA_CERTIFICATION_FAILED", blocked_receipt: blockedReceipt };
  5474	  }
  5475	
  5476	  // Pass compliance QA
  5477	  pkg.compliance_verified = true;
  5478	  if (pkg.media_task) {
  5479	    pkg.media_task.qa_status = "PASSED_COMPLIANCE_QA";
  5480	    pkg.media_task.rights_verified = true;
  5481	  }
  5482	  if (pkg.staged_tiktok_draft) {
  5483	    pkg.staged_tiktok_draft.status = "STAGED_RENDERED_VIDEO_PENDING_APPROVAL";
  5484	  }
  5485	  const note = `Visual and compliance QA certified by ${input.reviewer_agent || "founder"} at ${new Date().toISOString()}: ${input.visual_qa_notes || "All media and copy checks passed"}`;
  5486	  pkg.compliance_notes.push(note);
  5487	  pkg.blocked_receipt = undefined;
  5488	
  5489	  appendOfficeActivityEvent(ledger, {
  5490	    venture_id: pkg.company_scope,
  5491	    actor_agent: "sophie",
  5492	    phase: "SOCIAL_PACKAGE_QA_CERTIFIED",
  5493	    status: "COMPLETED_PROVEN",
  5494	    label: `Sophie social package ${pkg.package_id} certified publish-ready (QA passed, media rights verified)`,
  5495	  });
  5496	
  5497	  writeOfficeLedger(ledger, input.runtimeRoot);
  5498	  return { ok: true, package: pkg };
  5499	}

  5528	    writable_company_scopes: string[];

  5567	    assets_ready: number;
  5568	    qa_approved: number;
  5569	    canary_simulated: number;
  5570	    approved_to_send: number;
  5571	    provider_accepted: number;
  5572	    sent: number;
  5573	    delivered: number;
  5574	    bounced: number;
  5575	    replied: number;
  5576	    manual_review: number;

  5578	  channel_health: Record<string, string>;

  5586	    system_operator: boolean;

  5604	      {
  5605	        sender_id: "gmail" | "zoho";
  5606	        cohort: "COHORT_A_NO_WEBSITE" | "COHORT_B_LEGACY_REDESIGN";

  5625	        bounces: number;
  5626	        positive_replies: number;
  5627	        meetings_or_quotes_requested: number;
  5628	        revenue_closed_gbp: number;
  5629	      }
  5630	    >;
  5631	  };

  5633	
  5634	export function buildOfficeFloorSnapshot(
  5635	  options: {
  5636	    companyScope?: string | null;
  5637	    runtimeRoot?: string;
  5638	  } = {}
  5639	): OfficeFloorSnapshot {
  5640	  const runtimeRoot = options.runtimeRoot ?? geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot();
  5641	  const filterScope = options.companyScope
  5642	    ? normalizeCompanyScope(options.companyScope)
  5643	    : "holding";
  5644	  const ledger = readOfficeLedger(runtimeRoot);
  5645	  const dual = resolveDualRuntimeSelection();
  5646	  const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
  5647	
  5648	  const smartSiteStore = new SmartSiteStore(runtimeRoot);
  5649	  const prospects = smartSiteStore.listProspects("entreprenuity-business-solutions");
  5650	
  5651	  const scopeMatches = (itemScope: string) =>
  5652	    filterScope === "holding" || itemScope === "holding" || itemScope === filterScope;
  5653	
  5654	  const filteredTasks = ledger.tasks.filter((t) => scopeMatches(t.company_scope)).slice(-40);
  5655	  const filteredCheckpoints = ledger.task_checkpoints.filter(c => scopeMatches(c.company_scope)).slice(-100);
  5656	  const filteredLocks = ledger.prospect_locks
  5657	    .filter((l) => scopeMatches(l.company_scope))
  5658	    .slice(-40);
  5659	  const filteredHandoffs = ledger.handoffs
  5660	    .filter((h) => scopeMatches(h.company_scope))
  5661	    .slice(-40);
  5662	  const filteredReceipts = ledger.receipts
  5663	    .filter((r) => scopeMatches(r.company_scope))
  5664	    .slice(-40);
  5665	  const filteredDelegations = ledger.delegation_receipts
  5666	    .filter((d) => scopeMatches(d.company_scope))
  5667	    .slice(-40);
  5668	  const filteredEvents = ledger.activity_events
  5669	    .filter((e) => scopeMatches(e.venture_id))
  5670	    .slice(-50);
  5671	  const filteredEmailDrafts = ledger.email_drafts
  5672	    .filter((d) => scopeMatches(d.company_scope))
  5673	    .slice(-30);
  5674	  const filteredMediaTasks = ledger.media_tasks
  5675	    .filter((m) => scopeMatches(m.venture_id))
  5676	    .slice(-30);
  5677	  const filteredPackages = ledger.wild_botanics_packages
  5678	    .filter((p) => scopeMatches(p.company_scope))
  5679	    .slice(-20);
  5680	  const filteredSophiePackages = ledger.sophie_social_packages
  5681	    .filter((p) => scopeMatches(p.company_scope))
  5682	    .slice(-20);
  5683	  const filteredApprovals = ledger.pending_approvals.filter((a) =>
  5684	    scopeMatches(a.company_scope)
  5685	  );
  5686	  const filteredIncidents = ledger.incidents.filter(
  5687	    (i) => i.status !== "RESOLVED_VERIFIED" && scopeMatches(i.company_scope)
  5688	  );
  5689	  const recentlyResolvedIncidents = ledger.incidents.filter(
  5690	    (i) => i.status === "RESOLVED_VERIFIED" && scopeMatches(i.company_scope) &&
  5691	      Date.parse(i.updated_at) > Date.now() - 60 * 60 * 1000
  5692	  ).slice(-12);
  5693	
  5694	  const canonical = buildCanonicalOperatorSnapshot();
  5695	  const zohoReady = canonical.channels.zoho_mail;
  5696	  const gmailReady = canonical.channels.gmail;
  5697	  const runwayReady = Boolean(String(process.env.RUNWAYML_API_SECRET ?? process.env.RUNWAY_API_KEY ?? "").trim());
  5698	  const oauthStatus = getSafeOAuthStatus(runtimeRoot);
  5699	  const metricoolChannelState: "MANUAL_SOCIAL_CONNECTION_REQUIRED" | "API_CONFIGURED_UNVERIFIED" | "CONNECTED" =
  5700	    oauthStatus.connected
  5701	      ? "CONNECTED"
  5702	      : String(process.env.METRICOOL_API_TOKEN ?? "").trim() && /^\d+$/.test(String(process.env.METRICOOL_USER_ID ?? "").trim())
  5703	      ? "API_CONFIGURED_UNVERIFIED"
  5704	      : "MANUAL_SOCIAL_CONNECTION_REQUIRED";
  5705	  const browserConnected = canonical.channels.browser_operator;
  5706	  const systemConnected = canonical.channels.system_operator;
  5707	
  5708	  const nowMs = Date.now();
  5709	  const activeTasks = filteredTasks.filter(
  5710	    (t) => (t.status === "IN_PROGRESS" || t.status === "HANDED_OFF") && Date.parse(t.lease_expires_at) > nowMs
  5711	  );
  5712	
  5713	  const researchedCount =
  5714	    filterScope === "wild-botanics"
  5715	      ? 0
  5716	      : Math.max(prospects.length, filteredLocks.length);
  5717	  const conceptReadyCount = filteredLocks.filter((l) => Boolean(l.concept_url)).length;
  5718	  const assetsReadyCount =
  5719	    filterScope === "wild-botanics"
  5720	      ? filteredPackages.length
  5721	      : filteredLocks.filter((l) => Boolean(l.mockup_artifact_sha256)).length;
  5722	  const qaApprovedCount = filteredLocks.filter((l) => Boolean(l.qa_receipt_id)).length;
  5723	  const canarySimulatedCount = filteredLocks.filter(
  5724	    (l) => l.stage === "INTERNAL_CANARY_SIMULATED"
  5725	  ).length;
  5726	  const approvedToSendCount = filteredLocks.filter(
  5727	    (l) => l.stage === "APPROVED_TO_SEND"
  5728	  ).length;
  5729	  const providerAcceptedCount = filteredLocks.filter(
  5730	    (l) => l.stage === "PROVIDER_ACCEPTED"
  5731	  ).length;
  5732	  const deliveredCount = filteredLocks.filter((l) => l.stage === "DELIVERED").length;
  5733	  const sentCount = filteredLocks.filter((l) => l.stage === "SENT").length;
  5734	  const bouncedCount = filteredLocks.filter((l) => l.stage === "BOUNCED").length;
  5735	  const repliedCount = filteredLocks.filter((l) => l.stage === "REPLIED").length;
  5736	
  5737	  const HEARTBEAT_TTL_MS = 120_000;
  5738	  const agents = OFFICE_AGENT_IDS.map((agentId) => {
  5739	    const manifest = OFFICE_AGENT_MANIFESTS[agentId];
  5740	    const state = ledger.agent_states[agentId] ?? {
  5741	      status: "IDLE" as const,
  5742	      runtime_used: dual.runtime_used,
  5743	      company_scope: manifest.primary_company_scope,
  5744	      current_task_id: null,
  5745	      current_prospect_id: null,
  5746	      active_subagents: [],
  5747	      activity_label: "Standing by on Office Floor",
  5748	      blocked_reason: null,
  5749	      last_receipt_hash: null,
  5750	      last_active_at: null,
  5751	      heartbeat_at: null,
  5752	      heartbeat_provenance: null,
  5753	    };
  5754	    const lastReceiptForAgent = [...ledger.receipts]
  5755	      .reverse()
  5756	      .find((r) => r.agent_id === agentId);
  5757	
  5758	    const heartbeatTime = state.heartbeat_at ? Date.parse(state.heartbeat_at) : 0;
  5759	    const isHeartbeatFresh = heartbeatTime > 0 && (nowMs - heartbeatTime) < HEARTBEAT_TTL_MS;
  5760	
  5761	    let presence: "CONNECTED" | "STANDBY" | "STALE" | "DISCONNECTED" | "BLOCKED" = "STANDBY";
  5762	    if (state.status === "BLOCKED" || state.status === "ESCALATED_TO_AG") {
  5763	      presence = "BLOCKED";
  5764	    } else if (isHeartbeatFresh || agentId === "geminx") {
  5765	      presence = "CONNECTED";
  5766	    } else if (heartbeatTime > 0) {
  5767	      presence = (nowMs - heartbeatTime) < (HEARTBEAT_TTL_MS * 5) ? "STALE" : "DISCONNECTED";
  5768	    } else {
  5769	      presence = "STANDBY";
  5770	    }
  5771	
  5772	    const hasUnexpiredLease = activeTasks.some(
  5773	      (t) => t.owner_agent === agentId && Date.parse(t.lease_expires_at) > nowMs
  5774	    );
  5775	    const workState: OfficeFloorSnapshot["agents"][number]["work_state"] =
  5776	      state.status === "WORKING" && hasUnexpiredLease
  5777	        ? "WORKING"
  5778	        : state.status === "WAITING_HANDOFF"
  5779	          ? "WAITING_FOR_APPROVAL"
  5780	          : state.status === "BLOCKED" || state.status === "ESCALATED_TO_AG"
  5781	            ? "BLOCKED"
  5782	            : "IDLE";
  5783	    return {
  5784	      agent_id: agentId,
  5785	      display_name: manifest.display_name,
  5786	      badge_label: manifest.badge_label,
  5787	      role_title: manifest.role_title,
  5788	      department: manifest.department,
  5789	      runtime: state.runtime_used,
  5790	      presence,
  5791	      work_state: workState,
  5792	      status: state.status,
  5793	      runtime_used: state.runtime_used,
  5794	      company_scope: state.company_scope,
  5795	      readable_company_scopes: manifest.readable_company_scopes,
  5796	      writable_company_scopes: manifest.writable_company_scopes,
  5797	      current_task_id: state.current_task_id,
  5798	      current_prospect_id: state.current_prospect_id,
  5799	      activity_label:
  5800	        state.activity_label ||
  5801	        (lastReceiptForAgent
  5802	          ? lastReceiptForAgent.summary.slice(0, 140)
  5803	          : `Ready (${manifest.badge_label})`),
  5804	      activity_at: state.last_active_at,
  5805	      last_receipt_id: lastReceiptForAgent?.receipt_id ?? null,

  6001	export function formatOfficeFloorHudForAgent(
  6002	  agentId: unknown,
  6003	  companyScope?: string | null
  6004	): string {
  6005	  const normalizedAgent = normalizeOfficeAgentId(agentId, "neo");
  6006	  const manifest = OFFICE_AGENT_MANIFESTS[normalizedAgent];
  6007	  const scope = normalizeCompanyScope(companyScope, normalizedAgent);
  6008	  const snapshot = buildOfficeFloorSnapshot({ companyScope: scope });
  6009	
  6010	  const colleaguesLine = snapshot.agents
  6011	    .map(
  6012	      (a) =>
  6013	        `${a.display_name}(${a.agent_id}):${a.status}${a.current_prospect_id ? `[${a.current_prospect_id}]` : ""}`
  6014	    )
  6015	    .join(" | ");
  6016	
  6017	  const subagentsLine =
  6018	    manifest.subagents.length > 0
  6019	      ? manifest.subagents.map((s) => `${s.id} (${s.role})`).join(", ")
  6020	      : "None (Direct execution / Department delegation)";
  6021	
  6022	  return [
  6023	    `[AI_OFFICE_FLOOR_SNAPSHOT v1 | active_agent=${manifest.display_name} (${normalizedAgent}) | role="${manifest.role_title}" | company_scope=${scope} | runtime=${snapshot.dual_runtime.active_runtime}]`,
  6024	    `IDENTITY & ROLE MANDATE: ${manifest.system_persona_summary}`,
  6025	    `SUB-AGENTS AVAILABLE: ${subagentsLine}`,
  6026	    `COLLEAGUE STATUS: ${colleaguesLine}`,
  6027	    `PIPELINE COUNTERS (${scope}): researched=${snapshot.pipeline_counters.researched} concept_ready=${snapshot.pipeline_counters.concept_ready} assets_ready=${snapshot.pipeline_counters.assets_ready} qa_approved=${snapshot.pipeline_counters.qa_approved} canary_simulated=${snapshot.pipeline_counters.canary_simulated} external_sent=${snapshot.pipeline_counters.sent} manual_review=${snapshot.pipeline_counters.manual_review} open_incidents=${snapshot.open_incidents.length}`,
  6028	    `ESCALATION RULE: Operating agents (neo, brian, mark, kerry, sophie, ads) are forbidden from editing runtime code or running unrestricted shell commands. If a tool or route fails, call 'escalate_to_ag' so AG Sentinel repairs and verifies it.`,
  6029	  ].join("\n");
  6030	}

     8	/**
     9	 * Durable Companion conversation state.
    10	 *
    11	 * This is NOT a second memory backend or chat control plane. It is a tiny,
    12	 * bounded, file-backed store that reuses the same discipline as GeminxRuntimeStore
    13	 * (atomic writes, safe runtime ids, stored-identity verification) so a Companion
    14	 * conversation keeps a durable conversation_id + active run/task binding that
    15	 * survives a browser refresh/reopen. Conversation history is bounded so a
    16	 * follow-up like "are you building it right now?" can be resolved against the
    17	 * active Roblox/Creator task instead of being treated as a fresh, stateless prompt.
    18	 */
    19

    22	const DEFAULT_MAX_MESSAGE_CHARS = 6_000;

    78	/** No native tool-call group may be replayed without exactly one matching tool
    79	 * receipt. A missing result after a crash is an unknown outcome, not success.
    80	 */
    81	export function unresolvedNativeToolCallIds(messages: readonly ExecutionTranscriptMessage[]): string[] {
    82	  const missing: string[] = [];
    83	  for (let i = 0; i < messages.length; i += 1) {
    84	    const message = messages[i]!;
    85	    if (message.role !== "assistant" || !Array.isArray(message.tool_calls)) continue;
    86	    const toolIds = message.tool_calls.map((call) => String(call.id ?? "")).filter(Boolean);
    87	    for (const id of toolIds) {
    88	      const receipts = messages.slice(i + 1).filter((candidate) => candidate.role === "tool" && candidate.tool_call_id === id);
    89	      if (receipts.length !== 1) missing.push(id);
    90	    }
    91	  }
    92	  return [...new Set(missing)];
    93	}
    94	
    95	function scrubNativeToolArguments(value: unknown, depth = 0, sensitivePayload = false): unknown {
    96	  if (depth > 16) throw new Error("GEMINX_EXECUTION_CHECKPOINT_ARGUMENT_DEPTH_EXCEEDED");
    97	  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map((item) => scrubNativeToolArguments(item, depth + 1, sensitivePayload));
    98	  if (value && typeof value === "object") {
    99	    return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>).map(([key, entry]) => [
   100	      key,
   101	      /(?:secret|password|access_token|refresh_token|api_key|cookie|authorization|credential|private_key)/i.test(key)
   102	        ? "[REDACTED_SECRET_FIELD]"
   103	        : sensitivePayload && /^(?:content|body|value|text|password|file_contents)$/i.test(key)
   104	          ? "[REDACTED_MUTATION_CONTENT]"
   105	          : scrubNativeToolArguments(entry, depth + 1, sensitivePayload)
   106	    ]));
   107	  }
   108	  return typeof value === "string" ? redactDurableMemoryText(value, 8_000) : value;
   109	}
   110	
   111	function safeNativeToolArguments(raw: string, toolName: string): string {
   112	  const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as unknown;
   113	  if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== "object" || Array.isArray(parsed)) {
   114	    throw new Error("GEMINX_EXECUTION_CHECKPOINT_ARGUMENT_SCHEMA_INVALID");
   115	  }
   116	  const sensitivePayload = /^(?:geminx_scratch_(?:write|delete)|system_(?:file|shell|desktop)|browser_|chrome_|device_|ag_live_)/.test(toolName);
   117	  return JSON.stringify(scrubNativeToolArguments(parsed, 0, sensitivePayload));
   118	}
   119	
   120	function safeNativeToolReceipt(raw: string): string {
   121	  let parsed: unknown;
   122	  try { parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as unknown; }
   123	  catch { throw new Error("GEMINX_EXECUTION_CHECKPOINT_RECEIPT_JSON_INVALID"); }
   124	  if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== "object" || Array.isArray(parsed)) {
   125	    throw new Error("GEMINX_EXECUTION_CHECKPOINT_RECEIPT_JSON_INVALID");
   126	  }
   127	  const safe = JSON.stringify(scrubNativeToolArguments(parsed));
   128	  // Never truncate native JSON in the middle; that corrupts proof and makes a
   129	  // previously completed tool appear missing. Block and reconcile instead.
   130	  if (safe.length > 16_000) throw new Error("GEMINX_EXECUTION_CHECKPOINT_RECEIPT_OVERSIZED");
   131	  return safe;
   132	}

   178	export function sanitizeConversationTitle(raw: unknown): string {
   179	  if (typeof raw !== "string") throw new Error("INVALID_TITLE_TYPE");
   180	  const title = raw.replace(/[\x00-\x1f\x7f]+/g, " ").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
   181	  if (!title) throw new Error("EMPTY_CONVERSATION_TITLE");
   182	  if (title.length > 120) throw new Error("CONVERSATION_TITLE_TOO_LONG");
   183	  return title;
   184	}
   185	
   186	export function deriveConversationTitle(record: ConversationRecord): string {
   187	  if (typeof record.title === "string" && record.title.trim()) return record.title;
   188	  const first = record.turns.find(turn => turn.role === "user")?.content?.trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
   189	  return first?.slice(0, 90) || "New GeminX chat";
   190	}

   204	function ensureDirectory(targetPath: string): void {
   205	  mkdirSync(targetPath, { recursive: true });
   206	}
   207	
   208	function readJsonFile<T>(filePath: string): T | null {
   209	  try {
   210	    return JSON.parse(readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")) as T;
   211	  } catch (error) {
   212	    const code = (error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code;
   213	    if (code === "ENOENT") return null;
   214	
   215	    throw new Error(
   216	      `Conversation state is unreadable or corrupt at ${filePath}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`
   217	    );
   218	  }
   219	}
   220	
   221	function atomicWrite(filePath: string, content: string): void {
   222	  ensureDirectory(path.dirname(filePath));
   223	  const tempPath = `${filePath}.${process.pid}.${randomUUID()}.tmp`;
   224	
   225	  try {
   226	    writeFileSync(tempPath, content, { encoding: "utf8", flag: "wx" });
   227	    renameSync(tempPath, filePath);
   228	  } catch (error) {
   229	    rmSync(tempPath, { force: true });
   230	    throw error;
   231	  }
   232	}
   233	
   234	function assertSafeRuntimeId(label: string, value: string): string {
   235	  const hasWindowsUnsafeFilenameCharacter = /[<>:"|?*\u0000-\u001f]/u.test(value);
   236	  if (
   237	    value.length === 0 ||
   238	    value === "." ||
   239	    value === ".." ||
   240	    value.includes("/") ||
   241	    value.includes("\\") ||
   242	    hasWindowsUnsafeFilenameCharacter
   243	  ) {
   244	    throw new Error(`${label} contains an unsafe runtime path identifier.`);
   245	  }
   246	  return value;
   247	}
   248	
   249	function assertStoredIdentity(label: string, requestedId: string, storedId: unknown, filePath: string): void {
   250	  if (typeof storedId !== "string" || storedId !== requestedId) {
   251	    throw new Error(
   252	      `Conversation state identity mismatch at ${filePath}: expected ${label} ${requestedId}, found ${String(storedId)}`
   253	    );
   254	  }
   255	}
   256	
   257	function readIndexedRecord<T extends { conversation_id: string }>(root: string, entry: string): T | null {
   258	  const filePath = path.join(root, entry);
   259	  const record = readJsonFile<T>(filePath);
   260	  if (record === null) return null;
   261	  const requestedId = entry.slice(0, -".json".length);
   262	  assertStoredIdentity("conversation id", requestedId, record.conversation_id, filePath);
   263	  return record;
   264	}
   265	
   266	export class ConversationStore {
   267	  readonly conversationsRoot: string;
   268	  readonly executionTranscriptsRoot: string;
   269	  private readonly maxTurns: number;
   270	  private readonly maxSummaryChars: number;
   271	  private readonly maxMessageChars: number;
   272	  private readonly memoryStore: DurableMemoryStore;
   273	
   274	  constructor(options: ConversationStoreOptions = {}) {
   275	    const root = path.resolve(options.root ?? geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot());
   276	    this.conversationsRoot = path.join(root, "conversations");
   277	    this.executionTranscriptsRoot = path.join(root, "execution-transcripts");
   278	    this.maxTurns = Math.max(2, Math.min(options.maxTurns ?? DEFAULT_MAX_TURNS, 40));
   279	    this.maxSummaryChars = Math.max(40, options.maxSummaryChars ?? DEFAULT_MAX_SUMMARY_CHARS);
   280	    this.maxMessageChars = Math.max(100, options.maxMessageChars ?? DEFAULT_MAX_MESSAGE_CHARS);
   281	    this.memoryStore = options.memoryStore ?? new DurableMemoryStore({ root });
   282	    ensureDirectory(this.conversationsRoot);
   283	    ensureDirectory(this.executionTranscriptsRoot);
   284	  }
   285	
   286	  executionCheckpointPath(objectiveId: string): string {
   287	    return path.join(this.executionTranscriptsRoot, `${assertSafeRuntimeId("objectiveId", objectiveId)}.json`);
   288	  }
   289	
   290	  getExecutionCheckpoint(objectiveId: string): ExecutionTranscriptCheckpoint | null {
   291	    const filePath = this.executionCheckpointPath(objectiveId);
   292	    const value = readJsonFile<ExecutionTranscriptCheckpoint>(filePath);
   293	    if (value === null) return null;
   294	    if (value.schema_version !== "geminx.execution-transcript.v1" || value.objective_id !== objectiveId) {
   295	      throw new Error("GEMINX_EXECUTION_CHECKPOINT_IDENTITY_INVALID");
   296	    }
   297	    if (!Array.isArray(value.messages) ||
   298	        !["CLEAN", "PENDING_TOOL", "TERMINAL"].includes(value.state)) {
   299	      throw new Error("GEMINX_EXECUTION_CHECKPOINT_FORMAT_INVALID");
   300	    }
   301	    if (value.archived_execution_evidence !== undefined) {
   302	      if (!Array.isArray(value.archived_execution_evidence) ||
   303	          value.archived_execution_evidence.length > 512 ||
   304	          value.archived_execution_evidence.some((item) =>
   305	            !item || typeof item.tool_call_id !== "string" ||
   306	            !/^[a-zA-Z0-9_:-]{1,180}$/.test(item.tool_call_id) ||
   307	            !/^[a-zA-Z0-9_]{1,180}$/.test(item.tool) ||
   308	            !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(item.args_sha256) ||
   309	            !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(item.result_sha256) ||
   310	            !["succeeded", "failed", "blocked"].includes(item.status))) {
   311	        throw new Error("GEMINX_EXECUTION_CHECKPOINT_ARCHIVE_INVALID");
   312	      }
   313	    }
   314	    const missing = unresolvedNativeToolCallIds(value.messages);
   315	    if (value.state !== "PENDING_TOOL" && missing.length > 0) {
   316	      throw new Error("GEMINX_EXECUTION_CHECKPOINT_TOOL_RESULT_MISSING");
   317	    }
   318	    return value;
   319	  }
   320	
   321	  saveExecutionCheckpoint(value: ExecutionTranscriptCheckpoint): string {
   322	    if (value.schema_version !== "geminx.execution-transcript.v1") {
   323	      throw new Error("GEMINX_EXECUTION_CHECKPOINT_FORMAT_INVALID");
   324	    }
   325	    const filePath = this.executionCheckpointPath(value.objective_id);
   326	    // Tool outputs can contain secrets; checkpoint only the redacted provider
   327	    // transcript. No raw reasoning_content, raw credentials or unbounded data.
   328	    const messages = value.messages.map((message): ExecutionTranscriptMessage => ({
   329	      role: message.role,
   330	      content: message.content === null
   331	        ? null : message.role === "tool"
   332	          ? safeNativeToolReceipt(String(message.content))
   333	          : redactDurableMemoryText(String(message.content ?? ""), 16_000),
   334	      ...(message.tool_call_id ? { tool_call_id: String(message.tool_call_id).slice(0, 180) } : {}),
   335	      ...(Array.isArray(message.tool_calls) ? {
   336	        tool_calls: message.tool_calls.map((call) => ({
   337	          id: String(call.id ?? "").slice(0, 180),
   338	          type: "function",
   339	          function: {
   340	            name: String(call.function?.name ?? "").slice(0, 180),
   341	            arguments: safeNativeToolArguments(String(call.function?.arguments ?? "{}"), String(call.function?.name ?? ""))
   342	          }
   343	        }))
   344	      } : {})
   345	    }));
   346	    const archive = (value.archived_execution_evidence ?? []).slice(-512);
   347	    if (archive.length > 0 && archive.some((item) =>
   348	      !/^[a-zA-Z0-9_:-]{1,180}$/.test(item.tool_call_id) ||
   349	      !/^[a-zA-Z0-9_]{1,180}$/.test(item.tool) ||
   350	      !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(item.args_sha256) ||
   351	      !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(item.result_sha256) ||
   352	      !["succeeded", "failed", "blocked"].includes(item.status))) {
   353	      throw new Error("GEMINX_EXECUTION_CHECKPOINT_ARCHIVE_INVALID");
   354	    }
   355	    const persisted = JSON.stringify({ ...value, archived_execution_evidence: archive, messages });
   356	    if (Buffer.byteLength(persisted, "utf8") > 1_500_000) {
   357	      throw new Error("GEMINX_EXECUTION_CHECKPOINT_OVERSIZED");
   358	    }
   359	    atomicWrite(filePath, persisted);
   360	    return filePath;
   361	  }
   362	
   363	  /** Seal only after the inbox worker verifies the exact objective and assigns
   364	   * its canonical final receipt. Model prose is not completion authority.
   365	   */
   366	  markVerifiedExecutionTerminal(objectiveId: string, receiptId: string): void {
   367	    if (!receiptId || !receiptId.startsWith("receipt-")) {
   368	      throw new Error("GEMINX_EXECUTION_TERMINAL_RECEIPT_INVALID");
   369	    }
   370	    const existing = this.getExecutionCheckpoint(objectiveId);
   371	    if (!existing || existing.state !== "CLEAN") {
   372	      throw new Error("GEMINX_EXECUTION_TERMINAL_CHECKPOINT_UNAVAILABLE");
   373	    }
   374	    this.saveExecutionCheckpoint({
   375	      ...existing, state: "TERMINAL", pending_tool_id: null,
   376	      updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
   377	    });
   378	  }
   379	
   380	  static intentSha256(intent: string): string {
   381	    return createHash("sha256").update(intent, "utf8").digest("hex");
   382	  }
   383	
   384	  conversationPath(conversationId: string): string {
   385	    return path.join(this.conversationsRoot, `${assertSafeRuntimeId("conversationId", conversationId)}.json`);
   386	  }
   387	
   388	  get(conversationId: string): ConversationRecord | null {
   389	    const filePath = this.conversationPath(conversationId);
   390	    const record = readJsonFile<ConversationRecord>(filePath);
   391	    if (record === null) return null;
   392	    assertStoredIdentity("conversation id", conversationId, record.conversation_id, filePath);
   393	    this.backfillDurableContinuity(record);
   394	    return record;
   395	  }
   396	
   397	  create(
   398	    id?: string,
   399	    modelPreference: string | null = null,
   400	    projectId?: string | null,
   401	    projectName?: string | null
   402	  ): ConversationRecord {
   403	    const conversationId = id ? assertSafeRuntimeId("conversationId", id) : randomUUID();
   404	    const record: ConversationRecord = {
   405	      conversation_id: conversationId,
   406	      active_run_id: null,
   407	      active_task_id: null,
   408	      project_id: projectId ?? null,
   409	      project_name: projectName ?? null,
   410	      model_preference: modelPreference,
   411	      domain: "general",
   412	      summary: "",
   413	      turns: [],
   414	      created_at: nowIso(),
   415	      updated_at: nowIso()
   416	    };
   417	    this.save(record);
   418	    this.memoryStore.append({
   419	      stream_type: "conversation",
   420	      stream_id: conversationId,
   421	      conversation_id: conversationId,
   422	      event_type: "CONVERSATION_CREATED",
   423	      actor: "geminx",
   424	      status: "created",
   425	      summary: "Founder conversation created.",
   426	      record_ref: `conversation:${conversationId}`,
   427	      idempotency_key: `conversation-created:${conversationId}`
   428	    });
   429	    return record;
   430	  }
   431	
   432	  /**
   433	   * Serialises same-conversation writes across backend processes on the shared
   434	   * persistent volume. Stale locks from crashed processes (missing owner or
   435	   * expired lease) are reclaimed safely without stealing live locks across processes.
   436	   */
   437	  private withConversationWriteLock<T>(conversationId: string, task: () => T): T {
   438	    const lockDir = this.conversationPath(conversationId) + ".write-lock";
   439	    const lockOwnerFile = path.join(lockDir, "owner.json");
   440	    const started = Date.now();
   441	    while (true) {
   442	      try {
   443	        mkdirSync(lockDir);
   444	        try {
   445	          writeFileSync(
   446	            lockOwnerFile,
   447	            JSON.stringify({ pid: process.pid, created_at: Date.now(), nonce: randomUUID() }),
   448	            { encoding: "utf8" }
   449	          );
   450	        } catch {
   451	          // If writing owner fails, directory still acts as basic lock
   452	        }
   453	        break;
   454	      } catch (error) {
   455	        if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== "EEXIST") throw error;
   456	
   457	        // Check for stale lock from a crashed process
   458	        let isStale = false;
   459	        try {
   460	          const stat = statSync(lockDir);
   461	          const lockAgeMs = Date.now() - stat.mtimeMs;
   462	          if (lockAgeMs > 10_000) {
   463	            isStale = true;
   464	          } else {
   465	            try {
   466	              const ownerContent = readFileSync(lockOwnerFile, "utf8");
   467	              const owner = JSON.parse(ownerContent) as { pid?: number; created_at?: number };
   468	              if (typeof owner.created_at === "number" && Date.now() - owner.created_at > 10_000) {
   469	                isStale = true;
   470	              } else if (typeof owner.pid === "number") {
   471	                try {
   472	                  process.kill(owner.pid, 0);
   473	                } catch (killErr) {
   474	                  if ((killErr as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === "ESRCH") {
   475	                    isStale = true;
   476	                  }
   477	                }
   478	              }
   479	            } catch {
   480	              // Owner file not yet written or unreadable; rely on stat age if needed
   481	            }
   482	          }
   483	        } catch {
   484	          // Directory may have been concurrently removed; retry acquisition
   485	          continue;
   486	        }
   487	
   488	        if (isStale) {
   489	          try {
   490	            rmSync(lockDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   491	          } catch {
   492	            // Concurrently removed; retry
   493	          }
   494	          continue;
   495	        }
   496	
   497	        if (Date.now() - started > 15_000) throw new Error("CONVERSATION_WRITE_LOCK_TIMEOUT");
   498	        Atomics.wait(new Int32Array(new SharedArrayBuffer(4)), 0, 0, 15);
   499	      }
   500	    }
   501	    try { return task(); }
   502	    finally { rmSync(lockDir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
   503	  }
   504	
   505	  private persistUnlocked(record: ConversationRecord): string {
   506	    const filePath = this.conversationPath(record.conversation_id);
   507	    atomicWrite(filePath, JSON.stringify(record, null, 2));
   508	    return filePath;
   509	  }
   510	
   511	  save(record: ConversationRecord): string {
   512	    return this.withConversationWriteLock(record.conversation_id, () => {
   513	      const fresh = this.get(record.conversation_id);
   514	      // Older running jobs can hold a snapshot predating a rename or unpin.
   515	      // Only updateMetadata owns these metadata fields.
   516	      const next: ConversationRecord = fresh ? {
   517	        ...record,
   518	        // A stale long-running caller cannot erase a newer canonical turn tail.
   519	        turns: (fresh.updated_at > record.updated_at && fresh.turns.length >= record.turns.length)
   520	          ? fresh.turns : record.turns,
   521	        title: fresh.title,
   522	        is_pinned: fresh.is_pinned,
   523	        pin_updated_at: fresh.pin_updated_at,
   524	        metadata_updated_at: fresh.metadata_updated_at,
   525	        project_id: record.project_id !== undefined ? record.project_id : fresh.project_id,
   526	        project_name: record.project_name !== undefined ? record.project_name : fresh.project_name,
   527	        last_activity_at: record.last_activity_at ?? fresh.last_activity_at
   528	      } : record;
   529	      return this.persistUnlocked(next);
   530	    });
   531	  }
   532	
   533	  setModelPreference(conversation: ConversationRecord, modelPreference: string | null): ConversationRecord {
   534	    return this.withConversationWriteLock(conversation.conversation_id, () => {
   535	      const latest = this.get(conversation.conversation_id) ?? conversation;
   536	      const next: ConversationRecord = {
   537	        ...latest,
   538	        model_preference: modelPreference,
   539	        ...(modelPreference === "antigravity" ? {} : { managed_antigravity_session: undefined }),
   540	        metadata_updated_at: nowIso()
   541	      };
   542	      this.persistUnlocked(next);
   543	      return next;
   544	    });
   545	  }
   546	
   547	  updateMetadata(conversationId: string, patch: ConversationMetadataPatch): ConversationRecord | null {
   548	    return this.withConversationWriteLock(conversationId, () => {
   549	      let latest = this.get(conversationId);
   550	      const now = nowIso();
   551	      let changed = false;
   552	      if (!latest) {
   553	        assertSafeRuntimeId("conversationId", conversationId);
   554	        latest = {
   555	          conversation_id: conversationId,
   556	          active_run_id: null,
   557	          active_task_id: null,
   558	          project_id: patch.project_id ?? null,
   559	          project_name: patch.project_name ?? null,
   560	          model_preference: patch.model_preference ?? null,
   561	          domain: "general",
   562	          summary: "",
   563	          turns: [],
   564	          created_at: now,
   565	          updated_at: now
   566	        };
   567	        changed = true;
   568	      }
   569	      const next = { ...latest };
   570	      if (patch.title !== undefined) {
   571	        if (patch.title !== null) next.title = sanitizeConversationTitle(patch.title);
   572	        else next.title = null;
   573	        changed = true;
   574	      }
   575	      if (patch.is_pinned !== undefined) {
   576	        if (typeof patch.is_pinned !== "boolean") throw new Error("INVALID_PIN_STATE");
   577	        if (!patch.only_if_unset || !next.pin_updated_at) {
   578	          next.is_pinned = patch.is_pinned;
   579	          next.pin_updated_at = now;
   580	          changed = true;
   581	        }
   582	      }
   583	      if (patch.model_preference !== undefined) {
   584	        next.model_preference = patch.model_preference;
   585	        if (patch.model_preference !== "antigravity") next.managed_antigravity_session = undefined;
   586	        changed = true;
   587	      }
   588	      if (patch.project_id !== undefined) {
   589	        next.project_id = patch.project_id ? assertSafeRuntimeId("projectId", patch.project_id) : null;
   590	        changed = true;
   591	      }
   592	      if (patch.project_name !== undefined) {
   593	        next.project_name = patch.project_name ? String(patch.project_name).trim().slice(0, 120) : null;
   594	        changed = true;
   595	      }
   596	      if (changed) {
   597	        next.metadata_updated_at = now;
   598	        next.last_activity_at ??= latest.updated_at || latest.created_at;
   599	        this.persistUnlocked(next);
   600	      }
   601	      return next;
   602	    });
   603	  }
   604	
   605	  list(): ConversationRecord[] {
   606	    return readdirSync(this.conversationsRoot)
   607	      .filter((entry) => entry.endsWith(".json"))
   608	      .map((entry) => readIndexedRecord<ConversationRecord>(this.conversationsRoot, entry))
   609	      .filter((entry): entry is ConversationRecord => entry !== null)
   610	      .sort((left, right) => (right.last_activity_at || right.updated_at).localeCompare(left.last_activity_at || left.updated_at));
   611	  }
   612	
   613	  findByRunId(runId: string): ConversationRecord | null {
   614	    if (!runId) return null;
   615	    const records = this.list();
   616	    for (const record of records) {
   617	      if (record.active_run_id === runId) return record;
   618	      if (record.turns.some((turn) => turn.message_id === runId || turn.durable_job_id === runId || turn.client_message_id === runId)) {
   619	        return record;
   620	      }
   621	    }
   622	    return null;
   623	  }
   624	
   625	  /**
   626	   * Append one turn to the permanent continuity ledger before refreshing the
   627	   * bounded UI/model working tail. The bounded tail may rotate; memory does not.
   628	   */
   629	  appendTurn(
   630	    conversation: ConversationRecord,
   631	    turn: ConversationTurn,
   632	    binding?: { active_run_id?: UUID | null; active_task_id?: UUID | null; domain?: string; summary?: string }
   633	  ): ConversationRecord {
   634	    return this.withConversationWriteLock(conversation.conversation_id, () => {
   635	      const current = this.get(conversation.conversation_id) ?? conversation;
   636	    const safeContent = redactDurableMemoryText(turn.content, this.maxMessageChars);
   637	    const messageId = typeof turn.message_id === "string" ? boundedText(turn.message_id.trim(), 180) : "";
   638	    if (messageId && current.turns.some(existing => existing.role === turn.role && existing.message_id === messageId)) {
   639	      return current;
   640	    }
   641	    const activeRunId = binding?.active_run_id !== undefined ? binding.active_run_id : current.active_run_id;
   642	    const activeTaskId = binding?.active_task_id !== undefined ? binding.active_task_id : current.active_task_id;
   643	    this.memoryStore.appendConversationTurn({
   644	      conversation_id: conversation.conversation_id,
   645	      role: turn.role,
   646	      content: turn.content,
   647	      at: turn.at,
   648	      active_run_id: activeRunId,
   649	      active_task_id: activeTaskId,
   650	      domain: binding?.domain ?? current.domain,
   651	      idempotency_key: messageId ? `turn:${turn.role}:${messageId}` : `turn:${turn.role}:${turn.at}`
   652	    });
   653	
   654	    const next = {
   655	      ...current,
   656	      turns: [
   657	        ...current.turns,
   658	        {
   659	          ...turn,
   660	          ...(messageId ? { message_id: messageId } : {}),
   661	          content: safeContent,
   662	          ...(Array.isArray(turn.attachments)
   663	            ? {
   664	                attachments: turn.attachments.slice(0, 24).map((item) => ({
   665	                  artifact_sha256: String(item.artifact_sha256 ?? "").slice(0, 64),
   666	                  filename: boundedText(String(item.filename ?? ""), 180),
   667	                  content_type: boundedText(String(item.content_type ?? ""), 120),
   668	                  size_bytes: Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(item.size_bytes ?? 0))),
   669	                  role: item.role == null ? null : boundedText(String(item.role), 64),
   670	                  source_name: item.source_name == null ? null : boundedText(String(item.source_name), 180)
   671	                }))
   672	              }
   673	            : {})
   674	        }
   675	      ].slice(-this.maxTurns),
   676	      active_run_id: activeRunId,
   677	      active_task_id: activeTaskId,
   678	      domain: binding?.domain ?? current.domain,
   679	      summary: summarySince(redactDurableMemoryText(binding?.summary ?? current.summary, this.maxSummaryChars), this.maxSummaryChars),
   680	      updated_at: nowIso(),
   681	      last_activity_at: nowIso()
   682	    };
   683	    this.persistUnlocked(next);
   684	    return next;
   685	    });
   686	  }
   687	
   688	  rehydrateContinuity(query: string, limit = 16): string {
   689	    return this.memoryStore.rehydrate(query, limit);
   690	  }
   691	
   692	  queryDurableMemory(query: string, limit = 16) {
   693	    return this.memoryStore.query(query, limit);
   694	  }
   695	
   696	  verifyContinuity(conversationId: string) {
   697	    return this.memoryStore.verify("conversation", conversationId);
   698	  }
   699	
   700	  verifyTaskMemory(taskId: string) {
   701	    return this.memoryStore.verify("task", taskId);
   702	  }
   703	
   704	  flushMemoryMirror(limit = 20) {
   705	    return this.memoryStore.flushBrainMirror(limit);
   706	  }
   707	
   708	  private backfillDurableContinuity(record: ConversationRecord): void {
   709	    if (this.memoryStore.readStream("conversation", record.conversation_id).length > 0) return;
   710	    this.memoryStore.append({
   711	      stream_type: "conversation",
   712	      stream_id: record.conversation_id,
   713	      conversation_id: record.conversation_id,
   714	      event_type: "CONVERSATION_IMPORTED",
   715	      actor: "geminx",
   716	      status: "imported",
   717	      timestamp: record.created_at,
   718	      summary: record.summary || "Existing Founder conversation imported into durable continuity.",
   719	      record_ref: `conversation:${record.conversation_id}`,
   720	      idempotency_key: `conversation-imported:${record.conversation_id}`
   721	    });
   722	    record.turns.forEach((existingTurn, index) => {
   723	      this.memoryStore.appendConversationTurn({
   724	        conversation_id: record.conversation_id,
   725	        role: existingTurn.role,
   726	        content: existingTurn.content,
   727	        at: existingTurn.at,
   728	        active_run_id: record.active_run_id,
   729	        active_task_id: record.active_task_id,
   730	        domain: record.domain,
   731	        idempotency_key: `legacy-turn:${index}:${existingTurn.role}:${existingTurn.at}`
   732	      });
   733	    });
   734	  }
   735	}
   736	
   737	function boundedText(value: string, ceiling: number): string {
   738	  return value.length <= ceiling ? value : `${value.slice(0, Math.max(0, ceiling - 14))}\n[truncated]`;
   739	}
   740	
   741	function summarySince(current: string, ceiling: number): string {
   742	  const next = current.trim();
   743	  return boundedText(next, ceiling);
   744	}

     1	import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
     2	import { mkdirSync, readdirSync, readFileSync, renameSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
     3	import path from "node:path";
     4	import { geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot } from "../runtime-root.js";
     5	import type { ISOTimestamp } from "../../types/index.js";
     6	
     7	export interface ProjectRecord {
     8	  id: string;
     9	  name: string;
    10	  description?: string;
    11	  is_default?: boolean;
    12	  created_at: ISOTimestamp;
    13	  updated_at: ISOTimestamp;
    14	}
    15	
    16	export interface CreateProjectInput {
    17	  name: string;
    18	  description?: string;
    19	  id?: string;
    20	}
    21	
    22	export const DEFAULT_PROJECT_DEFS: Array<{ id: string; name: string; description: string }> = [
    23	  {
    24	    id: "geminx-project",
    25	    name: "Geminex Project",
    26	    description: "Core GeminX workspace and development"
    27	  },
    28	  {
    29	    id: "digital-ambush",
    30	    name: "Digital Ambush",
    31	    description: "Outreach, lead gen, and campaign automation"
    32	  },
    33	  {
    34	    id: "roblox-creator",
    35	    name: "Roblox Creator",
    36	    description: "Roblox games and procedural asset creation"
    37	  },
    38	  {
    39	    id: "general",
    40	    name: "General",
    41	    description: "General tasks and conversations"
    42	  }
    43	];
    44	
    45	function nowIso(): ISOTimestamp {
    46	  return new Date().toISOString();
    47	}
    48	
    49	function ensureDirectory(targetPath: string): void {
    50	  mkdirSync(targetPath, { recursive: true });
    51	}
    52	
    53	function readJsonFile<T>(filePath: string): T | null {
    54	  try {
    55	    return JSON.parse(readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")) as T;
    56	  } catch (error) {
    57	    const code = (error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code;
    58	    if (code === "ENOENT") return null;
    59	    throw new Error(
    60	      `Project state is unreadable or corrupt at ${filePath}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`
    61	    );
    62	  }
    63	}
    64	
    65	function atomicWrite(filePath: string, content: string): void {
    66	  ensureDirectory(path.dirname(filePath));
    67	  const tempPath = `${filePath}.${process.pid}.${randomUUID()}.tmp`;
    68	  try {
    69	    writeFileSync(tempPath, content, { encoding: "utf8", flag: "wx" });
    70	    renameSync(tempPath, filePath);
    71	  } catch (error) {
    72	    rmSync(tempPath, { force: true });
    73	    throw error;
    74	  }
    75	}
    76	
    77	function assertSafeProjectId(value: string): string {
    78	  const trimmed = value.trim();
    79	  if (!trimmed || trimmed.length > 120) {
    80	    throw new Error("INVALID_PROJECT_ID_LENGTH");
    81	  }
    82	  if (/[<>:"|?*\\/\u0000-\u001f]/u.test(trimmed) || trimmed.includes("..")) {
    83	    throw new Error("UNSAFE_PROJECT_ID");
    84	  }
    85	  return trimmed;
    86	}
    87	
    88	export function sanitizeProjectName(raw: unknown): string {
    89	  if (typeof raw !== "string") throw new Error("INVALID_PROJECT_NAME_TYPE");
    90	  const name = raw.replace(/[\x00-\x1f\x7f]+/g, " ").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    91	  if (!name) throw new Error("EMPTY_PROJECT_NAME");
    92	  if (name.length > 100) throw new Error("PROJECT_NAME_TOO_LONG");
    93	  return name;
    94	}
    95	
    96	export function slugifyProjectName(name: string): string {
    97	  const base = name
    98	    .toLowerCase()
    99	    .trim()
   100	    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
   101	    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "");
   102	  return base || `project-${Date.now().toString(36)}`;
   103	}
   104	
   105	export class ProjectStore {
   106	  readonly projectsRoot: string;
   107	
   108	  constructor(options: { root?: string } = {}) {
   109	    const root = path.resolve(options.root ?? geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot());
   110	    this.projectsRoot = path.join(root, "projects");
   111	    ensureDirectory(this.projectsRoot);
   112	    this.ensureDefaults();
   113	  }
   114	
   115	  projectPath(projectId: string): string {
   116	    return path.join(this.projectsRoot, `${assertSafeProjectId(projectId)}.json`);
   117	  }
   118	
   119	  ensureDefaults(): void {
   120	    const now = nowIso();
   121	    for (const def of DEFAULT_PROJECT_DEFS) {
   122	      const filePath = this.projectPath(def.id);
   123	      if (readJsonFile<ProjectRecord>(filePath) === null) {
   124	        const record: ProjectRecord = {
   125	          id: def.id,
   126	          name: def.name,
   127	          description: def.description,
   128	          is_default: true,
   129	          created_at: now,
   130	          updated_at: now
   131	        };
   132	        atomicWrite(filePath, JSON.stringify(record, null, 2));
   133	      }
   134	    }
   135	  }
   136	
   137	  get(projectId: string): ProjectRecord | null {
   138	    if (!projectId) return null;
   139	    try {
   140	      const filePath = this.projectPath(projectId);
   141	      return readJsonFile<ProjectRecord>(filePath);
   142	    } catch {
   143	      return null;
   144	    }
   145	  }
   146	
   147	  create(input: CreateProjectInput): ProjectRecord {
   148	    const name = sanitizeProjectName(input.name);
   149	    let id: string;
   150	    if (input.id) {
   151	      id = assertSafeProjectId(input.id);
   152	    } else {
   153	      let candidate = slugifyProjectName(name);
   154	      if (this.get(candidate)) {
   155	        candidate = `${candidate}-${randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;
   156	      }
   157	      id = candidate;
   158	    }
   159	
   160	    if (this.get(id)) {
   161	      throw new Error("PROJECT_ALREADY_EXISTS");
   162	    }
   163	
   164	    const now = nowIso();
   165	    const record: ProjectRecord = {
   166	      id,
   167	      name,
   168	      ...(input.description ? { description: String(input.description).trim().slice(0, 500) } : {}),
   169	      is_default: false,
   170	      created_at: now,
   171	      updated_at: now
   172	    };
   173	
   174	    atomicWrite(this.projectPath(id), JSON.stringify(record, null, 2));
   175	    return record;
   176	  }
   177	
   178	  list(): ProjectRecord[] {
   179	    this.ensureDefaults();
   180	    const entries = readdirSync(this.projectsRoot).filter((e) => e.endsWith(".json"));
   181	    const records: ProjectRecord[] = [];
   182	    for (const entry of entries) {
   183	      const filePath = path.join(this.projectsRoot, entry);
   184	      const rec = readJsonFile<ProjectRecord>(filePath);
   185	      if (rec && rec.id && rec.name) {
   186	        records.push(rec);
   187	      }
   188	    }
   189	
   190	    // Default projects first in fixed order, then custom projects sorted alphabetically by name
   191	    const defaultOrder = DEFAULT_PROJECT_DEFS.map((d) => d.id);
   192	    return records.sort((a, b) => {
   193	      const aIdx = defaultOrder.indexOf(a.id);
   194	      const bIdx = defaultOrder.indexOf(b.id);
   195	      if (aIdx !== -1 && bIdx !== -1) return aIdx - bIdx;
   196	      if (aIdx !== -1) return -1;
   197	      if (bIdx !== -1) return 1;
   198	      return a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
   199	    });
   200	  }
   201	}
   202	
   203	export const nativeNeoProjectStore = new ProjectStore();

    38	function safeString(value: unknown, max: number): string | null {
    39	  if (typeof value !== "string") return null;
    40	  const trimmed = value.trim();
    41	  return trimmed ? trimmed.slice(0, max) : null;
    42	}
    43	
    44	function secureEqual(left: string, right: string): boolean {
    45	  const a = Buffer.from(left);
    46	  const b = Buffer.from(right);
    47	  return a.length === b.length && timingSafeEqual(a, b);
    48	}

    50	function smartSiteAuth(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void {
    51	  const expected = process.env.GEMINX_SMART_SITE_TOKEN;
    52	  if (!expected) {
    53	    res.status(503).json({ ok: false, error: "smart-site connector not configured" });
    54	    return;
    55	  }
    56	
    57	  const header = req.header("authorization") ?? "";
    58	  const supplied = header.startsWith("Bearer ") ? header.slice(7) : "";
    59	  if (!supplied || !secureEqual(supplied, expected)) {
    60	    res.status(401).json({ ok: false, error: "unauthorized" });
    61	    return;
    62	  }
    63	  next();
    64	}

    66	function siteIdFrom(req: Request): string {

    68	    ?? "entreprenuity-business-solutions";

    71	export function smartSiteRouter(): Router {

   132	        receipt_id: receipt.event_id,
   133	        result,
   134	      });
   135	    } catch (error) {
   136	      if (error instanceof PublicProofError) {
   137	        res.status(error.statusCode).json({ ok: false, status: "BLOCKED", message: error.publicMessage });
   138	        return;
   139	      }
